// ============================================================
//  CYD SUPER MARIO  v4.3 JUICY + LUIGI FRIEND (ESP32-2432S028, ILI9341 320x240)
//  Levels live in MARIO.txt on the SD card (FAT32).
//  v3.1 compile-hardening: Bowser dialogue array is BW_LINES (not BW_TALK)
//  because BW_TALK is already used by the Bowser state enum.
//  Mushroom House prologue, friend choice, and TWO chapter endings.
//  Chapter 2 (Bowser's Homeworld): every regular enemy is Bowser's race
//  (Bowserlings). Intro dialogue is kept and reused for Chapter 2 briefing.
//  Old robot/beam finale removed.
//  Libraries: TFT_eSPI (User_Setup.h), SD, WiFi.
//  Board: ESP32 Dev Module. Partition: Huge APP if needed.
//  Controller: ESP-NOW (magic 0xA5, button bitmask).
//
//  D-PAD  = move          A = jump (hold = higher)
//  B      = roll / somersault     SELECT = ability wheel
//  Movement is always at running speed; B no longer toggles walking/running.
//  START  = pause
//
//  Every "?" block gives ONE random power. A power can be used
//  ONE time, then it is gone - hit more blocks to get more.
//   11 powers: IRON FLY, HEAL, SHOCK, ROCKET, GHOST, HULK,
//              STAR, FIRE, FREEZE, SPRING, CLONE MARIO
//  CLONE MARIO creates 4 autonomous copies (green, pink, blue, white) for 10 seconds.
//  Enemies: goomba, koopa, bat, flame, snowman  (normal)
//           knight 'a' (armored, 3 hits)
//           turret 't' (shoots)
//           bowserling 'q' (Chapter 2 race - all Ch2 foes become this)
//  Boss: Bowser / UFO hybrid encounters remain available in levels.
// ============================================================
#include <Arduino.h>
#include <WiFi.h>
#include <esp_now.h>
#include <esp_wifi.h>
#include <SPI.h>
#include <SD.h>
#include <TFT_eSPI.h>
#include <math.h>

// ============================================================
//  FX
// ============================================================
#define NPART 72
struct Particle { float x,y,vx,vy; uint16_t col; uint8_t life,size,kind; bool active; };
Particle particles[NPART];
uint8_t fxFlash = 0;

void fxClear() { for (auto &p : particles) p.active = false; fxFlash = 0; shakeT = 0; hitStop = 0; }
static int fxSlot() {
  for (int i = 0; i < NPART; i++) if (!particles[i].active) return i;
  return -1;
}
void fxBurst(float x, float y, uint16_t col, uint8_t amount = 7) {
  for (int n = 0; n < amount; n++) {
    int k = fxSlot(); if (k < 0) return;
    Particle &p = particles[k];
    float a = random(0, 628) / 100.0f, sp = random(5, 20) / 10.0f;
    p.x = x; p.y = y; p.vx = cosf(a) * sp; p.vy = sinf(a) * sp - 0.8f;
    p.col = col; p.life = random(8, 22); p.size = random(1, 3); p.kind = 0; p.active = true;
  }
}
// fast streak sparks (drawn as short lines, no gravity)
void fxSpark(float x, float y, uint16_t col, uint8_t amount = 6) {
  for (int n = 0; n < amount; n++) {
    int k = fxSlot(); if (k < 0) return;
    Particle &p = particles[k];
    float a = random(0, 628) / 100.0f, sp = random(20, 55) / 10.0f;
    p.x = x; p.y = y; p.vx = cosf(a) * sp; p.vy = sinf(a) * sp;
    p.col = col; p.life = random(7, 15); p.size = 1; p.kind = 1; p.active = true;
  }
}
void fxTrail(float x, float y, uint16_t col) {
  int k = fxSlot(); if (k < 0) return;
  Particle &p = particles[k];
  p.x = x + random(-2, 3); p.y = y + random(-2, 3); p.vx = random(-4, 5) / 10.0f; p.vy = random(-4, 5) / 10.0f;
  p.col = col; p.life = random(6, 11); p.size = 1; p.kind = 0; p.active = true;
}
void fxRing(float x, float y, uint16_t col, uint8_t amount = 12, float speed = 2.2f) {
  for (int n = 0; n < amount; n++) {
    int k = fxSlot(); if (k < 0) return;
    Particle &p = particles[k];
    float a = (n * 628.0f) / (amount * 100.0f);
    p.x = x; p.y = y; p.vx = cosf(a) * speed; p.vy = sinf(a) * speed;
    p.col = col; p.life = 16; p.size = 2; p.kind = 2; p.active = true;
  }
}
void fxDust(float x, float y) {
  for (int n = 0; n < 2; n++) {
    int k = fxSlot(); if (k < 0) return;
    Particle &p = particles[k];
    p.x = x + random(-3, 4); p.y = y; p.vx = random(-8, 9) / 10.0f; p.vy = -random(2, 8) / 10.0f;
    p.col = RGB(225, 225, 215); p.life = random(6, 11); p.size = 1; p.kind = 0; p.active = true;
  }
}
void fxUpdate() {
  for (auto &p : particles) if (p.active) {
    p.x += p.vx; p.y += p.vy;
    if (p.kind == 1) { p.vx *= 0.92f; p.vy *= 0.92f; }
    else if (p.kind == 2) { p.vx *= 0.93f; p.vy *= 0.93f; }
    else p.vy += 0.10f;
    if (p.life) p.life--;
    if (!p.life) p.active = false;
  }
  if (fxFlash > 12) fxFlash -= 3; else if (fxFlash) fxFlash--;
  if (shakeT > 0) shakeT--;
}
void fxWorld(int cam, int y0) {
  for (auto &p : particles) if (p.active) {
    int x = (int)p.x - cam, y = (int)p.y - y0;
    if (x < -8 || x >= SCRW + 8 || y < -8 || y >= BANDH + 8) continue;
    if (p.kind == 1) spr.drawLine(x, y, x - (int)(p.vx * 1.8f), y - (int)(p.vy * 1.8f), p.col);
    else if (p.kind == 2) { spr.fillRect(x - 1, y - 1, 3, 3, p.col); if (p.life > 10) spr.drawPixel(x, y, TFT_WHITE); }
    else if (p.size <= 1) spr.fillRect(x, y, 2, 2, p.col);
    else spr.fillCircle(x, y, p.size, p.col);
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  INPUT
// ============================================================
enum { BTN_UP = 1, BTN_DOWN = 2, BTN_LEFT = 4, BTN_RIGHT = 8,
       BTN_A = 16, BTN_B = 32, BTN_START = 64, BTN_SELECT = 128 };

struct __attribute__((packed)) Pkt { uint8_t magic, btn, seq; };

volatile uint8_t  rxHeld = 0, rxEdge = 0;
volatile uint32_t rxTime = 0;
uint8_t H = 0, E = 0;

// ============================================================
//  THEME IDS
//  Declared before the sound code because the background music
//  selector uses these constants.
// ============================================================
enum ThemeId : uint8_t {
  TH_GRASS = 0, TH_WINTER, TH_VOLCANO, TH_CASTLE, TH_DESERT,
  TH_NIGHT, TH_JUNGLE, TH_SKY, TH_CAVE, TH_SPACE
};

// ============================================================
//  PASSIVE BUZZER (CYD GPIO 22)
// ============================================================
#define BUZZER_PIN 22
// Lower PWM duty = noticeably quieter passive buzzer.
// 8-bit range: 255 is full duty; 40 is about 16%.
#define BUZZER_DUTY 12
uint32_t buzzUntil = 0;

// Non-blocking 8-bit sound sequencer for the passive buzzer on GPIO22.
struct SoundSeq {
  const uint16_t *notes;
  const uint16_t *dur;
  uint8_t count;
  uint8_t pos;
  uint32_t nextAt;
  bool active;
} snd = {nullptr, nullptr, 0, 0, 0, false};

void toneNow(uint16_t hz) {
#if defined(ESP_ARDUINO_VERSION_MAJOR) && ESP_ARDUINO_VERSION_MAJOR >= 3
  ledcWriteTone(BUZZER_PIN, hz);
  if (hz) ledcWrite(BUZZER_PIN, BUZZER_DUTY);
#else
  ledcWriteTone(7, hz);
  if (hz) ledcWrite(7, BUZZER_DUTY);
#endif
}

void soundStop() { snd.active = false; toneNow(0); buzzUntil = 0; }

void soundStart(const uint16_t *notes, const uint16_t *dur, uint8_t count) {
  snd.notes = notes; snd.dur = dur; snd.count = count; snd.pos = 0; snd.nextAt = 0; snd.active = true;
}

void soundUpdate() {
  if (!snd.active) return;
  uint32_t now = millis();
  if ((int32_t)(now - snd.nextAt) < 0) return;
  if (snd.pos >= snd.count) { soundStop(); return; }
  uint16_t hz = snd.notes[snd.pos];
  uint16_t ms = snd.dur[snd.pos];
  toneNow(hz);
  snd.nextAt = now + ms;
  snd.pos++;
}

void buzzerBegin() {
#if defined(ESP_ARDUINO_VERSION_MAJOR) && ESP_ARDUINO_VERSION_MAJOR >= 3
  ledcAttach(BUZZER_PIN, 2000, 8);
  ledcWriteTone(BUZZER_PIN, 0);
#else
  ledcSetup(7, 2000, 8);
  ledcAttachPin(BUZZER_PIN, 7);
  ledcWriteTone(7, 0);
#endif
}
void buzzerOff() {
#if defined(ESP_ARDUINO_VERSION_MAJOR) && ESP_ARDUINO_VERSION_MAJOR >= 3
  ledcWriteTone(BUZZER_PIN, 0);
#else
  ledcWriteTone(7, 0);
#endif
  buzzUntil = 0;
}
void buzz(uint16_t hz, uint16_t ms) {
  if (hz < 80) hz = 80;
  if (ms > 70) ms = 70; // keep the CYD buzzer quiet and short
#if defined(ESP_ARDUINO_VERSION_MAJOR) && ESP_ARDUINO_VERSION_MAJOR >= 3
  ledcWriteTone(BUZZER_PIN, hz);
  ledcWrite(BUZZER_PIN, BUZZER_DUTY);
#else
  ledcWriteTone(7, hz);
  ledcWrite(7, BUZZER_DUTY);
#endif
  buzzUntil = millis() + ms;
}
void buzzerUpdate() {
  if (snd.active) { soundUpdate(); return; }
  if (buzzUntil && (int32_t)(millis() - buzzUntil) >= 0) buzzerOff();
  // Background music disabled: only sound effects are used.
}

// Background music is intentionally disabled. Sound effects only.
bool bgActive = false;
void bgSelect(uint8_t theme, bool bossMode) { (void)theme; (void)bossMode; bgActive = false; }
void bgStop() { bgActive = false; if (!snd.active) toneNow(0); }
void bgUpdate() {}

void handleRx(const uint8_t *data, int len) {
  if (len != (int)sizeof(Pkt)) return;
  const Pkt *p = (const Pkt *)data;
  if (p->magic != 0xA5) return;
  noInterrupts();
  rxEdge = (uint8_t)(rxEdge | (p->btn & ~rxHeld));
  rxHeld = p->btn;
  rxTime = millis();
  interrupts();
}
#if defined(ESP_ARDUINO_VERSION_MAJOR) && ESP_ARDUINO_VERSION_MAJOR >= 3
void onRx(const esp_now_recv_info_t *, const uint8_t *d, int l) { handleRx(d, l); }
#else
void onRx(const uint8_t *, const uint8_t *d, int l) { handleRx(d, l); }
#endif

bool padOnline() { return rxTime != 0 && (millis() - rxTime) < 500; }

void readInput() {
  noInterrupts();
  uint8_t held = rxHeld;
  uint8_t edge = rxEdge;
  rxEdge = (uint8_t)(rxEdge & ~edge);
  interrupts();
  if (!padOnline()) held = 0;
  H = held;
  E = edge;
}

uint32_t nextFrame = 0;
void frameWait(uint16_t ms) {
  uint32_t now = millis();
  if ((int32_t)(nextFrame - now) > 0) delay(nextFrame - now);
  else nextFrame = now;
  nextFrame += ms;
}

// ============================================================
//  SD
// ============================================================
bool readLine(File &f, char *buf, int maxLen) {
  int n = 0;
  bool any = false;
  while (f.available()) {
    int c = f.read();
    any = true;
    if (c == '\n') break;
    if (c == '\r') continue;
    if (n < maxLen - 1) buf[n++] = (char)c;
  }
  buf[n] = 0;
  return any;
}

#define MAXLV 16
#define NSKIN 6
char lvTitles[MAXLV][24];
int  nLevelsCached = 0;
char saveName[48];

struct Save {
  int hi, bank;          // bank = persistent coins
  uint16_t unlock;       // bit i = level i+1 open
  uint8_t skin, owned;   // owned bit0 always classic
  uint8_t secrets, endings, usedPwr;
  uint8_t ufoOwned;         // 1 = captured UFO is now Mario's
};
Save sv;

const char *SKIN_NAME[NSKIN] = { "CLASSIC", "BLUE", "PINK", "BLACK", "GOLD", "ICE" };
const int   SKIN_COST[NSKIN] = { 0, 20, 25, 30, 40, 35 };

void saveDefault() {
  sv.hi = 0; sv.bank = 0; sv.unlock = 1; sv.skin = 0; sv.owned = 1;
  sv.secrets = 0; sv.endings = 0; sv.usedPwr = 0; sv.ufoOwned = 0;
}
// SAVE SYSTEM DISABLED: progress is RAM-only and is reset on reboot.
void loadSave(const char *game) {
  (void)game;
  saveDefault();
  saveName[0] = 0;
}
void writeSave() {
  // Intentionally empty: nothing is written to the SD card.
}
int loadHi(const char *game) {
  (void)game;
  saveDefault();
  return 0;
}
void saveHi(const char *game, int v) {
  (void)game;
  sv.hi = v;
}

// Chapter selection / story state
int selectedChapter = 0;
int chapter2Level = 0;
int bossStyle = 0;
bool chapter2StoryDone = false;
bool companionCombat = false;
bool inChapter2 = false;   // true for the whole Chapter 2 campaign
int  worldLevelShown = 1;
int  levelClock = 300;

struct GameInfo { char file[32]; char name[24]; char type[12]; };
GameInfo games[16];
int nGames = 0;

void scanGames() {
  nGames = 0;
  File root = SD.open("/");
  if (!root) return;
  while (nGames < 16) {
    File f = root.openNextFile();
    if (!f) break;
    String nm = f.name();
    bool isDir = f.isDirectory();
    f.close();
    if (isDir) continue;
    if (nm.startsWith("/")) nm = nm.substring(1);
    if (nm.startsWith(".")) continue;
    String low = nm; low.toLowerCase();
    if (!low.endsWith(".txt")) continue;
    if (nm.length() > 30) continue;

    GameInfo &g = games[nGames];
    snprintf(g.file, sizeof(g.file), "/%s", nm.c_str());
    strncpy(g.name, nm.substring(0, nm.length() - 4).c_str(), 23);
    g.name[23] = 0;
    g.type[0] = 0;

    File h = SD.open(g.file);
    if (h) {
      char line[80];
      for (int i = 0; i < 24 && readLine(h, line, sizeof(line)); i++) {
        if (line[0] == '[') break;
        if (!strncmp(line, "name=", 5)) { strncpy(g.name, line + 5, 23); g.name[23] = 0; }
        else if (!strncmp(line, "type=", 5)) { strncpy(g.type, line + 5, 11); g.type[11] = 0; }
      }
      h.close();
    }
    if (!g.type[0]) continue;
    if (strcmp(g.type, "platformer") && strcmp(g.type, "mario")) continue;
    nGames++;
  }
  root.close();
}

// ============================================================
//  LEVEL FILE
//  Tiles:  # ground  B brick  ? lucky  U used  S stone(hard)
//          L R l r pipe (top cap L R, body l r)   c coin
//          F flag  ^ spikes  @ start
//          = horiz platform  ~ vert platform
//          H secret exit
//  Foes :  g goomba  k koopa  f bat  e flame  n snowman
//          a ARMORED knight   t TURRET (shoots)  q BOWSERLING
// ============================================================
#define MAXW 240
#define MAXH 15
char lvl[MAXH][MAXW];
char lvOrig[MAXH][MAXW];      // untouched copy (boss level refills blocks)
int LW = 0, LR = 0;
char lvName[24];
uint16_t lvSky;
uint8_t lvTheme = 0;

int countLevels(const char *path) {
  File f = SD.open(path);
  if (!f) return 0;
  static char line[300];
  int n = 0;
  while (readLine(f, line, sizeof(line))) if (line[0] == '[') {
    if (n < MAXLV) {
      char *e = strchr(line, ']'); if (e) *e = 0;
      strncpy(lvTitles[n], line + 1, 23); lvTitles[n][23] = 0;
    }
    n++;
  }
  f.close();
  nLevelsCached = n;
  return n;
}

uint8_t parseTheme(const char *s) {
  if (!strncmp(s, "winter", 6) || !strncmp(s, "snow", 4) || !strncmp(s, "ice", 3)) return TH_WINTER;
  if (!strncmp(s, "volcano", 7) || !strncmp(s, "lava", 4) || !strncmp(s, "fire", 4)) return TH_VOLCANO;
  if (!strncmp(s, "castle", 6) || !strncmp(s, "dungeon", 7)) return TH_CASTLE;
  if (!strncmp(s, "desert", 6) || !strncmp(s, "sand", 4)) return TH_DESERT;
  if (!strncmp(s, "night", 5) || !strncmp(s, "dark", 4)) return TH_NIGHT;
  if (!strncmp(s, "jungle", 6) || !strncmp(s, "forest", 6)) return TH_JUNGLE;
  if (!strncmp(s, "sky", 3) || !strncmp(s, "cloud", 5)) return TH_SKY;
  if (!strncmp(s, "cave", 4) || !strncmp(s, "under", 5)) return TH_CAVE;
  if (!strncmp(s, "space", 5) || !strncmp(s, "ufo", 3) || !strncmp(s, "boss", 4)) return TH_SPACE;
  return TH_GRASS;
}

uint16_t themeSky(uint8_t t) {
  switch (t) {
    case TH_WINTER:  return RGB(170, 205, 240);
    case TH_VOLCANO: return RGB(18, 78, 196);
    case TH_CASTLE:  return RGB(16, 70, 188);
    case TH_DESERT:  return RGB(245, 205, 120);
    case TH_NIGHT:   return RGB(8, 10, 34);
    case TH_JUNGLE:  return RGB(60, 150, 190);
    case TH_SKY:     return RGB(110, 185, 255);
    case TH_CAVE:    return RGB(16, 14, 26);
    case TH_SPACE:   return RGB(6, 4, 22);
    default:         return RGB(92, 148, 252);
  }
}

bool loadLevel(const char *path, int n) {
  File f = SD.open(path);
  if (!f) return false;
  static char line[300];
  memset(lvl, ' ', sizeof(lvl));
  LW = 0; LR = 0; lvName[0] = 0;
  lvTheme = TH_GRASS;
  lvSky = themeSky(TH_GRASS);
  int idx = 0;
  bool inMap = false;
  while (readLine(f, line, sizeof(line))) {
    if (line[0] == '[') {
      if (inMap) break;
      if (++idx == n) {
        inMap = true;
        char *e = strchr(line, ']');
        if (e) *e = 0;
        strncpy(lvName, line + 1, 23); lvName[23] = 0;
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (!inMap || line[0] == ';') continue;
    if (!strncmp(line, "sky=", 4)) {
      uint32_t c = strtoul(line + 4, NULL, 16);
      lvSky = RGB((c >> 16) & 255, (c >> 8) & 255, c & 255);
      continue;
    }
    if (!strncmp(line, "theme=", 6)) {
      lvTheme = parseTheme(line + 6);
      lvSky = themeSky(lvTheme);
      continue;
    }
    if (line[0] == 0) { if (LR > 0) break; else continue; }
    if (LR < MAXH) {
      int l = strlen(line);
      if (l > MAXW) l = MAXW;
      memcpy(lvl[LR], line, l);
      if (l > LW) LW = l;
      LR++;
    }
  }
  f.close();
  return inMap && LR > 0;
}

// ============================================================
//  POWERS - every power is ONE use. Get more from "?" blocks.
// ============================================================
enum { PWR_FLY=0, PWR_HEAL, PWR_SHOCK, PWR_ROCKET, PWR_INVIS, PWR_HULK, PWR_STAR,
       PWR_FIRE, PWR_FREEZE, PWR_SPRING, PWR_CLONE, PWR_N };

const char *PWR_NAME[PWR_N] = {
  "HULKBUSTER", "HEAL", "SHOCK", "ROCKET", "GHOST", "HULK", "STAR", "FIRE", "FREEZE", "SPRING", "CLONE MARIO"
};

uint8_t pwrActive[PWR_N];   // 1 while running
int     pwrTimer[PWR_N];

int flyPhase = 0;           // 0 off, 1 suit-up, 3 flight
static const int HB_W = 42;
static const int HB_H = 64;
int flyAnim = 0;
float shockX, shockY, shockR; int shockLife = 0;
int rkState = 0;            // 0 off, 1 home in, 2 grab, 3 ride, 4 boom
float rkX, rkY, rkVx, rkVy; int rkLife = 0; int rkTarget = -1;
float boomX, boomY, boomR; int boomLife = 0;
int freezeT = 0;
int fireLeft = 0, fireT = 0;
int invuln = 0, blinkT = 0;
int pwrAnimT = 0;                 // smooth activation animation, no frame blinking
int pwrAnimId = -1;

char msgTxt[24]; int msgT = 0;
void setMsg(const char *s) { strncpy(msgTxt, s, 23); msgTxt[23] = 0; msgT = 60; }

void pwrResetRun() {
  for (int i = 0; i < PWR_N; i++) { pwrActive[i] = 0; pwrTimer[i] = 0; }
  flyPhase = 0; shockLife = 0; rkState = 0; rkTarget = -1; boomLife = 0;
  invuln = 0; blinkT = 0; freezeT = 0; fireLeft = 0;
  pwrAnimT = 0; pwrAnimId = -1;
  cloneReset();
}
void pwrResetAll() {
  for (int i = 0; i < PWR_N; i++) pwrCnt[i] = 0;
  pwrResetRun();
}

// ============================================================
//  POP-UPS (power icon popping out of a block) + block bumps
// ============================================================
struct Pop { float x, y, vy; int8_t id; uint8_t life; int16_t val; };
Pop pops[10];
struct Bump { int16_t tx, ty; uint8_t t; };
Bump bumps[8];

void popsClear() {
  for (auto &p : pops) p.life = 0;
  for (auto &b : bumps) b.t = 0;
}
void spawnPop(float x, float y, int id) {
  for (auto &p : pops) if (!p.life) { p.x = x; p.y = y; p.vy = -2.6f; p.id = id; p.life = 50; p.val = 0; return; }
}
void spawnScore(float x, float y, int v) {
  for (auto &p : pops) if (!p.life) { p.x = x; p.y = y; p.vy = -1.4f; p.id = -1; p.life = 30; p.val = v; return; }
}
void bumpStart(int tx, int ty) {
  for (auto &b : bumps) if (!b.t) { b.tx = tx; b.ty = ty; b.t = 8; return; }
}
int bumpOffset(int tx, int ty) {
  for (auto &b : bumps) if (b.t && b.tx == tx && b.ty == ty) return b.t > 4 ? 8 - b.t : b.t;
  return 0;
}
void popsUpdate() {
  for (auto &p : pops) if (p.life) {
    p.y += p.vy;
    if (p.id < 0) p.vy *= 0.93f;
    else { p.vy += 0.16f; if (p.vy > 0.6f) p.vy = 0.6f; }
    p.life--;
  }
  for (auto &b : bumps) if (b.t) b.t--;
}

int grantRandomPower(float x, float y) {
  int miss[PWR_N], nm = 0;
  for (int i = 0; i < PWR_N; i++) if (!hasP(i)) miss[nm++] = i;
  int pick = (nm && random(100) < 75) ? miss[random(nm)] : (int)random(PWR_N);
  if (pick == PWR_FLY) pwrCnt[pick] = 1; else if (pwrCnt[pick] < 9) pwrCnt[pick]++;
  spawnPop(x, y, pick);
  return pick;
}

inline char tileAt(int tx, int ty) {
  if (tx < 0 || tx >= LW) return '#';
  if (ty < 0 || ty >= MAXH) return '.';
  return lvl[ty][tx];
}
inline bool solidCh(char c) {
  switch (c) {
    case '#': case 'B': case '?': case 'U': case 'S':
    case 'L': case 'R': case 'l': case 'r': return true;
  }
  return false;
}
bool platHit(float x, float y, int w, int h) {
  for (int i = 0; i < nPl; i++)
    if (overlap(x, y, w, h, plats[i].x, plats[i].y, TS * 2, 8)) return true;
  return false;
}
bool boxHit(float x, float y, int w, int h) {
  int x0 = (int)floorf(x / TS), x1 = (int)floorf((x + w - 0.01f) / TS);
  int y0 = (int)floorf(y / TS), y1 = (int)floorf((y + h - 0.01f) / TS);
  for (int ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++)
    for (int tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++)
      if (solidCh(tileAt(tx, ty))) return true;
  return platHit(x, y, w, h);
}
static inline bool overlap(float ax, float ay, float aw, float ah, float bx, float by, float bw, float bh) {
  return ax < bx + bw && ax + aw > bx && ay < by + bh && ay + ah > by;
}

// ---- damage ------------------------------------------------
void killNow() {
  if (dying) return;
  dying = true; dyingT = 0; vy = -6.5f; vx = 0; hp = 0; sfxMarioDeath();
  pwrResetRun();
  PW = 12; PH = 15;
  fxBurst(px + 6, py + 6, RGB(255, 80, 60), 10);
}
bool isInvincible() {
  return invuln > 0 || onP(PWR_STAR) || onP(PWR_INVIS) || onP(PWR_HULK) || (rkState >= 2 && rkState <= 4);
}
void hurt(int dmg) {
  if (dying || won || isInvincible()) return;
  hp -= dmg;
  fxBurst(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, RGB(255, 70, 70), 10);
  shakeT = 8; fxFlash = 12; hitStop = 3;
  fxSpark(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, RGB(255, 240, 120), 8);
  if (hp <= 0) { killNow(); return; }
  invuln = 60; blinkT = 60;
  vy = -4.2f; vx = -face * 2.4f; onGround = false;
}

// ---- blocks ------------------------------------------------
void openLucky(int tx, int ty) {
  if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= LW || ty >= MAXH) return;
  if (lvl[ty][tx] != '?') return;
  lvl[ty][tx] = 'U';
  coins++; score += 200;
  bumpStart(tx, ty);
  int id = grantRandomPower(tx * TS + 8, ty * TS - 4);
  fxBurst(tx * TS + 8, ty * TS + 8, RGB(255, 220, 80), 12);
  fxFlash = 20;
  (void)id;
}
void bumpNearby() {
  float hx0 = px - 8, hx1 = px + PW + 8;
  float hy0 = py - 18, hy1 = py + 6;
  int x0 = (int)floorf(hx0 / TS), x1 = (int)floorf(hx1 / TS);
  int y0 = (int)floorf(hy0 / TS), y1 = (int)floorf(hy1 / TS);
  for (int ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
    if (ty < 0 || ty >= MAXH) continue;
    for (int tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
      if (tx < 0 || tx >= LW) continue;
      if (lvl[ty][tx] == '?') { openLucky(tx, ty); return; }
    }
  }
}
void bumpBlock(int ty) {
  bumpNearby();
  if (ty < 0 || ty >= MAXH) return;
  int mid = (int)((px + PW / 2) / TS);
  for (int tx = mid - 1; tx <= mid + 1; tx++) {
    if (tx < 0 || tx >= LW) continue;
    if (lvl[ty][tx] == '?') openLucky(tx, ty);
    else if (lvl[ty][tx] == 'B' && !onP(PWR_HULK) && overlap(px, py - 2, PW, 4, tx * TS, ty * TS, TS, TS)) bumpStart(tx, ty);
  }
}
void hulkBreakAround() {
  if (!onP(PWR_HULK)) return;
  int x0 = (int)floorf(px / TS) - 1, x1 = (int)floorf((px + PW) / TS) + 1;
  int y0 = (int)floorf(py / TS) - 1, y1 = (int)floorf((py + PH) / TS) + 1;
  for (int ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++)
    for (int tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
      if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= LW || ty >= MAXH) continue;
      char c = lvl[ty][tx];
      if (c == 'B' || c == 'U') {
        lvl[ty][tx] = '.';
        score += 30;
        fxBurst(tx * TS + 8, ty * TS + 8, RGB(120, 220, 90), 6);
        shakeT = 4;
      }
    }
}

void movePX(float dx) {
  int n = (int)ceilf(fabsf(dx) * 2);
  if (!n) return;
  float s = dx / n;
  for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    px += s;
    if (boxHit(px, py, PW, PH)) {
      if (onP(PWR_HULK)) hulkBreakAround();
      if (boxHit(px, py, PW, PH)) { px -= s; vx = 0; break; }
    }
  }
}
void movePY(float dy) {
  int n = (int)ceilf(fabsf(dy) * 2);
  if (!n) return;
  float s = dy / n;
  for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    py += s;
    if (boxHit(px, py, PW, PH)) {
      if (s < 0) bumpBlock((int)floorf(py / TS));
      if (onP(PWR_HULK)) hulkBreakAround();
      if (boxHit(px, py, PW, PH)) {
        py -= s; vy = 0;
        if (s > 0) onGround = true;
        break;
      }
    }
  }
}
// pop Mario out of a wall after a scripted move (rocket ride)
void unstick() {
  int guard = 0;
  while (boxHit(px, py, PW, PH) && guard++ < 60) py -= 1;
  if (boxHit(px, py, PW, PH)) { py += guard; px -= 8; }
}

// ============================================================
//  ENEMIES
// ============================================================
void spawnEnemy(char ch, int x, int y) {
  if (nEn >= MAXEN) return;
  uint8_t k = 255;
  switch (ch) {
    case 'g': k = EK_GOOMBA; break;
    case 'k': k = EK_KOOPA; break;
    case 'f': k = EK_FLY; break;
    case 'e': k = EK_FIRE; break;
    case 'n': k = EK_SNOW; break;
    case 'a': k = EK_ARMOR; break;
    case 't': k = EK_SHOOT; break;
    case 'r': k = EK_RUNNER; break;
    case 'j': k = EK_JUMPER; break;
    case 'q': k = EK_BOWSERLING; break;
  }
  // Chapter 2 is Bowser's homeworld: every regular foe is his race.
  if (inChapter2 && k != 255) k = EK_BOWSERLING;
  if (k == 255) return;
  Enemy &e = en[nEn++];
  e.x = x * TS + 1; e.y = y * TS + 2; e.vx = 0; e.vy = 0;
  e.alive = true; e.awake = false; e.squish = 0; e.kind = k; e.cd = 0;
  e.hp = (k == EK_ARMOR) ? 3 : ((k == EK_RUNNER || k == EK_JUMPER) ? 2 : ((k == EK_BOWSERLING) ? 4 : 1));
  e.shoot = 50 + (x * 7) % 40;
}

void spawnBullet(float x, float y, float bvx, float bvy, uint8_t kind) {
  for (auto &b : ebul) if (!b.alive) { b.x = x; b.y = y; b.vx = bvx; b.vy = bvy; b.kind = kind; b.alive = true; return; }
}

void killEnemy(int i, uint16_t col) {
  Enemy &e = en[i];
  e.alive = false; e.squish = 14;
  sfxEnemyDefeat();
  score += (e.kind == EK_ARMOR) ? 400 : 100;
  fxBurst(e.x + 7, e.y + 7, col, 8);
  fxSpark(e.x + 7, e.y + 7, col, 6);
  spawnScore(e.x + 7, e.y - 2, (e.kind == EK_ARMOR) ? 400 : 100);
}
void hitEnemy(int i, int dmg, uint16_t col) {
  Enemy &e = en[i];
  if (!e.alive) return;
  if (e.kind == EK_ARMOR) {
    if (e.cd > 0) return;
    if (e.hp > dmg) {
      e.hp -= dmg; e.cd = 20; score += 40;
      fxBurst(e.x + 7, e.y + 4, RGB(220, 230, 255), 6);
      e.vx = (px < e.x) ? fabsf(e.vx) : -fabsf(e.vx);
      return;
    }
  }
  killEnemy(i, col);
}

bool nearestTarget(float &tx, float &ty, int &idx) {
  float bd = 1e9f; idx = -1;
  for (int i = 0; i < nEn; i++) if (en[i].alive && fabsf(en[i].x - px) < SCRW) {
    float dx = en[i].x - px, dy = en[i].y - py;
    float d = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    if (d < bd) { bd = d; idx = i; }
  }
  if (idx >= 0) { tx = en[idx].x + 7; ty = en[idx].y + 7; return true; }
  if (boss.active && boss.dieT == 0) { idx = -2; tx = boss.x + BOSS_W / 2; ty = boss.y + BOSS_H / 2; return true; }
  return false;
}

// ============================================================
//  BOSS  (UFO alien)
// ============================================================
void spawnBoss(float x, float y) {
  memset(&boss, 0, sizeof(boss));
  bossStyle = (chapter2Level > 0) ? ((chapter2Level - 1) % 5) : 0;
  boss.active = true; boss.x = x; boss.y = y;
  boss.hpMax = 40; boss.hp = boss.hpMax; boss.atk = 120; boss.phase = 1;
  for (auto &o : orbs) o.alive = false;
}
static void refillBlocks() {
  for (int y = 0; y < MAXH; y++) for (int x = 0; x < LW; x++)
    if (lvl[y][x] == 'U' && lvOrig[y][x] == '?') { lvl[y][x] = '?'; fxBurst(x * TS + 8, y * TS + 8, RGB(255, 240, 120), 3); }
}
// d = damage. soft = small hit that ignores the short invulnerability (fireballs)
void hurtBoss(int d, bool soft) {
  if (!boss.active || boss.dieT > 0) return;
  float bcx = boss.x + BOSS_W / 2, bcy = boss.y + BOSS_H / 2;
  if (boss.shield > 0) { fxSpark(bcx, bcy, RGB(180, 240, 255), 3); return; }
  if (!soft && boss.hurtT > 0) return;
  boss.hp -= d;
  if (!soft) {
    boss.hurtT = 24; shakeT = 10; hitStop = 4; fxFlash = 10; score += 300;
    fxBurst(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 240, 120), 12);
    fxSpark(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 255, 255), 10);
    fxRing(bcx, bcy, RGB(120, 255, 200), 14, 2.8f);
    spawnScore(bcx, boss.y - 6, 300);
  } else {
    score += 50; fxBurst(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 170, 50), 4);
  }
  if (boss.hp > 0) {
    int ph = (boss.hp * 3 <= boss.hpMax) ? 3 : ((boss.hp * 3 <= boss.hpMax * 2) ? 2 : 1);
    if (ph > boss.phase) {
      boss.phase = ph; boss.atk = 75; boss.shield = 80; boss.hurtT = 0;
      fxFlash = 45; shakeT = 22; hitStop = 6;
      setMsg(ph == 2 ? "UFO ANGRY!" : "UFO FURIOUS!");
      fxRing(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 90, 60), 22, 3.2f);
      fxRing(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 230, 120), 16, 2.0f);
      fxSpark(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 200, 80), 14);
      for (auto &o : orbs) if (o.alive && o.st != 1) { o.st = 1; o.t = 200; }
    }
  }
  if (boss.hp <= 0) {
    boss.hp = 0; boss.dieT = 150; boss.shield = 0;
    for (auto &o : orbs) o.alive = false;
    for (auto &b : ebul) b.alive = false;
    setMsg("UFO DOWN!");
    fxFlash = 40; hitStop = 8;
  }
}
void spawnOrb(float x, float y, float ovx, float ovy) {
  // ~10 s chase at 33 ms/frame
  for (auto &o : orbs) if (!o.alive) { o.x = x; o.y = y; o.vx = ovx; o.vy = ovy; o.st = 0; o.t = 300; o.alive = true; return; }
}
static int orbsAlive() { int n = 0; for (auto &o : orbs) if (o.alive) n++; return n; }
static bool orbBlocked(float x, float y) { return solidCh(tileAt((int)floorf(x / TS), (int)floorf(y / TS))); }

// smooth steering: aim at (tx,ty); feelers bend the heading around solid tiles
static void orbSteer(Orb &o, float tx, float ty, float spd, float blend, bool avoid) {
  float base = atan2f(ty - o.y, tx - o.x), a = base;
  if (avoid) {
    static const float offs[9] = { 0, 0.4f, -0.4f, 0.8f, -0.8f, 1.2f, -1.2f, 1.7f, -1.7f };
    for (int k = 0; k < 9; k++) {
      float aa = base + offs[k];
      if (!orbBlocked(o.x + cosf(aa) * 9, o.y + sinf(aa) * 9) && !orbBlocked(o.x + cosf(aa) * 19, o.y + sinf(aa) * 19)) { a = aa; break; }
    }
  }
  o.vx += (cosf(a) * spd - o.vx) * blend;
  o.vy += (sinf(a) * spd - o.vy) * blend;
}
static void orbMove(Orb &o, bool avoid) {
  float nx = o.x + o.vx, ny = o.y + o.vy;
  if (avoid) {
    if (orbBlocked(nx, o.y)) { o.vx *= -0.2f; nx = o.x; }
    if (orbBlocked(nx, ny)) { o.vy *= -0.2f; ny = o.y; }
  }
  o.x = nx; o.y = ny;
  if (o.x < 4) o.x = 4;
  if (o.x > LW * TS - 4) o.x = LW * TS - 4;
  if (o.y < 4) o.y = 4;
}
// true = orb consumed (hit Mario)
static bool orbVsPlayer(Orb &o) {
  if (dying || won) return false;
  if (!overlap(px, py, PW, PH, o.x - 6, o.y - 5, 12, 10)) return false;
  // jump over / stomp: feet above the orb = safe
  if (py + PH <= o.y + 3) {
    if (vy > 0.4f) {                      // stomp = send it back at the UFO
      o.st = 1; o.t = 200; vy = -5.8f; onGround = false; score += 100;
      spawnScore(o.x, o.y - 6, 100);
      fxRing(o.x, o.y, RGB(120, 255, 255), 12, 2.6f); fxSpark(o.x, o.y, RGB(200, 255, 255), 6);
    }
    return false;
  }
  if (isInvincible()) return false;
  hurt(1);
  fxBurst(o.x, o.y, RGB(255, 90, 170), 12); fxRing(o.x, o.y, RGB(255, 120, 200), 10);
  return true;
}

void updateBoss() {
  if (!boss.active) return;
  float bcx = boss.x + BOSS_W / 2, bcy = boss.y + BOSS_H / 2;
  if (boss.dieT > 0) {
    boss.dieT--; boss.t++;
    boss.y += 0.45f; shakeT = 4;
    if (boss.dieT % 4 == 0) fxBurst(boss.x + random(0, BOSS_W), boss.y + random(0, BOSS_H), RGB(255, 150 + random(100), 40), 6);
    if (boss.dieT % 9 == 0) fxSpark(bcx + random(-14, 15), bcy + random(-6, 7), RGB(255, 255, 220), 8);
    if (boss.dieT % 18 == 0) { fxRing(bcx + random(-16, 17), bcy + random(-6, 7), RGB(255, 210, 90), 14, 2.6f); fxFlash = 8; }
    if (boss.dieT == 0) {
      boss.active = false; won = true; winT = 0; score += 3000; fxFlash = 90; shakeT = 30; sfxBossDefeat();
      spawnScore(bcx, bcy - 10, 3000);
      fxRing(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 255, 255), 26, 3.6f);
      fxRing(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 200, 60), 22, 2.4f);
      fxBurst(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 255, 255), 24); fxBurst(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 140, 40), 24);
      fxSpark(bcx, bcy, RGB(255, 240, 160), 20);
    }
    return;
  }
  boss.t++;
  if (boss.hurtT) boss.hurtT--;
  if (boss.shield) boss.shield--;
  boss.refill = 0;   // open arena — no blocks to refill
  // damage smoke / sparks
  if (boss.hp * 3 <= boss.hpMax * 2 && (tAnim % 5) == 0) fxBurst(boss.x + random(4, BOSS_W - 4), boss.y + 12, RGB(95, 95, 108), 1);
  if (boss.phase == 3 && (tAnim % 7) == 0) fxSpark(boss.x + random(4, BOSS_W - 4), boss.y + 14, RGB(255, 190, 70), 2);

  if (boss.freeze > 0) boss.freeze--;
  else {
    float xMin = 10, xMax = LW * TS - BOSS_W - 10, cen = (xMin + xMax) / 2;
    boss.ph += (boss.phase == 1) ? 0.024f : ((boss.phase == 2) ? 0.032f : 0.042f);
    float amp = (boss.phase == 1) ? 8 : ((boss.phase == 2) ? 12 : 16);
    boss.x = cen + sinf(boss.ph) * (cen - xMin);
    boss.y = 28 + sinf(boss.t * 0.07f) * amp;
    if (!dying && !won && --boss.atk <= 0) {
      int n = boss.phase;
      if (orbsAlive() + n <= 6) {
        float ox = boss.x + BOSS_W / 2, oy = boss.y + BOSS_H;
        float base = atan2f(py + PH / 2 - oy, px + PW / 2 - ox);
        static const float spread[3][3] = { { 0, 0, 0 }, { -0.35f, 0.35f, 0 }, { -0.55f, 0, 0.55f } };
        for (int k = 0; k < n; k++) {
          float a = base + spread[n - 1][k];
          spawnOrb(ox, oy, cosf(a) * 1.7f, sinf(a) * 1.7f);
        }
        fxRing(ox, oy, RGB(255, 120, 200), 10, 2.4f);
        fxSpark(ox, oy, RGB(255, 180, 230), 6);
      }
      boss.atk = (boss.phase == 1) ? 130 : ((boss.phase == 2) ? 110 : 95);
    }
  }
  // contact with the saucer
  if (!dying && !won && overlap(px, py, PW, PH, boss.x + 2, boss.y + 4, BOSS_W - 4, BOSS_H - 6)) {
    if (vy > 1 && py + PH < boss.y + BOSS_H / 2 + 4) {
      hurtBoss(3); vy = -6.5f; onGround = false;
    } else hurt(1);
  }

  float spd = (boss.phase == 1) ? 1.9f : ((boss.phase == 2) ? 2.2f : 2.55f);
  float floorY = (float)((MAXH - 1) * TS - 4);
  for (auto &o : orbs) if (o.alive) {
    if (o.st == 0) {                       // FLY after Mario for 10 s
      float tx = px + PW / 2;
      float ty = py + PH * 0.35f;          // aim at chest, stay airborne
      ty += sinf(tAnim * 0.18f + o.x * 0.05f) * 10.0f;
      if (dying || won) { tx = o.x; ty = o.y; }
      orbSteer(o, tx, ty, spd, 0.13f, false);
      orbMove(o, false);
      if (o.y > floorY) { o.y = floorY; o.vy *= -0.4f; }
      if (o.y < 8) { o.y = 8; o.vy = fabsf(o.vy) * 0.4f; }
      fxTrail(o.x, o.y, (o.t < 40) ? RGB(255, 190, 60) : RGB(255, 90, 170));
      if (orbVsPlayer(o)) { o.alive = false; continue; }
      if (o.st == 0 && --o.t <= 0) {
        o.st = 1; o.t = 200;
        fxRing(o.x, o.y, RGB(255, 170, 60), 8, 1.8f);
      }
    } else {                               // fly back into the UFO
      if (o.t > 0) o.t--;
      orbSteer(o, boss.x + BOSS_W / 2, boss.y + BOSS_H / 2, 3.2f, 0.14f, false);
      orbMove(o, false);
      fxTrail(o.x, o.y, RGB(80, 220, 255));
      if (overlap(o.x - 4, o.y - 4, 8, 8, boss.x + 2, boss.y + 2, BOSS_W - 4, BOSS_H - 2)) {
        o.alive = false; hurtBoss(4);
      }
    }
  }
}

void bwClearMinions() {
  for (auto &m : mush) m.alive = false;
  for (auto &b : bombs) b.alive = false;
}
void spawnBowser(float x, float y) {
  memset(&bw, 0, sizeof(bw));
  bw.st = BW_CHASE; bw.x = x; bw.y = y; bw.hpMax = 72; bw.hp = 72;
  bw.face = -1; bw.cd = 40;
  bwClearMinions();
  levelBossCourse = true;
  setMsg("BOWSER!");
}
void spawnMush(float x, float y) {
  for (auto &m : mush) if (!m.alive) {
    m.x = x; m.y = y; m.vx = (px > x) ? 0.8f : -0.8f; m.hp = 1; m.alive = true;
    return;
  }
}
void spawnBomb(float x, float y) {
  for (auto &b : bombs) if (!b.alive) {
    b.x = x; b.y = y; b.vy = 1.2f; b.alive = true; return;
  }
}
void hurtBowser(int d) {
  if (bw.st != BW_CHASE && bw.st != BW_UFO && bw.st != BW_VULN) return;
  if (bw.cd > 12 && bw.st != BW_VULN) return;
  bw.hp -= d; if (bw.hp < 0) bw.hp = 0;
  bw.cd = 18; shakeT = 8; fxFlash = 8;
  fxBurst(bw.x + 14, bw.y + 10, RGB(255, 160, 40), 8);
  if (bw.st == BW_UFO && bw.hp <= 0) { bw.st = BW_CRASH; bw.vy = 0.4f; bw.cd = 0; setMsg("SHIP DOWN!"); }
  if (bw.st == BW_CHASE && bw.hp <= 40 && bw.ufoAt == 0) {
    bw.st = BW_UFO; bw.ufoAt = millis(); bw.vy = -2.2f; bw.cd = 20; setMsg("UFO RIDE!");
  }
  if (bw.st == BW_VULN) {
    bw.hits++;
    if (bw.hits >= 3 || bw.hp <= 0) { bw.st = BW_TALK; bw.talkI = 0; bw.talkT = 0; bw.vy = 0; }
  }
}
static void updateBowserChapter2Final() {
  if (bw.st == BW_OFF) return;
  if (bw.cd > 0) bw.cd--;
  float floorY = (MAXH - 2) * TS - 28;
  bw.face = (px > bw.x) ? 1 : -1;
  bw.vx += (bw.face * 1.05f - bw.vx) * 0.10f;
  if (bw.vx > 2.0f) bw.vx = 2.0f; if (bw.vx < -2.0f) bw.vx = -2.0f;
  bw.x += bw.vx;
  if (bw.x < 10) bw.x = 10; if (bw.x > LW*TS-40) bw.x = LW*TS-40;
  bw.vy += 0.45f; bw.y += bw.vy;
  if (bw.y > floorY) { bw.y=floorY; bw.vy=0; if (bw.cd==0 && fabsf(px-bw.x)<120) { bw.vy=-6.5f; bw.cd=34; } }
  if (!dying && !won && overlap(px,py,PW,PH,bw.x+4,bw.y+5,28,23)) {
    if (vy>0 && py+PH < bw.y+12) { hurtBowser(4); vy=-6.2f; } else if(!isInvincible()) hurt(1);
  }
  // Three distinct attacks: fireballs, shock rings, and clone summons.
  if ((tAnim % 48)==0) {
    float d = (px >= bw.x) ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
    spawnBullet(bw.x+18+d*10,bw.y+8,d*2.5f,-0.18f,2);
    spawnBullet(bw.x+18+d*10,bw.y+10,d*2.1f,0.0f,2);
    fxRing(bw.x+18,bw.y+12,RGB(255,80,45),10,2.4f);
  }
  if ((tAnim % 115)==0 && nEn < MAXEN-2) {
    spawnEnemy('q',(int)(bw.x/TS)-2,(int)(bw.y/TS));
    spawnEnemy('q',(int)(bw.x/TS)+2,(int)(bw.y/TS));
    setMsg("BOWSER CLONES!"); fxFlash=12;
  }
  if ((tAnim % 90)==0) {
    int rr=18+(int)(sinf(tAnim*0.2f)*6); fxRing(bw.x+18,bw.y+12,RGB(130,90,255),rr,1.8f);
    if (fabsf(px-(bw.x+18))<rr+20 && fabsf(py-(bw.y+12))<34 && !isInvincible()) hurt(1);
  }
  if (bw.hp <= 0) {
    bw.st=BW_OFF; won=true; winT=0; score+=5000; fxFlash=70; shakeT=28; sfxBossDefeat();
    setMsg("BOWSER DEFEATED!");
  }
}

void updateBowser() {
  if (bw.st == BW_OFF) return;
  if (chapter2Level == 10) { updateBowserChapter2Final(); return; }
  if (bw.cd > 0) bw.cd--;
  float floorY = (MAXH - 2) * TS - 28;
  if (floorY < 140) floorY = 176;

  for (auto &m : mush) if (m.alive) {
    m.x += m.vx;
    if (m.x < 8 || m.x > LW * TS - 16) m.vx = -m.vx;
    if (!dying && !won && overlap(px, py, PW, PH, m.x, m.y, 14, 14)) {
      if (vy > 0 && py + PH < m.y + 10) { m.alive = false; vy = -5.2f; score += 80; sfxEnemyDefeat(); }
      else if (!isInvincible()) hurt(1);
    }
  }
  for (auto &b : bombs) if (b.alive) {
    b.y += b.vy; b.vy += 0.12f;
    if (b.y > MAXH * TS) b.alive = false;
    else if (!dying && overlap(px, py, PW, PH, b.x - 4, b.y - 4, 8, 8)) {
      if (!isInvincible()) hurt(1);
      b.alive = false; fxBurst(b.x, b.y, RGB(255, 140, 40), 8);
    }
  }

  if (bw.st == BW_CHASE) {
    bw.face = (px > bw.x) ? 1 : -1;
    bw.vx += (bw.face * 1.15f - bw.vx) * 0.12f;
    bw.x += bw.vx;
    if (bw.x < 10) bw.x = 10;
    if (bw.x > LW * TS - 36) bw.x = LW * TS - 36;
    bw.vy += 0.45f; bw.y += bw.vy;
    if (bw.y > floorY) { bw.y = floorY; bw.vy = 0; }
    if (onGround && py + PH < bw.y - 2 && fabsf(px - bw.x) < 70 && bw.cd == 0) { bw.vy = -6.4f; bw.cd = 28; }
    if (bw.cd == 0) { spawnMush(bw.x + 8, floorY + 8); bw.cd = 70; setMsg("MUSHROOMS!"); }
    if (!dying && overlap(px, py, PW, PH, bw.x + 4, bw.y + 6, 24, 22)) {
      if (vy > 0 && py + PH < bw.y + 12) { hurtBowser(2); vy = -6.0f; }
      else if (!isInvincible()) hurt(1);
    }
    bw.talkT++;
    if (bw.talkT > 450 && bw.st == BW_CHASE) {
      bw.st = BW_UFO; bw.ufoAt = millis(); bw.vy = -2.4f; bw.talkT = 0; setMsg("UFO RIDE!");
    }
  } else if (bw.st == BW_UFO) {
    bw.x += sinf(tAnim * 0.06f) * 2.1f;
    if (bw.x < 8) bw.x = 8;
    if (bw.x > LW * TS - 48) bw.x = LW * TS - 48;
    bw.y += (36 - bw.y) * 0.08f;
    if (bw.cd == 0) { spawnBomb(bw.x + 18, bw.y + 22); bw.cd = 22; }
    if (!dying && overlap(px, py, PW, PH, bw.x, bw.y, 44, 24)) {
      if (vy > 0 && py + PH < bw.y + 14) { hurtBowser(2); vy = -6.2f; }
      else if (!isInvincible()) hurt(1);
    }
    int left = 60 - (int)((millis() - bw.ufoAt) / 1000);
    if (left < 0) left = 0;
    if (left == 0) { bw.st = BW_CRASH; bw.vy = 0.6f; setMsg("SHIP BREAKS!"); }
  } else if (bw.st == BW_CRASH) {
    bw.vy += 0.35f; bw.y += bw.vy; bw.x += 0.4f;
    if ((tAnim & 3) == 0) fxBurst(bw.x + 16, bw.y + 10, RGB(255, 160, 40), 5);
    if (bw.y >= floorY) {
      bw.y = floorY; bw.vy = 0; bw.st = BW_VULN; bw.hits = 0; bw.hp = max(bw.hp, 8);
      setMsg("GET HIM!"); shakeT = 16; fxFlash = 20;
    }
  } else if (bw.st == BW_VULN) {
    bw.vx *= 0.8f; bw.x += bw.vx;
    if (!dying && overlap(px, py, PW, PH, bw.x + 2, bw.y + 8, 26, 20)) {
      if (vy > 0 && py + PH < bw.y + 16) { hurtBowser(3); vy = -6.0f; }
      else if (!isInvincible()) hurt(1);
    }
  } else if (bw.st == BW_TALK) {
    vx = 0; vy = 0;
    bw.talkT++;
    if (bw.talkT > 90) {
      bw.talkT = 0; bw.talkI++;
      if (bw.talkI >= BW_LINES_N) { bw.st = BW_HOOK; bw.hookY = (int)bw.y; bw.talkT = 0; }
    }
    if (E & BTN_A) { bw.talkT = 91; }
  } else if (bw.st == BW_HOOK) {
    vx = 0; vy = 0;
    bw.talkT++;
    bw.hookY -= 4;
    if (bw.talkT > 55) {
      bw.st = BW_OFF; won = true; winT = 0; score += 5000; sfxBossDefeat(); setMsg("HE GOT AWAY!");
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  SCREEN HELPERS
// ============================================================
void bigMsg(const char *a, const char *b, uint16_t col) {
  tft.fillScreen(RGB(8, 8, 16));
  tft.fillRect(36, 62, 248, 116, RGB(16, 16, 28));
  tft.drawRect(36, 62, 248, 116, RGB(255, 220, 70));
  tft.drawRect(38, 64, 244, 112, RGB(80, 70, 40));
  tft.setTextColor(col); tft.drawCentreString(a, 160, 82, 4);
  tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString(b, 160, 128, 2);
  tft.fillRect(70, 158, 180, 3, col);
}
void waitA() {
  nextFrame = millis();
  readInput();
  while (true) {
    delay(15);
    readInput();
    if (E & (BTN_A | BTN_START)) return;
  }
}


// ============================================================
//  POWER ACTIVATION  (returns true when it was really used)
// ============================================================
bool activatePower(int i) {
  if (i < 0 || i >= PWR_N || !hasP(i)) return false;
  if (dying || won) return false;
  if (undergroundActive && i == PWR_ROCKET) { setMsg("ROCKET LOCKED"); return false; }
  if (onP(i)) { setMsg("ALREADY ACTIVE"); return false; }
  if (flyPhase != 0 && i != PWR_HEAL) { setMsg("BUSY"); return false; }
  if (rkState != 0) { setMsg("BUSY"); return false; }
  if (sv.usedPwr < 250) sv.usedPwr++;
  pwrAnimId = i;
  pwrAnimT = 18;

  switch (i) {
    case PWR_FLY: {
      // Hulkbuster: replace Mario's body with a full-height armor hitbox.
      int oldH = PH;
      PW = HB_W; PH = HB_H;
      py -= (PH - oldH);
      if (!undergroundActive && boxHit(px, py, PW, PH)) {
        py += (PH - oldH); PW = 12; PH = 15;
        setMsg("NO ROOM FOR HULKBUSTER");
        return false;
      }
      flyPhase = 1; flyAnim = 0; vx = 0; vy = 0;
      pwrActive[PWR_FLY] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_FLY] = 300;
      fxRing(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, RGB(255, 200, 40), 16);
      fxFlash = 25; shakeT = 8;
      break; }
    case PWR_HEAL:
      if (hp < HPMAX) hp = HPMAX;
      else if (lives < 9) lives++;
      else { setMsg("ALREADY FULL"); return false; }
      invuln = 40; pwrActive[PWR_HEAL] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_HEAL] = 26;
      fxRing(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, RGB(80, 255, 120), 14);
      fxFlash = 25; score += 50;
      break;
    case PWR_SHOCK:
      shockX = px + PW / 2; shockY = py + PH / 2; shockR = 6; shockLife = 24;
      pwrActive[PWR_SHOCK] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_SHOCK] = 24;
      fxRing(shockX, shockY, RGB(255, 230, 80), 16);
      fxFlash = 40; shakeT = 12;
      break;
    case PWR_ROCKET: {
      float tx, ty; int idx;
      if (!nearestTarget(tx, ty, idx)) { setMsg("NO TARGET"); return false; }
      rkState = 1; rkX = px - 90; rkY = py - 50; rkVx = rkVy = 0; rkLife = 240; rkTarget = -1;
      pwrActive[PWR_ROCKET] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_ROCKET] = 400;
      fxBurst(rkX, rkY, RGB(255, 140, 40), 8);
      break; }
    case PWR_INVIS:
      pwrActive[PWR_INVIS] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_INVIS] = 180;
      fxBurst(px + 6, py + 6, RGB(60, 255, 90), 10);
      break;
    case PWR_HULK: {
      // try to grow; find a spot where the big body fits
      int oldPW = PW, oldPH = PH; float ox = px, oy = py;
      PW = 18; PH = 24; py -= 9;
      const int tryX[5] = { 0, -3, 3, -6, 6 };
      bool ok = false;
      for (int k = 0; k < 5 && !ok; k++) {
        px = ox + tryX[k] - 3;
        if (undergroundActive || !boxHit(px, py, PW, PH)) ok = true;
      }
      if (!ok) { PW = oldPW; PH = oldPH; px = ox; py = oy; setMsg("NO ROOM"); return false; }
      pwrActive[PWR_HULK] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_HULK] = 210;
      fxBurst(px + 9, py + 12, RGB(60, 230, 80), 14);
      fxFlash = 30; shakeT = 10;
      break; }
    case PWR_STAR:
      pwrActive[PWR_STAR] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_STAR] = 190;
      fxBurst(px + 6, py + 6, RGB(255, 255, 80), 14);
      break;
    case PWR_FIRE:
      fireLeft = 3; fireT = 0;
      pwrActive[PWR_FIRE] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_FIRE] = 30;
      break;
    case PWR_FREEZE:
      freezeT = 210;
      if (boss.active) boss.freeze = 150;
      pwrActive[PWR_FREEZE] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_FREEZE] = 30;
      fxRing(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, RGB(150, 230, 255), 16);
      fxFlash = 40;
      break;
    case PWR_SPRING:
      // Strong but controlled launch: noticeable horizontal travel without flying across the level.
      vy = -10.2f;
      vx = face * 4.7f;
      onGround = false;
      fxRing(px + PW / 2, py + PH, RGB(120, 255, 160), 12, 2.8f);
      pwrActive[PWR_SPRING] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_SPRING] = 20;
      fxBurst(px + PW / 2, py + PH, RGB(120, 255, 160), 12);
      break;
    case PWR_CLONE:
      pwrActive[PWR_CLONE] = 1; pwrTimer[PWR_CLONE] = 600;
      cloneSpawnAll();
      fxFlash = 30; shakeT = 6;
      for(int k=0;k<4;k++){
        fxRing(px + PW/2 + (k-1)*14, py + PH/2, CLONE_COL[k][0], 10 + k*2, 2.0f);
        fxBurst(px + PW/2 + (k-1)*14, py + PH/2, CLONE_COL[k][3], 6);
      }
      setMsg("CLONE MARIO! 10s");
      break;
  }
  pwrCnt[i]--;
  fxRing(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, (i == PWR_FLY) ? RGB(255, 180, 70) : RGB(140, 230, 255), 10, 2.1f);
  fxSpark(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, RGB(255, 255, 220), 5);
  sfxAbility();
  return true;
}

void updatePowers() {
  if (pwrAnimT > 0) pwrAnimT--;
  else pwrAnimId = -1;
  for (int i = 0; i < PWR_N; i++) {
    if (pwrTimer[i] > 0) {
      pwrTimer[i]--;
      if (pwrTimer[i] == 0) {
        pwrActive[i] = 0;
        if (i == PWR_HULK) { PW = 12; PH = 15; if(!undergroundActive){py += 9; px += 3; unstick();} }
        if (i == PWR_FLY) {
          flyPhase = 0;
          if(!undergroundActive) py += (PH - 15);
          PW = 12; PH = 15;
          if(!undergroundActive) unstick();
        }
        if (i == PWR_ROCKET && rkState != 3 && rkState != 4) rkState = 0;
        if (i == PWR_CLONE) cloneReset();
      }
    }
  }
  updateClones();
  updateFriend();

  if (onP(PWR_STAR) && !dying) {
    static const uint16_t rbw[6] = { RGB(255,80,80), RGB(255,180,40), RGB(255,255,60), RGB(80,255,80), RGB(80,160,255), RGB(200,80,255) };
    fxSpark(px + random(0, PW), py + random(0, PH), rbw[(tAnim / 2) % 6], 1);
  }
  if (invuln > 0) invuln--;
  if (blinkT > 0) blinkT--;
  if (msgT > 0) msgT--;
  if (freezeT > 0) freezeT--;

  // HULKBUSTER: suit assembles, then 10 seconds of free flight
  if (onP(PWR_FLY)) {
    if (flyPhase == 1) {
      flyAnim++;
      // Armor pieces are rendered in drawPlayer so the assembly is part of the stable frame.
      int cx = (int)(px + PW / 2), cy = (int)(py + PH / 2);
      if (flyAnim % 4 == 0) fxBurst(cx + random(-14, 15), cy + random(-16, 12), RGB(255, 190, 40), 2);
      if (flyAnim >= 26) {
        flyPhase = 3; fxRing(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, RGB(255, 220, 90), 14);
        fxBurst(px + 6, py + 8, RGB(255, 60, 30), 12); fxFlash = 30; shakeT = 8;
      }
    } else if (flyPhase == 3) {
      if (tAnim & 1) fxBurst(px + 3 + random(0, 7), py + PH, RGB(80, 190, 255), 1);
    }
  }

  // FIRE: 3 bouncing fireballs, one every 8 frames
  if (fireLeft > 0 && --fireT <= 0) {
    for (auto &f : pfire) if (!f.alive) {
      f.alive = true; f.life = 110;
      f.x = px + PW / 2 + face * 8; f.y = py + 6;
      f.vx = face * 3.4f; f.vy = -0.5f;
      fxBurst(f.x, f.y, RGB(255, 150, 30), 4);
      break;
    }
    fireLeft--; fireT = 8;
  }

  // SHOCK dome
  if (shockLife > 0) {
    shockR += 4.2f; shockLife--;
    for (int i = 0; i < nEn; i++) if (en[i].alive) {
      float dx = en[i].x + 7 - shockX, dy = en[i].y + 7 - shockY;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < shockR * shockR) hitEnemy(i, 2, RGB(255, 220, 80));
    }
    if (undergroundActive) for (int i = 0; i < nUGE; i++) if (uge[i].alive) {
      float dx = uge[i].x + 8 - shockX, dy = uge[i].y + 8 - shockY;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < shockR * shockR) { uge[i].hp -= 2; fxBurst(uge[i].x+8, uge[i].y+8, RGB(255,220,80), 5); if(uge[i].hp<=0){uge[i].alive=false;score+=250;} }
    }
    if (boss.active && boss.dieT == 0) {
      float dx = boss.x + BOSS_W / 2 - shockX, dy = boss.y + BOSS_H / 2 - shockY;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (shockR + 14) * (shockR + 14)) hurtBoss(2);
    }
    if (bw.st != BW_OFF && bw.st < BW_TALK) {
      float dx = bw.x + 16 - shockX, dy = bw.y + 12 - shockY;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (shockR + 16) * (shockR + 16)) hurtBowser(2);
    }
    for (auto &o : orbs) if (o.alive && o.st != 1) {
      float dx = o.x - shockX, dy = o.y - shockY;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < shockR * shockR) { o.st = 1; o.t = 200; fxRing(o.x, o.y, RGB(150, 255, 255), 10, 2.6f); fxSpark(o.x, o.y, RGB(255, 255, 255), 6); }
    }
    for (auto &b : ebul) if (b.alive) {
      float dx = b.x - shockX, dy = b.y - shockY;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < shockR * shockR) { b.alive = false; fxBurst(b.x, b.y, RGB(255, 230, 120), 4); }
    }
  }
  if (boomLife > 0) {
    boomR += 3.6f; boomLife--;
    for (int i = 0; i < nEn; i++) if (en[i].alive) {
      float dx = en[i].x + 7 - boomX, dy = en[i].y + 7 - boomY;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < boomR * boomR) hitEnemy(i, 3, RGB(255, 140, 40));
    }
    if (boss.active && boss.dieT == 0) {
      float dx = boss.x + BOSS_W / 2 - boomX, dy = boss.y + BOSS_H / 2 - boomY;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (boomR + 14) * (boomR + 14)) hurtBoss(3);
    }
    if (bw.st != BW_OFF && bw.st < BW_TALK) {
      float dx = bw.x + 16 - boomX, dy = bw.y + 12 - boomY;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (boomR + 16) * (boomR + 16)) hurtBowser(3);
    }
  }

  // ROCKET: fly in -> grab Mario -> ride to target -> boom
  if (rkState == 1) {
    float tx = px + PW / 2, ty = py + 4;
    float dx = tx - rkX, dy = ty - rkY;
    float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy) + 0.01f;
    rkVx = dx / len * 3.6f; rkVy = dy / len * 3.6f;
    rkX += rkVx; rkY += rkVy;
    if (tAnim & 1) fxBurst(rkX - 6, rkY, RGB(255, 140, 40), 1);
    if (--rkLife <= 0) { rkState = 0; pwrActive[PWR_ROCKET] = 0; }
    if (len < 14) { rkState = 2; rkLife = 16; score += 80; fxRing(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, RGB(255, 160, 40), 10); }
  } else if (rkState == 2) {
    rkX = px + PW / 2; rkY = py - 6;
    if (--rkLife <= 0) {
      float tx, ty; int idx;
      if (nearestTarget(tx, ty, idx)) rkTarget = idx; else rkTarget = -1;
      rkState = 3; rkLife = 80; vx = 0; vy = 0;
    }
  } else if (rkState == 3) {
    float tx, ty; int idx;
    if (rkTarget >= 0 && rkTarget < nEn && en[rkTarget].alive) { tx = en[rkTarget].x + 7; ty = en[rkTarget].y + 7; }
    else if (rkTarget == -2 && boss.active && boss.dieT == 0) { tx = boss.x + BOSS_W / 2; ty = boss.y + BOSS_H / 2; }
    else if (nearestTarget(tx, ty, idx)) rkTarget = idx;
    else { tx = px + face * 80; ty = py; }
    float dx = tx - rkX, dy = ty - rkY;
    float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy) + 0.01f;
    rkVx = dx / len * 5.0f; rkVy = dy / len * 5.0f;
    rkX += rkVx; rkY += rkVy;
    px = rkX - PW / 2; py = rkY - PH / 2;
    if (rkVx > 0.3f) face = 1; else if (rkVx < -0.3f) face = -1;
    fxBurst(rkX - rkVx * 3, rkY - rkVy * 3, RGB(255, 120, 30), 2);
    if (--rkLife <= 0 || len < 16) {
      boomX = rkX; boomY = rkY; boomR = 8; boomLife = 18;
      fxRing(boomX, boomY, RGB(255, 200, 60), 16);
      fxBurst(boomX, boomY, RGB(255, 80, 20), 16);
      fxFlash = 35; shakeT = 14;
      rkState = 4; rkLife = 16;
    }
  } else if (rkState == 4) {
    if (--rkLife <= 0) {
      rkState = 0; pwrActive[PWR_ROCKET] = 0;
      unstick(); vy = -3.5f; onGround = false; invuln = 30;
    }
  }
}

// player fireballs
void updateFire() {
  for (auto &f : pfire) if (f.alive) {
    f.x += f.vx;
    if (!undergroundActive && solidCh(tileAt((int)floorf((f.x + (f.vx > 0 ? 3 : -3)) / TS), (int)floorf(f.y / TS)))) {
      f.alive = false; fxBurst(f.x, f.y, RGB(255, 140, 30), 5); continue;
    }
    f.vy += 0.3f; f.y += f.vy;
    if (!undergroundActive && f.vy > 0 && solidCh(tileAt((int)floorf(f.x / TS), (int)floorf((f.y + 3) / TS)))) { f.y -= f.vy; f.vy = -3.0f; }
    else if (undergroundActive && f.vy > 0 && f.y >= UG_FLOOR - 5) { f.y = UG_FLOOR - 5; f.vy = -3.0f; }
    if (f.y > MAXH * TS + 8 || --f.life == 0) { f.alive = false; continue; }
    if ((tAnim & 1) == 0) fxBurst(f.x, f.y, RGB(255, 200, 60), 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < nEn; i++) if (en[i].alive && overlap(f.x - 3, f.y - 3, 6, 6, en[i].x, en[i].y, 14, 14)) {
      hitEnemy(i, 1, RGB(255, 140, 30)); f.alive = false;
      fxBurst(f.x, f.y, RGB(255, 140, 30), 6); break;
    }
    if (undergroundActive && f.alive) for (int i=0;i<nUGE;i++) if(uge[i].alive && overlap(f.x-3,f.y-3,6,6,uge[i].x,uge[i].y,(uge[i].kind==0)?30:16,(uge[i].kind==0)?24:14)) {
      uge[i].hp--; f.alive=false; fxBurst(f.x,f.y,RGB(255,140,30),6); if(uge[i].hp<=0){uge[i].alive=false;score+=250;sfxEnemyDefeat();} break;
    }
    if (f.alive && boss.active && boss.dieT == 0 &&
        overlap(f.x - 3, f.y - 3, 6, 6, boss.x, boss.y, BOSS_W, BOSS_H)) {
      hurtBoss(1, true); f.alive = false;
    }
    if (f.alive && bw.st != BW_OFF && bw.st != BW_TALK && bw.st != BW_HOOK &&
        overlap(f.x - 3, f.y - 3, 6, 6, bw.x, bw.y, 32, 28)) {
      hurtBowser(1); f.alive = false;
    }
  }
}

// enemy bullets
void updateBullets() {
  for (auto &b : ebul) if (b.alive) {
    b.x += b.vx; b.y += b.vy;
    if (b.x < camX - 40 || b.x > camX + SCRW + 40 || b.y < -20 || b.y > 260) { b.alive = false; continue; }
    if (solidCh(tileAt((int)floorf(b.x / TS), (int)floorf(b.y / TS)))) { b.alive = false; fxBurst(b.x, b.y, RGB(255, 160, 60), 3); continue; }
    if (!dying && !won && overlap(px, py, PW, PH, b.x - 3, b.y - 3, 6, 6)) {
      if (!isInvincible()) { hurt(1); b.alive = false; fxBurst(b.x, b.y, RGB(255, 120, 60), 6); }
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  START PORTAL - Mario must run forward out of the portal
// ============================================================
void updateStartPortal() {
  startPortalActive = false;
  startPortalClose = 0;
}


void drawStartPortal(int y0) {
  (void)y0;
}



// ============================================================
//  HULKBUSTER BODY / CRUSH
//  The armor has its own full-height body and collision box.
// ============================================================
void hulkbusterCrushPath() {
  if (flyPhase != 3 || !onP(PWR_FLY)) return;
  // Break solid blocks in the armor's footprint, except the floor (#),
  // so the player does not accidentally erase the level floor.
  int x0 = (int)floorf(px / TS) - 1;
  int x1 = (int)floorf((px + PW) / TS) + 1;
  int y0 = (int)floorf(py / TS) - 1;
  int y1 = (int)floorf((py + PH) / TS) + 1;
  for (int ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) for (int tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
    if (tx < 0 || ty < 0 || tx >= LW || ty >= MAXH) continue;
    char c = lvl[ty][tx];
    if (c == 'B' || c == '?' || c == 'U' || c == 'S' || c == 'L' || c == 'R' || c == 'l' || c == 'r') {
      lvl[ty][tx] = '.';
      score += 20;
      fxBurst(tx * TS + 8, ty * TS + 8, RGB(150, 210, 240), 5);
      fxSpark(tx * TS + 8, ty * TS + 8, RGB(255, 220, 100), 3);
    }
  }
  // Everything enemy-sized in the body path gets crushed.
  for (int i = 0; i < nEn; i++) if (en[i].alive) {
    if (overlap(px, py, PW, PH, en[i].x, en[i].y, 14, 14))
      hitEnemy(i, 99, RGB(120, 230, 255));
  }
  if (boss.active && boss.dieT == 0 &&
      overlap(px, py, PW, PH, boss.x, boss.y, BOSS_W, BOSS_H)) {
    hurtBoss(3);
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  PLAYER
// ============================================================
void updatePlayer() {
  // Portal removed: Mario can move immediately when a level starts.

  if (rkState >= 2 && rkState <= 4) return;

  // B is now an action button, not a speed toggle. Running is always enabled.
  if ((E & BTN_B) && !dying && !won) {
    if (onGround && rollT == 0) {
      rollT = 16; actionT = 16;
      vx = face * 4.6f;
      fxBurst(px + PW / 2, py + PH - 2, RGB(210, 230, 255), 5);
      fxRing(px + PW / 2, py + PH, RGB(170, 210, 255), 8, 2.4f);
      sfxRoll();
    } else if (!onGround && flipT == 0) {
      flipT = 18; actionT = 18;
      vy = -5.4f;
      fxBurst(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, RGB(255, 230, 90), 6);
      sfxFlip();
    }
  }

  if (rollT > 0) {
    rollT--;
    vx = face * 4.6f;
  }
  if (flipT > 0) flipT--;
  if (actionT > 0) actionT--;

  // ---- flight ----
  if (flyPhase == 1) { vx = 0; vy = 0; onGround = false; return; }
  if (flyPhase == 3) {
    float tvx = 0, tvy = -0.2f;
    if (H & BTN_LEFT)       { tvx = -4.1f; face = -1; }
    else if (H & BTN_RIGHT) { tvx =  4.1f; face = 1; }
    if (H & BTN_UP)         tvy = -3.8f;
    else if ((H & BTN_DOWN) || (H & BTN_B)) tvy = 4.2f;
    vx += (tvx - vx) * 0.28f; vy += (tvy - vy) * 0.28f;
    onGround = false;
    movePX(vx); movePY(vy);
    hulkbusterCrushPath();
    if (py < 0) { py = 0; vy = 0; }
    if (py > MAXH * TS + 8) killNow();
    return;
  }

  float acc = 0.28f;
  float maxv = 3.2f;
  if (onP(PWR_HULK)) { acc = 0.30f; maxv = 3.5f; }

  // Real running skid: reversing direction keeps momentum for a moment,
  // throws sparks/dust, then snaps Mario into the new direction.
  int inputDir = (H & BTN_LEFT) ? -1 : ((H & BTN_RIGHT) ? 1 : 0);
  if (onGround && inputDir != 0 && vx * inputDir < -1.15f) {
    skidT = 9;
    vx *= 0.88f;
    face = inputDir;
    if ((tAnim & 1) == 0) {
      fxDust(px + PW / 2 - face * 4, py + PH);
      fxSpark(px + PW / 2 - face * 6, py + PH - 2, RGB(255, 225, 120), 2);
    }
  } else if (skidT > 0) {
    skidT--;
  }

  if (inputDir < 0) { vx -= acc; face = -1; }
  else if (inputDir > 0) { vx += acc; face = 1; }
  else { vx *= 0.82f; if (fabsf(vx) < 0.05f) vx = 0; }
  if (vx > maxv) vx = maxv;
  if (vx < -maxv) vx = -maxv;

  // skid puff
  if (onGround && (((H & BTN_LEFT) && vx > 1.3f) || ((H & BTN_RIGHT) && vx < -1.3f))) fxDust(px + PW / 2, py + PH);
  else if (onGround && fabsf(vx) > 2.9f && (tAnim % 6) == 0) fxDust(px + PW / 2 - face * 4, py + PH);

  // coyote time + jump buffer = forgiving, Mario-like jumps
  if (onGround) coyote = 6; else if (coyote > 0) coyote--;
  if (E & BTN_A) jbuf = 6; else if (jbuf > 0) jbuf--;
  if (jbuf > 0 && coyote > 0) {
    vy = onP(PWR_HULK) ? -8.8f : -7.4f;
    jbuf = 0; coyote = 0;
    sfxJump();
    fxBurst(px + PW / 2, py + PH, RGB(230, 240, 255), 4);
  }
  if (vy < 0) bumpNearby();
  if (!(H & BTN_A) && vy < -2.5f) vy = -2.5f;
  vy += onP(PWR_HULK) ? 0.55f : 0.5f;
  if (vy > 8) vy = 8;

  bool was = onGround;
  onGround = false;
  movePX(vx);
  movePY(vy);
  if (onGround && !was && vy == 0) {
    landT = 8;
    fxDust(px + PW / 2, py + PH);
    if (onP(PWR_HULK)) { fxRing(px + PW / 2, py + PH, RGB(120, 255, 100), 12, 2.6f); shakeT = 6; }
  }
  if (onGround && fabsf(vx) > 2.9f && (tAnim % 3) == 0) fxSpark(px + PW / 2 - face * 6, py + PH - 3, RGB(255, 255, 255), 1);
  if (onGround) animT += fabsf(vx);
  if (landT > 0) landT--;
  if (py > MAXH * TS + 8) killNow();

  ridePl = -1;
  if (onGround) {
    for (int i = 0; i < nPl; i++) {
      if (overlap(px, py + PH - 2, PW, 4, plats[i].x, plats[i].y, TS * 2, 8)) { ridePl = i; break; }
    }
  }
}

void updatePlats() {
  for (int i = 0; i < nPl; i++) {
    MPlat &p = plats[i];
    float *v = (p.axis == 0) ? &p.x : &p.y;
    *v += p.spd;
    if (*v < p.minv) { *v = p.minv; p.spd = fabsf(p.spd); }
    if (*v > p.maxv) { *v = p.maxv; p.spd = -fabsf(p.spd); }
  }
  if (ridePl >= 0 && ridePl < nPl && !dying && !won) {
    if (plats[ridePl].axis == 0) movePX(plats[ridePl].spd);
    else { py += plats[ridePl].spd; }
  }
}

static bool findMate(char kind, int sx, int sy, int &dx, int &dy) {
  int seen = 0, fx = -1, fy = -1;
  for (int y = 0; y < MAXH; y++)
    for (int x = 0; x < LW; x++) {
      if (lvl[y][x] != kind && !(kind == 'L' && (lvl[y][x] == 'L' || lvl[y][x] == 'R'))) continue;
      if (kind != 'L' && lvl[y][x] != kind) continue;
      if (x == sx && y == sy) { seen = 1; continue; }
      if (seen) { dx = x; dy = y; return true; }
      if (fx < 0) { fx = x; fy = y; }
    }
  if (fx >= 0) { dx = fx; dy = fy; return true; }
  return false;
}

void installEndPipe() {
  endPipeX = -1; pipeStandT = 0;
  if (levelBossCourse || LW < 12) return;
  endPipeX = LW - 8;
  if (endPipeX + 1 >= LW) endPipeX = LW - 3;
  // Sit the exit pipe on the first solid row at the end, so it is never buried.
  int ground = MAXH - 2;
  for (int y = 0; y < MAXH - 1; y++) {
    char a = lvl[y][endPipeX], b = lvl[y][endPipeX + 1];
    if (a == '#' || a == 'S' || a == 'B' || b == '#' || b == 'S' || b == 'B') { ground = y; break; }
  }
  endPipeTop = ground - 1;
  if (endPipeTop < 3) endPipeTop = 3;
  if (endPipeTop > MAXH - 4) endPipeTop = MAXH - 4;
  for (int y = 0; y <= endPipeTop + 1 && y < MAXH; y++) {
    if (y < ground) {
      lvl[y][endPipeX] = '.';
      lvl[y][endPipeX + 1] = '.';
    }
  }
  lvl[endPipeTop][endPipeX] = 'L';
  lvl[endPipeTop][endPipeX + 1] = 'R';
  if (endPipeTop + 1 < MAXH) {
    lvl[endPipeTop + 1][endPipeX] = 'l';
    lvl[endPipeTop + 1][endPipeX + 1] = 'r';
  }
  // Keep a walkable floor under / beside the pipe.
  if (ground < MAXH) {
    if (endPipeX > 0 && lvl[ground][endPipeX - 1] == '.') lvl[ground][endPipeX - 1] = '#';
    if (endPipeX + 2 < LW && lvl[ground][endPipeX + 2] == '.') lvl[ground][endPipeX + 2] = '#';
  }
}

void beginUnderground() {
  if (undergroundActive || levelBossCourse || endPipeX < 0) return;
  undergroundEntering=true; undergroundActive=true; undergroundExiting=false;
  ugTimer=0; ugFade=0; ugX=54; ugY=UG_CEIL+8; ugVY=0; ugCamF=0;
  ugLiftIn = UG_CEIL + 22; ugLiftOut = UG_FLOOR;
  dying=false; won=false; onGround=true; vx=vy=0; nUGE=0;
  px = UG_IN_X + 8; py = ugLiftIn - PH; camX = 0;
  const uint8_t kinds[] = {0,1,0,2,1,3,0,2,1,0,3,1,0};
  const int xs[] = {360,520,700,860,1010,1180,1330,1480,1630,1810,2010,2250,2450};
  for (int i=0;i<13;i++) {
    uint8_t kk = inChapter2 ? 0 : kinds[i];
    uge[i].x=xs[i];
    if (kk==1) uge[i].y=72;
    else if (kk==3) uge[i].y=128;
    else uge[i].y=UG_FLOOR-18;
    uge[i].vx=(kk==2)?0.0f:((i&1)?-0.65f:0.65f);
    uge[i].vy=0;
    uge[i].hp=(kk==0)?4:3; uge[i].hpMax=uge[i].hp;
    uge[i].cd=40+i*9; uge[i].phase=i*13;
    uge[i].pose=(kk==0)?70:0;
    uge[i].kind=kk; uge[i].alive=true;
  }
  nUGE=13; for(auto &b:ebul) b.alive=false;
  sfxPipe(); fxFlash=20; shakeT=8;
}

void updateUndergroundEnemies() {
  for(int i=0;i<nUGE;i++) {
    UGEnemy &e=uge[i]; if(!e.alive) continue; if(e.cd)e.cd--; e.phase++;
    if(e.kind==0) {
      // Slime lives on the floor. Crawl toward Mario; hop only to close a gap or reach him above.
      if (e.pose >= 0 && e.y >= UG_FLOOR - 25 && e.vy >= 0) {
        e.y = UG_FLOOR - 18;
        e.vy = 0;
        float dist = px - e.x;
        float wave = sinf(e.phase * 0.31f);
        e.vx = ((dist > 0) ? 0.62f : -0.62f) + wave * 0.28f;
        e.x += e.vx;
        if (e.x < 40) e.x = 40;
        if (e.x > UG_EXIT_X - 20) e.x = UG_EXIT_X - 20;
        if ((e.phase & 3) == 0) fxDust(e.x + 16, UG_FLOOR);
        bool close = fabsf(dist) < 86;
        bool above = (py + PH < e.y + 2) && fabsf(dist) < 70;
        if (e.pose > 0) e.pose--;
        if (e.pose <= 0 && (close || above)) {
          e.y = UG_FLOOR - 24;
          e.vy = above ? -7.0f : -5.4f;
          e.vx = (dist > 0) ? 1.05f : -1.05f;
          e.pose = -1;
        } else if (e.pose <= 0) {
          e.pose = 24;
        }
      } else {
        e.vy += 0.48f; e.y += e.vy; e.x += e.vx;
        if (e.y >= UG_FLOOR - 18) {
          e.y = UG_FLOOR - 18; e.vy = 0; e.pose = 48;
          fxDust(e.x + 16, UG_FLOOR);
        }
      }
    } else if(e.kind==1) {
      // Bat stays in the air. Cruise high, dive only if Mario is below and near.
      if (e.pose > 22) {
        e.y += 2.4f; e.x += (px > e.x) ? 0.45f : -0.45f; e.pose--;
        if (e.y > UG_FLOOR - 36) { e.y = UG_FLOOR - 36; e.pose = 22; }
      } else if (e.pose > 0) {
        e.y -= 2.1f; e.x += e.vx * 0.4f; e.pose--;
        if (e.y < 56) e.y = 56;
      } else {
        e.x += e.vx * 1.15f;
        e.y = 70 + sinf(e.phase * 0.10f + i) * 16;
        if (e.x < 80 || e.x > UG_EXIT_X) e.vx = -e.vx;
        if (fabsf(px - e.x) < 78 && py > e.y + 8 && e.cd == 0) { e.pose = 38; e.cd = 70; }
      }
    } else if(e.kind==2) {
      // Turret is planted. Face Mario and fire only when he is in range.
      e.vx = 0;
      e.y = UG_FLOOR - 16;
      if (e.cd == 0 && fabsf(px - e.x) < 190 && fabsf(py - e.y) < 90) {
        spawnBullet(e.x + ((px > e.x) ? 14 : -2), e.y + 6, (px > e.x) ? 2.0f : -2.0f, 0, 0);
        e.cd = 88;
      }
    } else {
      // Mage keeps mid-range: backs off if too close, steps in if too far, then casts.
      float dx = px - e.x;
      if (fabsf(dx) < 95) e.x -= (dx > 0 ? 0.75f : -0.75f);
      else if (fabsf(dx) > 168) e.x += (dx > 0 ? 0.48f : -0.48f);
      e.y = 126 + sinf(e.phase * 0.11f) * 16;
      if (e.x < 80) e.x = 80;
      if (e.x > UG_EXIT_X - 10) e.x = UG_EXIT_X - 10;
      if (e.cd == 0 && fabsf(dx) > 70 && fabsf(dx) < 210) {
        float bx = (dx > 0) ? 1.9f : -1.9f;
        float by = (py > e.y) ? 0.45f : -0.25f;
        spawnBullet(e.x + 8, e.y + 6, bx, by, 1);
        e.cd = 86;
        fxRing(e.x + 8, e.y + 6, RGB(170, 120, 255), 8, 1.5f);
      }
    }
    float ew = (e.kind == 0) ? ((e.pose > 0) ? 36.0f : 28.0f) : 16.0f;
    float eh = (e.kind == 0) ? ((e.pose > 0) ? 16.0f : 24.0f) : 14.0f;
    if(!dying&&!won&&overlap(px,py,PW,PH,e.x,e.y,ew,eh)) {
      if((e.kind==0&&py+PH<e.y+10&&vy>0)||onP(PWR_HULK)||onP(PWR_STAR)) {
        e.hp-=onP(PWR_HULK)?3:1;vy=-6.0f;score+=120;fxRing(e.x+ew/2,e.y+eh/2,RGB(120,255,160),12,2.2f);fxBurst(e.x+ew/2,e.y+eh/2,RGB(170,240,255),8);
        if(e.hp<=0){e.alive=false;sfxEnemyDefeat();score+=250;}
      } else if(!isInvincible()) hurt(1);
    }
  }
}

void updateUnderground() {
  ugTimer++; tAnim++;
  if (undergroundEntering) {
    ugLiftIn += 2.3f;
    vx = 0; vy = 0;
    px = UG_IN_X + 8; py = ugLiftIn - PH; onGround = true;
    if (ugLiftIn >= UG_FLOOR) {
      ugLiftIn = UG_FLOOR; py = UG_FLOOR - PH;
      undergroundEntering = false;
      setMsg("UNDERGROUND!");
    }
  }
  if(dying)return;
  if (undergroundEntering) {
    ugCamF+=((px+PW/2)-SCRW/2-ugCamF)*0.16f;if(ugCamF<0)ugCamF=0;camX=(int)ugCamF;
    return;
  }
  if (undergroundExiting) {
    vx=0; vy=0; onGround=true;
    px = UG_EXIT_X + 14;
    ugLiftOut -= 2.5f;
    py = ugLiftOut - PH;
    if (ugLiftOut < UG_CEIL + 10) {
      undergroundExiting=false; won=true; winT=0; score+=1500; sfxLevelClear();
    }
    ugCamF+=((px+PW/2)-SCRW/2-ugCamF)*0.16f;if(ugCamF<0)ugCamF=0;
    float mx2=UG_W*TS-SCRW;if(ugCamF>mx2)ugCamF=mx2;camX=(int)ugCamF;
    return;
  }
  if(flyPhase==1){vx=vy=0;onGround=false;return;}
  if(flyPhase==3){
    float tvx=0,tvy=0; if(H&BTN_LEFT){tvx=-4.1f;face=-1;} else if(H&BTN_RIGHT){tvx=4.1f;face=1;}
    if(H&BTN_UP)tvy=-3.8f; else if(H&BTN_DOWN)tvy=3.8f;
    vx+=(tvx-vx)*0.25f;vy+=(tvy-vy)*0.25f;px+=vx;py+=vy;onGround=false;
    if(px<20)px=20;if(px>UG_W*TS-24)px=UG_W*TS-24;if(py<UG_CEIL)py=UG_CEIL;if(py>UG_FLOOR-PH)py=UG_FLOOR-PH;
    for(int i=0;i<nUGE;i++)if(uge[i].alive&&overlap(px,py,PW,PH,uge[i].x,uge[i].y,(uge[i].kind==0)?30:16,(uge[i].kind==0)?24:14)){uge[i].hp-=3;fxBurst(uge[i].x+8,uge[i].y+8,RGB(120,230,255),8);if(uge[i].hp<=0){uge[i].alive=false;score+=250;}}
  } else {
    int dir=(H&BTN_LEFT)?-1:((H&BTN_RIGHT)?1:0); if(dir){face=dir;vx+=dir*0.25f;}else vx*=0.84f;
    float maxv=onP(PWR_HULK)?3.6f:3.2f;vx=constrain(vx,-maxv,maxv);
    if((E&BTN_A)&&onGround){vy=onP(PWR_HULK)?-8.6f:-7.4f;onGround=false;sfxJump();fxDust(px+PW/2,UG_FLOOR);}
    if(!(H&BTN_A)&&vy<-2.5f)vy=-2.5f;vy+=0.5f;if(vy>8)vy=8;px+=vx;py+=vy;
    if(py>=UG_FLOOR-PH){py=UG_FLOOR-PH;vy=0;onGround=true;}else onGround=false;
    if(py<UG_CEIL){py=UG_CEIL;vy=0;}if(px<20)px=20;if(px>UG_W*TS-24)px=UG_W*TS-24;
    if(onGround&&fabsf(vx)>2.5f&&(tAnim&3)==0)fxDust(px+PW/2,UG_FLOOR); updateUndergroundEnemies();
  }
  for(auto &b:ebul)if(b.alive){b.x+=b.vx;b.y+=b.vy;if(b.x<ugCamF-30||b.x>ugCamF+SCRW+30||b.y<10||b.y>UG_FLOOR){b.alive=false;continue;}if(overlap(px,py,PW,PH,b.x-3,b.y-3,6,6)){if(!isInvincible())hurt(1);b.alive=false;}}
  if(onGround && px+PW/2>=UG_EXIT_X+6 && px+PW/2<=UG_EXIT_X+42){
    undergroundExiting=true; sfxElevator(); fxFlash=18; ugLiftOut=UG_FLOOR; setMsg("LIFT UP!");
  }
  ugCamF+=((px+PW/2)-SCRW/2-ugCamF)*0.16f;if(ugCamF<0)ugCamF=0;float mx=UG_W*TS-SCRW;if(ugCamF>mx)ugCamF=mx;camX=(int)ugCamF;
}

static uint16_t caveCol(char c) {
  switch (c) {
    case 'G': return RGB(64, 186, 86);
    case 'N': return RGB(24, 92, 44);
    case 'L': return RGB(150, 232, 160);
    case 'P': return RGB(156, 78, 214);
    case 'Q': return RGB(92, 40, 140);
    case 'A': return RGB(210, 170, 255);
    case 'T': return RGB(150, 154, 178);
    case 'E': return RGB(70, 74, 96);
    case 'F': return RGB(214, 58, 58);
    case 'O': return RGB(255, 186, 60);
    case 'C': return RGB(255, 220, 150);
    case 'S': return RGB(255, 204, 150);
    case 'K': return TFT_BLACK;
    case 'W': return TFT_WHITE;
    case 'R': return RGB(224, 36, 40);
    case 'B': return RGB(110, 58, 20);
    case 'U': return RGB(70, 60, 150);
    case 'Y': return RGB(255, 220, 60);
    case 'M': return RGB(78, 36, 16);
  }
  return 0;
}
void drawPixSpr(int x, int y, int dw, int dh, const char *const *rows, int nrows, int ncols, bool flip) {
  if (y + dh <= 0 || y >= BANDH) return;
  for (int r = 0; r < nrows; r++) {
    int y0 = y + r * dh / nrows, y1 = y + (r + 1) * dh / nrows, hh = y1 - y0;
    if (hh <= 0 || y1 <= 0 || y0 >= BANDH) continue;
    int c = 0;
    while (c < ncols) {
      char ch = rows[r][flip ? (ncols - 1 - c) : c];
      int c2 = c + 1;
      while (c2 < ncols && rows[r][flip ? (ncols - 1 - c2) : c2] == ch) c2++;
      if (ch != '.') {
        int xa = x + c * dw / ncols, xb = x + c2 * dw / ncols;
        spr.fillRect(xa, y0, xb - xa, hh, caveCol(ch));
      }
      c = c2;
    }
  }
}
