void drawLiftCabin(int x, int platY, int y0, bool upLight) {
  int top = 18 - y0;
  int py = platY - y0;
  spr.drawFastVLine(x + 4, top, py - top, RGB(90, 100, 120));
  spr.drawFastVLine(x + 36, top, py - top, RGB(90, 100, 120));
  spr.fillRect(x + 2, top, 38, 6, RGB(70, 78, 96));
  spr.fillRoundRect(x, py - 28, 42, 30, 3, RGB(48, 52, 68));
  spr.drawRoundRect(x, py - 28, 42, 30, 3, RGB(170, 180, 210));
  spr.fillRect(x + 6, py - 22, 12, 10, RGB(12, 16, 24));
  spr.fillRect(x + 24, py - 22, 12, 10, RGB(12, 16, 24));
  spr.fillRect(x + 2, py - 4, 38, 6, RGB(90, 200, 120));
  spr.drawFastHLine(x + 2, py - 4, 38, RGB(200, 255, 210));
  spr.setTextColor(upLight ? RGB(255, 230, 80) : RGB(140, 230, 255));
  spr.drawCentreString(upLight ? "UP" : "DOWN", x + 21, py - 18, 1);
}

void drawUndergroundBand(int y0) {
  spr.fillSprite(RGB(5,5,9));
  for(int y=0;y<BANDH;y+=4)spr.fillRect(0,y, SCRW,4,RGB(4+y/30,4+y/40,8+y/20));
  int cam=(int)ugCamF;
  for(int x=-16;x<SCRW+16;x+=TS){int wx=cam+x,seed=(wx/TS)&7,cy=UG_CEIL-(seed==0?8:0),fy=UG_FLOOR+((seed==3)?3:0);if(cy-y0<BANDH&&cy-y0+12>0)spr.fillRect(x,cy-y0,TS,14,RGB(24,24,34));if(fy-y0<BANDH&&fy-y0+16>0){spr.fillRect(x,fy-y0,TS,16,RGB(34,30,42));spr.drawFastHLine(x,fy-y0,TS,RGB(62,54,72));}}
  for(int lx=80;lx<UG_W*TS;lx+=270){int sx=lx-cam,sy=58-y0;if(sx<-50||sx>SCRW+50)continue;spr.fillCircle(sx,sy+35,30,RGB(18,15,18));spr.fillCircle(sx,sy+35,22,RGB(30,24,20));spr.fillCircle(sx,sy+35,13,RGB(62,45,26));spr.fillCircle(sx,sy+35,5+((tAnim>>2)&1),RGB(255,185,70));spr.drawFastVLine(sx,sy+8,20,RGB(75,65,72));}
  for(int i=0;i<18;i++){int wx=140+i*157,sx=wx-cam,yy=(i&1)?UG_FLOOR-4:UG_CEIL+8;if(sx>-10&&sx<SCRW+10){uint16_t cc=(i&1)?RGB(80,180,170):RGB(120,90,190);spr.fillTriangle(sx-4,yy-y0,sx,yy-16-y0,sx+5,yy-y0,cc);}}
  int ix=UG_IN_X-cam; if(ix>-50&&ix<SCRW+50) drawLiftCabin(ix, (int)ugLiftIn, y0, false);
  int ex=UG_EXIT_X-cam; if(ex>-50&&ex<SCRW+50) drawLiftCabin(ex, (int)ugLiftOut, y0, true);
  for(int i=0;i<nUGE;i++)if(uge[i].alive){
    UGEnemy &e=uge[i];
    int x=(int)e.x-cam, y=(int)e.y-y0;
    if(x<-50||x>SCRW+50||y<-50||y>BANDH+50) continue;
    bool flip = (px < e.x);
    if(e.kind==0){
      bool crawl = (e.pose > 0);
      const char *const *fr = crawl ? SP_SLIME2 : SP_SLIME;
      int dw = crawl ? 28 : 22, dh = crawl ? 16 : 20;
      spr.fillEllipse(x + dw/2, y + dh - 1, dw/2, 3, RGB(12,10,16));
      drawPixSpr(x, y, dw, dh, fr, 10, 12, flip);
      drawHpBar(x + 2, y - 8, 22, e.hp, e.hpMax, RGB(80, 255, 120));
    } else if(e.kind==1){
      const char *const *fr = ((e.phase / 4) & 1) ? SP_BAT2 : SP_BAT;
      spr.fillEllipse(x + 12, y + 16, 8, 2, RGB(12,10,16));
      drawPixSpr(x, y, 24, 18, fr, 10, 12, flip);
      drawHpBar(x + 2, y - 8, 18, e.hp, e.hpMax, RGB(255, 90, 140));
    } else if(e.kind==2){
      drawPixSpr(x - 2, y - 2, 24, 22, SP_TUR, 12, 12, flip);
      if (e.cd < 18) spr.fillCircle(x + (flip ? 2 : 20), y + 8, 2 + ((tAnim>>1)&1), RGB(255, 210, 70));
      drawHpBar(x, y - 10, 18, e.hp, e.hpMax, RGB(255, 90, 70));
    } else {
      int hover = (int)(sinf(e.phase * 0.12f) * 2.0f);
      drawPixSpr(x - 2, y - 4 + hover, 22, 24, SP_MAGE, 12, 12, flip);
      drawHpBar(x, y - 12 + hover, 18, e.hp, e.hpMax, RGB(140, 210, 255));
    }
  }
  for(auto &b:ebul)if(b.alive){int x=(int)b.x-cam,y=(int)b.y-y0;if(y>-8&&y<BANDH+8){spr.fillCircle(x,y,4,RGB(255,100,70));spr.fillCircle(x,y,2,RGB(255,230,130));}}
  drawCloneMarios(y0,cam);
  drawFriendWorld(y0,cam);
  drawPlayer((int)px-cam,(int)py-y0);fxWorld(cam,y0);
  if(y0==0){
    for (int i = 0; i < HPMAX; i++) drawHeart(4 + i * 11, 4, i < hp);
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(255, 220, 80));
    char buf[20];
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%d/%d", hp, HPMAX);
    spr.drawString(buf, 40, 16, 1);
    spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "x%d", coins);
    spr.fillCircle(50, 8, 4, RGB(255, 220, 30)); spr.drawFastVLine(50, 5, 6, RGB(200, 140, 0));
    spr.drawString(buf, 57, 0, 2);
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(160, 230, 255));
    spr.drawCentreString("UNDERGROUND", 160, 0, 1);
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "x%d", lives);
    spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
    spr.drawString(buf, 294, 0, 2);
    if (msgT > 0) {
      spr.setTextColor(RGB(255, 240, 100));
      spr.drawCentreString(msgTxt, 160, 14, 1);
    }
  }
  if (y0 == 192) {
    int ox = 3;
    for (int i = 0; i < PWR_N; i++) if (hasP(i)) {
      drawPwrIcon(i, ox, 28, true);
      if (onP(i)) spr.drawRect(ox - 1, 27, 20, 20, TFT_WHITE);
      char b[4]; snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "%d", pwrCnt[i]);
      spr.setTextColor(TFT_BLACK); spr.drawString(b, ox + 13, 40, 1);
      spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); spr.drawString(b, ox + 12, 39, 1);
      ox += 20;
    }
  }
}

void tryWarp() {
  if (undergroundActive) return;
  if (warpLock > 0) { warpLock--; return; }
  if (dying || won || flyPhase) return;
  if (!levelBossCourse && endPipeX >= 0) {
    float cx=px+PW/2; bool nearPipe=cx>=endPipeX*TS-12&&cx<=(endPipeX+2)*TS+12;
    bool onPipe=onGround&&py+PH<=endPipeTop*TS+6&&py+PH>=endPipeTop*TS-8;
    if(nearPipe&&onPipe&&((E&BTN_DOWN)||landT>0||(H&BTN_DOWN))){
      // Chapter 2 has no sewers: the exit pipe goes straight to the next level.
      if (inChapter2) {
        won = true; winT = 0; score += 1500; sfxLevelClear();
        fxBurst(px + PW / 2, py + PH / 2, RGB(80, 255, 140), 14);
        fxFlash = 40; setMsg("NEXT!");
      } else {
        beginUnderground();
      }
      warpLock=30;return;
    }
  }
  int tx = (int)((px + PW / 2) / TS), ty = (int)((py + PH / 2) / TS);
  char c = tileAt(tx, ty);
  char below = tileAt(tx, ty + 1);
  int dx, dy;
  if ((c == 'D' || below == 'D') && (E & BTN_UP)) {
    int sx = (c == 'D') ? tx : tx, sy = (c == 'D') ? ty : ty + 1;
    if (findMate('D', sx, sy, dx, dy)) {
      px = dx * TS + 2; py = dy * TS + 2; unstick();
      warpLock = 20; fxFlash = 16; setMsg("WARP!");
    }
  }
  if ((c == 'L' || c == 'R' || below == 'L' || below == 'R') && (E & BTN_DOWN) && onGround) {
    int sx = tx, sy = (below == 'L' || below == 'R') ? ty + 1 : ty;
    if (findMate('L', sx, sy, dx, dy)) {
      px = dx * TS + 2; py = dy * TS - PH - 2;
      if (py < 0) py = 0;
      unstick(); warpLock = 20; fxFlash = 16; setMsg("PIPE!");
    }
  }
}

void collectStuff() {
  if (inChapter2 && !levelBossCourse && !won && !dying && px + PW > (LW - 3) * TS) {
    won = true; winT = 0; score += 1500; sfxLevelClear();
    fxFlash = 36; setMsg("NEXT!");
  }
  int x0 = (int)floorf(px / TS), x1 = (int)floorf((px + PW - 0.01f) / TS);
  int y0 = (int)floorf(py / TS), y1 = (int)floorf((py + PH - 0.01f) / TS);
  for (int ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++)
    for (int tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
      if (tx < 0 || tx >= LW || ty < 0 || ty >= MAXH) continue;
      char c = lvl[ty][tx];
      if (c == 'c') {
        lvl[ty][tx] = '.'; coins++; score += 100;
        sfxCoin();
        fxBurst(tx * TS + 8, ty * TS + 8, RGB(255, 220, 40), 8);
        fxSpark(tx * TS + 8, ty * TS + 8, RGB(255, 250, 180), 4);
        spawnScore(tx * TS + 8, ty * TS, 100);
        if (coins % 25 == 0 && lives < 9) { lives++; setMsg("1UP!"); }
      } else if ((c == 'F' || c == 'H') && !won && levelBossCourse) {
        won = true; winT = 0; score += (c == 'H') ? 2500 : 1000; sfxLevelClear();
        secretExit = (c == 'H');
        if (secretExit) { sv.secrets++; setMsg("SECRET EXIT!"); }
        fxBurst(tx * TS + 8, ty * TS + 8, RGB(80, 255, 120), 16); fxFlash = 50;
      } else if (c == '^') {
        if (py + PH > ty * TS + 8 && px + PW > tx * TS + 3 && px < tx * TS + 13) hurt(1);
      }
    }
}

void updateEnemies() {
  bool ghost = onP(PWR_INVIS);
  bool frozen = freezeT > 0;
  for (int i = 0; i < nEn; i++) {
    Enemy &e = en[i];
    if (!e.alive) { if (e.squish) e.squish--; continue; }
    if (e.cd) e.cd--;
    if (!e.awake) {
      if (e.x < camX + SCRW + 24) {
        e.awake = true;
        float s = 0.6f;
        if (e.kind == EK_FLY) s = 0.7f; else if (e.kind == EK_KOOPA) s = 0.75f;
        else if (e.kind == EK_FIRE) s = 0.85f; else if (e.kind == EK_SNOW) s = 0.4f;
        else if (e.kind == EK_ARMOR) s = 0.5f; else if (e.kind == EK_SHOOT) s = 0; else if (e.kind == EK_BOWSERLING) s = 0.9f;
        e.vx = -s;
      } else continue;
    }

    if (!frozen) {
      if (e.kind == EK_FLY) {
        e.x += e.vx;
        e.y += sinf((tAnim + i * 20) * 0.18f) * 0.7f;
        if (e.y > MAXH * TS - 40) e.y = MAXH * TS - 40;
        if (e.x < camX - 40) e.vx = fabsf(e.vx);
        if (e.x > camX + SCRW + 40) e.vx = -fabsf(e.vx);
      } else if (e.kind == EK_BOWSERLING) {
        // Chapter 2 Bowserlings: chase, jump over simple gaps, and shoot.
        float dx = px - e.x;
        float dir = (dx >= 0) ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
        e.vx += (dir * 1.15f - e.vx) * 0.10f;
        if (e.vx > 2.0f) e.vx = 2.0f;
        if (e.vx < -2.0f) e.vx = -2.0f;
        e.x += e.vx;
        if (boxHit(e.x, e.y, 16, 16)) { e.x -= e.vx; e.vx = -e.vx; }
        e.vy += 0.5f; if (e.vy > 7) e.vy = 7;
        e.y += e.vy;
        if (boxHit(e.x, e.y, 16, 16)) {
          e.y -= e.vy;
          if (e.vy > 0 && fabsf(px-e.x) < 115 && e.cd == 0) { e.vy = -6.3f; e.cd = 55; fxDust(e.x+8,e.y+16); }
          else e.vy = 0;
        }
        if (!dying && !won && fabsf(dx) < 210 && fabsf(py-e.y) < 80 && --e.shoot <= 0) {
          float d = (dx >= 0) ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
          spawnBullet(e.x+8+d*9,e.y+6,d*2.1f,(py<e.y)?-0.25f:0.0f,2);
          fxBurst(e.x+8+d*9,e.y+6,RGB(255,70,50),4); e.shoot=78;
        }
      } else if (e.kind == EK_RUNNER) {
        // Chase Mario only on roughly the same floor. Otherwise patrol.
        bool sameFloor = fabsf(py - e.y) < 28;
        float dir = sameFloor ? ((px > e.x) ? 1.0f : -1.0f) : ((e.vx >= 0) ? 1.0f : -1.0f);
        e.vx += (dir * (sameFloor ? 1.35f : 0.8f) - e.vx) * 0.10f;
        if (e.vx > 2.6f) e.vx = 2.6f;
        if (e.vx < -2.6f) e.vx = -2.6f;
        e.x += e.vx;
        if (boxHit(e.x, e.y, 14, 14)) { e.x -= e.vx; e.vx = -e.vx; }
        e.vy += 0.5f; if (e.vy > 7) e.vy = 7;
        e.y += e.vy;
        if (boxHit(e.x, e.y, 14, 14)) { e.y -= e.vy; e.vy = 0; }
      } else if (e.kind == EK_JUMPER) {
        // Patrol on its ledge; leap only if Mario is near and on a similar height.
        e.x += e.vx;
        if (boxHit(e.x, e.y, 14, 14)) { e.x -= e.vx; e.vx = -e.vx; }
        e.vy += 0.5f; if (e.vy > 7) e.vy = 7;
        e.y += e.vy;
        if (boxHit(e.x, e.y, 14, 14)) {
          e.y -= e.vy;
          if (e.vy > 0) {
            e.vy = 0;
            if (fabsf(px - e.x) < 130 && fabsf(py - e.y) < 36 && e.cd == 0) {
              e.vy = -7.0f; e.vx = (px > e.x) ? 1.25f : -1.25f; e.cd = 55;
              fxDust(e.x + 7, e.y + 14);
            }
          }
        }
      } else {
        e.x += e.vx;
        if (boxHit(e.x, e.y, 14, 14)) { e.x -= e.vx; e.vx = -e.vx; }
        e.vy += 0.5f; if (e.vy > 6) e.vy = 6;
        e.y += e.vy;
        if (boxHit(e.x, e.y, 14, 14)) {
          e.y -= e.vy;
          if (e.vy > 0 && (e.kind == EK_KOOPA || e.kind == EK_ARMOR || e.kind == EK_SNOW) && e.vx != 0) {
            int ax = (int)floorf((e.x + 7 + (e.vx > 0 ? 10 : -10)) / TS);
            int ay = (int)floorf((e.y + 16) / TS);
            if (!solidCh(tileAt(ax, ay))) e.vx = -e.vx;
          }
          e.vy = 0;
        }
      }
      if (e.kind == EK_SHOOT && !dying && !won) {
        float dx = px - e.x;
        if (fabsf(dx) < 190 && fabsf(py - e.y) < 70) {
          if (--e.shoot <= 0) {
            float d = dx > 0 ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
            spawnBullet(e.x + 7 + d * 8, e.y + 6, d * 1.7f, 0, (lvTheme == TH_WINTER) ? 1 : 0);
            fxBurst(e.x + 7 + d * 8, e.y + 6, RGB(255, 200, 80), 3);
            e.shoot = 115;
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if (e.y > MAXH * TS + 32) { e.alive = false; e.squish = 0; continue; }

    if (!dying && !won && !ghost && overlap(px, py, PW, PH, e.x, e.y, 14, 14)) {
      if (onP(PWR_HULK)) hitEnemy(i, 2, RGB(90, 230, 90));
      else if (onP(PWR_STAR)) hitEnemy(i, 3, RGB(255, 240, 80));
      else if (rkState >= 2) { /* rocket ride: harmless */ }
      else if (vy > 0 && (py + PH) - e.y < 10) {
        hitEnemy(i, 1, RGB(255, 150, 50));
        vy = (H & BTN_A) ? -7.0f : -4.8f; score += 100;
        py = e.y - PH - 0.5f;
        fxDust(px + PW / 2, py + PH);
      } else if (!frozen) hurt(1);
    }
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  MARIO SPRITE  (12 x 16 pixel art, scalable, mirrorable)
//  R red  D dark red  U blue  S skin  B brown  M mustache
//  Y button  K eye  W white  H hair
// ============================================================
static const char *const MS_TOP[13] = {
  "....RRRRR...",
  "...RRRRRRRR.",
  "...HHHSSKSW.",
  "..HSMSSSKSSS",
  "..HSHHSSSKSS",
  "..HHSSSSHHHH",
  "....SSMMSS..",
  "...RRURRUR..",
  ".WRRRURRURRW",
  ".DRRUUUUUURR",
  "..WWUYUUYUWW",
  "..SSUUUUUUSS",
  "...UUU.UUU.."
};
static const char *const MS_LEG[4][3] = {
  { "...UUU.UUU..", "..BBB...BBB.", ".BBBB...BBBB" },
  { "..UUU..UUU..", ".BBB....BBBB", "BBBB.....BB." },
  { "...UUUUUU...", "....BBBB....", "...BBBBBB..." },
  { "..UUU...UUU.", ".BBB.....BBB", "BBB.......BB" }
};
uint16_t mR, mU, mS, mB, mY, mK, mW;
static uint16_t palCol(char c) {
  switch (c) {
    case 'R': return mR;
    case 'D': return RGB(148, 18, 16);
    case 'U': return mU; case 'S': return mS; case 'B': return mB; case 'H': return mB;
    case 'M': return RGB(78, 36, 16);
    case 'Y': return mY; case 'K': return mK; case 'W': return mW;
  }
  return 0;
}
void setMarioPalette() {
  mR = RGB(224, 36, 28); mU = RGB(36, 70, 224); mS = RGB(255, 204, 150);
  mB = RGB(110, 58, 20); mY = RGB(255, 220, 40); mK = RGB(0, 0, 0); mW = RGB(255, 255, 255);
  switch (sv.skin) {
    case 1: mR = RGB(40, 90, 230); mU = RGB(30, 50, 160); mY = RGB(180, 220, 255); break;
    case 2: mR = RGB(255, 90, 170); mU = RGB(230, 60, 140); mY = RGB(255, 200, 230); break;
    case 3: mR = RGB(30, 30, 34); mU = RGB(20, 20, 24); mS = RGB(210, 180, 140); mY = RGB(180, 180, 190); break;
    case 4: mR = RGB(240, 180, 30); mU = RGB(200, 140, 20); mY = RGB(255, 240, 120); break;
    case 5: mR = RGB(80, 200, 230); mU = RGB(200, 240, 255); mS = RGB(230, 245, 255); mY = RGB(180, 255, 255); break;
  }
  if (onP(PWR_HULK)) {
    mR = RGB(50, 180, 60); mS = RGB(100, 215, 90); mU = RGB(130, 50, 170); mB = RGB(70, 30, 100);
    mW = RGB(40, 150, 50); mY = RGB(220, 200, 60);
  }
  if (onP(PWR_INVIS)) {
    bool f = (tAnim & 3) < 2;
    mR = f ? RGB(60, 230, 100) : RGB(40, 180, 80); mU = RGB(20, 140, 60); mS = RGB(170, 255, 190);
    mB = RGB(20, 100, 50); mW = mR; mY = RGB(190, 255, 200);
  }
  if (onP(PWR_STAR)) {
    static const uint16_t rb[6] = { RGB(255,80,80), RGB(255,180,40), RGB(255,255,60), RGB(80,255,80), RGB(80,160,255), RGB(200,80,255) };
    int ph = (tAnim / 2) % 6;
    mR = rb[ph]; mU = rb[(ph + 3) % 6];
  }
  if (onP(PWR_HEAL)) { mR = RGB(90, 255, 130); mU = RGB(160, 255, 190); }
  if (onP(PWR_SHOCK)) { mR = RGB(255, 205, 55); mU = RGB(255, 245, 120); }
  if (onP(PWR_FIRE)) { mR = RGB(240, 70, 25); mU = RGB(255, 175, 45); }
  if (onP(PWR_FREEZE)) { mR = RGB(70, 170, 235); mU = RGB(180, 240, 255); }
  if (onP(PWR_SPRING)) { mR = RGB(55, 210, 145); mU = RGB(150, 255, 220); }
}

template <typename G>
void drawMarioG(G &g, int x, int y, int dw, int dh, int frame, bool flip, int clipH) {
  if (y + dh <= 0 || y >= clipH) return;
  const char *all[16];
  for (int r = 0; r < 13; r++) all[r] = MS_TOP[r];
  all[13] = MS_LEG[frame][0]; all[14] = MS_LEG[frame][1]; all[15] = MS_LEG[frame][2];
  for (int r = 0; r < 16; r++) {
    int y0 = y + r * dh / 16, y1 = y + (r + 1) * dh / 16, hh = y1 - y0;
    if (hh <= 0 || y1 <= 0 || y0 >= clipH) continue;
    int c = 0;
    while (c < 12) {
      char ch = all[r][flip ? 11 - c : c];
      int c2 = c + 1;
      while (c2 < 12 && all[r][flip ? 11 - c2 : c2] == ch) c2++;
      if (ch != '.') {
        int xa = x + c * dw / 12, xb = x + c2 * dw / 12;
        g.fillRect(xa, y0, xb - xa, hh, palCol(ch));
      }
      c = c2;
    }
  }
}

void cloneReset() {
  for (auto &c : cm) { c.x=0; c.y=0; c.vx=0; c.vy=0; c.face=1; c.atkT=0; c.jumpT=0; c.onGround=false; c.alive=false; c.color=0; }
}

void cloneSpawnAll() {
  const int ox[4] = {-30, -12, 12, 30};
  for (int i=0;i<4;i++) {
    CloneMario &c=cm[i];
    c.x=px+ox[i]; c.y=py; c.vx=0; c.vy=-1.5f; c.face=(i<2)?-1:1;
    c.atkT=5+i*3; c.jumpT=0; c.onGround=false; c.alive=true; c.color=i;
    if (c.x < 4) c.x=4;
    if (!undergroundActive) {
      int guard=0;
      while (boxHit(c.x,c.y,12,15) && guard++<30) c.y-=1;
    }
  }
}

static void clonePalette(uint8_t color) {
  uint8_t k = color & 3;
  mR=CLONE_COL[k][0]; mU=CLONE_COL[k][1]; mS=CLONE_COL[k][2];
  mB=RGB(110,58,20); mY=CLONE_COL[k][3]; mK=RGB(0,0,0); mW=RGB(255,255,255);
}

static bool cloneGroundAhead(CloneMario &c, float dir) {
  int tx=(int)floorf((c.x+6+dir*18)/TS);
  int ty=(int)floorf((c.y+17)/TS);
  if (tx<0 || tx>=LW) return true;
  if (solidCh(tileAt(tx,ty))) return true;
  return platHit(c.x+dir*18, c.y+15, 10, 3);
}

static void cloneMoveX(CloneMario &c, float dx) {
  int n=(int)ceilf(fabsf(dx)*2); if(!n) return;
  float st=dx/n;
  for(int k=0;k<n;k++){ c.x+=st; if(boxHit(c.x,c.y,12,15)){ c.x-=st; c.vx=0; break; } }
}
static void cloneMoveY(CloneMario &c, float dy) {
  int n=(int)ceilf(fabsf(dy)*2); if(!n) return;
  float st=dy/n;
  for(int k=0;k<n;k++){ c.y+=st; if(boxHit(c.x,c.y,12,15)){ c.y-=st; c.vy=0; if(st>0)c.onGround=true; break; } }
}

void cloneAttackSurface(CloneMario &c) {
  if (c.atkT>0) return;
  int best=-1; float bd=1e9f;
  for(int i=0;i<nEn;i++) if(en[i].alive){
    float dx=en[i].x+7-c.x, dy=en[i].y+7-c.y;
    if(fabsf(dx)>190 || fabsf(dy)>85) continue;
    float d=dx*dx+dy*dy; if(d<bd){bd=d;best=i;}
  }
  if(best>=0 && bd<34.0f*34.0f){
    hitEnemy(best,99,CLONE_COL[c.color][0]);
    c.atkT=14;
    fxRing(c.x+6,c.y+7,CLONE_COL[c.color][0],10,2.0f);
    fxSpark(c.x+6,c.y+7,CLONE_COL[c.color][3],4);
  }
}

void updateClones() {
  if (!onP(PWR_CLONE) || dying || won) return;
  for(int i=0;i<4;i++) {
    CloneMario &c=cm[i];
    if(!c.alive) continue;
    if(c.atkT>0)c.atkT--;
    if(c.jumpT>0)c.jumpT--;

    if(undergroundActive) {
      int best=-1; float bd=1e9f;
      for(int j=0;j<nUGE;j++) if(uge[j].alive){
        float dx=uge[j].x+8-c.x,dy=uge[j].y+8-c.y;
        if(fabsf(dx)>220||fabsf(dy)>90)continue;
        float d=dx*dx+dy*dy;if(d<bd){bd=d;best=j;}
      }
      float targetX=px+(-30+i*20);
      if(best>=0) targetX=uge[best].x;
      else if(fabsf(c.x-targetX)<10) targetX=px+(-30+i*20);
      float dir=(targetX>c.x)?1.0f:-1.0f;
      c.face=(dir>0)?1:-1; c.vx += (dir*2.7f-c.vx)*0.18f;
      if(c.vx>3.1f)c.vx=3.1f;if(c.vx<-3.1f)c.vx=-3.1f;
      c.x+=c.vx;
      c.y=UG_FLOOR-18; c.vy=0; c.onGround=true;
      if(best>=0 && bd<34.0f*34.0f && c.atkT==0){
        uge[best].hp=0; uge[best].alive=false; c.atkT=14; score+=250;
        fxRing(c.x+8,c.y+8,CLONE_COL[c.color][0],10,2.0f); fxBurst(c.x+8,c.y+8,CLONE_COL[c.color][3],6); sfxEnemyDefeat();
      }
      continue;
    }

    int best=-1; float bd=1e9f;
    for(int j=0;j<nEn;j++) if(en[j].alive){
      float dx=en[j].x+7-c.x,dy=en[j].y+7-c.y;
      if(fabsf(dx)>240||fabsf(dy)>100)continue;
      float d=dx*dx+dy*dy;if(d<bd){bd=d;best=j;}
    }
    float targetX=px + (-30+i*20);
    if(best>=0) targetX=en[best].x;
    float dir=(targetX>c.x)?1.0f:-1.0f;
    c.face=(dir>0)?1:-1;
    bool needJump = c.onGround && (!cloneGroundAhead(c,dir) || (best>=0 && en[best].y+10 < c.y && fabsf(en[best].x-c.x)<80));
    if(needJump && c.jumpT==0){ c.vy=-7.5f; c.onGround=false; c.jumpT=18; fxDust(c.x+6,c.y+15); }
    c.vx += (dir*2.55f-c.vx)*0.16f;
    if(c.vx>3.0f)c.vx=3.0f;if(c.vx<-3.0f)c.vx=-3.0f;
    cloneMoveX(c,c.vx);
    c.vy+=0.5f;if(c.vy>8)c.vy=8;
    c.onGround=false; cloneMoveY(c,c.vy);
    if(c.y>MAXH*TS+20 || c.y>py+48){
      c.x=px+(-30+i*20); c.y=py-18; c.vx=0; c.vy=-4.0f; c.onGround=false;
    }
    cloneAttackSurface(c);
  }
}

void drawCloneMarios(int y0, int cam) {
  if(!onP(PWR_CLONE)) return;
  for(int i=0;i<4;i++){
    CloneMario &c=cm[i]; if(!c.alive)continue;
    int x=(int)c.x-cam, y=(int)c.y-y0;
    if(x<-24||x>SCRW+24||y<-24||y>BANDH+24)continue;
    clonePalette(c.color);
    int frame=c.onGround?(fabsf(c.vx)<0.25f?0:1+(((int)(tAnim/5.0f))&1)):3;
    int bob=c.onGround?(int)(sinf(tAnim*0.18f+i)*0.6f):0;
    int w=12,h=16;
    spr.fillEllipse(x+6,y+16,5,2,RGB(20,20,28));
    drawMarioG(spr,x,y+bob,w,h,frame,c.face<0,BANDH);
    if(c.atkT==0 && ((tAnim+i*3)&3)==0){
      int ax=x+6+c.face*10, ay=y+7; spr.drawFastHLine(x+6,ay,c.face*8,CLONE_COL[c.color][3]);
    }
    if((tAnim+i*2)%7==0) fxSpark(c.x+6,c.y+7,CLONE_COL[c.color][3],1);
  }
}


void setFriendPalette() {
  // Literally Mario's sprite, green suit / cap, darker green overalls.
  mR = RGB(36, 196, 62);
  mU = RGB(18, 118, 46);
  mS = RGB(255, 208, 154);
  mB = RGB(110, 58, 20);
  mY = RGB(255, 230, 70);
  mK = RGB(0, 0, 0);
  mW = RGB(255, 255, 255);
}

void friendReset() {
  buddy.alive = false; buddy.vx = buddy.vy = 0; buddy.onGround = false;
  buddy.atkT = buddy.jumpT = buddy.shootT = 0; buddy.face = 1;
  for (auto &b : fshot) b.alive = false;
}

void friendSpawn() {
  if (!friendCompanion) { friendReset(); return; }
  buddy.alive = true;
  buddy.x = px - 28; if (buddy.x < 4) buddy.x = px + 22;
  buddy.y = py;
  buddy.vx = 0; buddy.vy = -1.2f;
  buddy.face = face;
  buddy.atkT = 0; buddy.jumpT = 8; buddy.shootT = 12;
  buddy.onGround = false;
  for (auto &b : fshot) b.alive = false;
  int guard = 0;
  while (!undergroundActive && boxHit(buddy.x, buddy.y, 12, 15) && guard++ < 30) buddy.y -= 1;
}

static bool friendGroundAhead(float dir) {
  int tx = (int)floorf((buddy.x + 6 + dir * 18) / TS);
  int ty = (int)floorf((buddy.y + 17) / TS);
  if (tx < 0 || tx >= LW) return true;
  if (solidCh(tileAt(tx, ty))) return true;
  return platHit(buddy.x + dir * 18, buddy.y + 15, 10, 3);
}

static void friendMoveX(float dx) {
  int n = (int)ceilf(fabsf(dx) * 2); if (!n) return;
  float st = dx / n;
  for (int k = 0; k < n; k++) {
    buddy.x += st;
    if (boxHit(buddy.x, buddy.y, 12, 15)) { buddy.x -= st; buddy.vx = 0; break; }
  }
}
static void friendMoveY(float dy) {
  int n = (int)ceilf(fabsf(dy) * 2); if (!n) return;
  float st = dy / n;
  for (int k = 0; k < n; k++) {
    buddy.y += st;
    if (boxHit(buddy.x, buddy.y, 12, 15)) {
      buddy.y -= st; buddy.vy = 0;
      if (st > 0) buddy.onGround = true;
      break;
    }
  }
}

static void friendFireAt(float tx, float ty) {
  if (buddy.shootT > 0) return;
  int slot = -1;
  for (int i = 0; i < MAXFSHOT; i++) if (!fshot[i].alive) { slot = i; break; }
  if (slot < 0) return;
  float dx = tx - (buddy.x + 6);
  float dy = ty - (buddy.y + 7);
  float len = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy); if (len < 1) len = 1;
  FShot &b = fshot[slot];
  b.alive = true; b.life = 28;
  b.x = buddy.x + 6 + buddy.face * 10;
  b.y = buddy.y + 8;
  b.vx = (dx / len) * 4.2f;
  b.vy = (dy / len) * 3.4f;
  if (fabsf(b.vy) > 2.2f) b.vy = (b.vy > 0) ? 2.2f : -2.2f;
  buddy.shootT = 18;
  buddy.face = (dx >= 0) ? 1 : -1;
  fxSpark(b.x, b.y, RGB(255, 230, 80), 3);
  sfxAttack();
}

void updateFriendShots() {
  for (auto &b : fshot) if (b.alive) {
    b.x += b.vx; b.y += b.vy;
    if (b.life) b.life--;
    if (!b.life || b.x < camF - 20 || b.x > camF + SCRW + 20 || boxHit(b.x, b.y, 4, 4)) {
      b.alive = false; continue;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < nEn; i++) if (en[i].alive && overlap(b.x - 2, b.y - 2, 6, 6, en[i].x, en[i].y, 14, 14)) {
      hitEnemy(i, 1, RGB(80, 230, 90));
      fxBurst(b.x, b.y, RGB(255, 220, 80), 5);
      b.alive = false;
      break;
    }
    if (!b.alive) continue;
    if (boss.active && overlap(b.x - 2, b.y - 2, 6, 6, boss.x, boss.y, BOSS_W, BOSS_H)) {
      hurtBoss(1, true); b.alive = false; fxBurst(b.x, b.y, RGB(255, 200, 60), 6);
    }
    if (!b.alive) continue;
    for (int i = 0; i < nUGE; i++) if (uge[i].alive && overlap(b.x - 2, b.y - 2, 6, 6, uge[i].x, uge[i].y, 14, 14)) {
      uge[i].hp -= 1;
      if (uge[i].hp <= 0) { uge[i].alive = false; score += 250; sfxEnemyDefeat(); }
      fxBurst(b.x, b.y, RGB(80, 230, 90), 5);
      b.alive = false;
      break;
    }
  }
}

void updateFriend() {
  if (!friendCompanion) { if (buddy.alive) friendReset(); return; }
  if (!buddy.alive) friendSpawn();
  if (buddy.atkT > 0) buddy.atkT--;
  if (buddy.jumpT > 0) buddy.jumpT--;
  if (buddy.shootT > 0) buddy.shootT--;

  if (undergroundActive) {
    float targetX = px - 22;
    int best = -1; float bd = 1e9f;
    for (int i = 0; i < nUGE; i++) if (uge[i].alive) {
      float d = fabsf(uge[i].x - buddy.x);
      if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = i; }
    }
    if (best >= 0) targetX = uge[best].x;
    float dir = (targetX > buddy.x) ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
    buddy.face = (dir > 0) ? 1 : -1;
    buddy.vx += (dir * 2.4f - buddy.vx) * 0.18f;
    buddy.x += buddy.vx;
    buddy.y = UG_FLOOR - 18; buddy.vy = 0; buddy.onGround = true;
    if (best >= 0 && bd < 90 && buddy.shootT == 0) friendFireAt(uge[best].x, uge[best].y);
    updateFriendShots();
    return;
  }

  int best = -1; float bd = 1e9f;
  for (int j = 0; j < nEn; j++) if (en[j].alive) {
    float dx = en[j].x + 7 - buddy.x, dy = en[j].y + 7 - buddy.y;
    if (fabsf(dx) > 210 || fabsf(dy) > 90) continue;
    float d = dx * dx + dy * dy;
    if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = j; }
  }
  float followX = px - 26.0f * ((face >= 0) ? 1.0f : -1.0f);
  if (fabsf(followX - px) < 16) followX = px - 24;
  float targetX = followX;
  if (best >= 0 && bd < 150.0f * 150.0f) targetX = en[best].x;
  float dir = (targetX > buddy.x) ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
  if (fabsf(targetX - buddy.x) > 4) buddy.face = (dir > 0) ? 1 : -1;
  else buddy.face = face;

  bool needJump = buddy.onGround && (
      !friendGroundAhead(dir) ||
      (best >= 0 && en[best].y + 8 < buddy.y && fabsf(en[best].x - buddy.x) < 90) ||
      (py + 6 < buddy.y && fabsf(px - buddy.x) < 70)
  );
  if (needJump && buddy.jumpT == 0) {
    buddy.vy = -7.6f; buddy.onGround = false; buddy.jumpT = 16;
    fxDust(buddy.x + 6, buddy.y + 15);
  }
  float spd = (best >= 0 && bd < 120 * 120) ? 2.7f : 2.35f;
  buddy.vx += (dir * spd - buddy.vx) * 0.18f;
  if (buddy.vx > 3.1f) buddy.vx = 3.1f;
  if (buddy.vx < -3.1f) buddy.vx = -3.1f;
  if (fabsf(targetX - buddy.x) < 3.5f) buddy.vx *= 0.55f;
  friendMoveX(buddy.vx);
  buddy.vy += 0.5f; if (buddy.vy > 8) buddy.vy = 8;
  buddy.onGround = false; friendMoveY(buddy.vy);

  if (buddy.y > MAXH * TS + 20 || buddy.y > py + 56 || buddy.x < px - 220 || buddy.x > px + 220) {
    buddy.x = px - 24; buddy.y = py - 16; buddy.vx = 0; buddy.vy = -4.0f; buddy.onGround = false;
  }

  if (best >= 0 && buddy.shootT == 0 && bd < 170.0f * 170.0f) {
    friendFireAt(en[best].x + 7, en[best].y + 7);
  } else if (boss.active && buddy.shootT == 0 && fabsf(boss.x - buddy.x) < 180) {
    friendFireAt(boss.x + 16, boss.y + 10);
  } else if (bw.st && bw.st != BW_OFF && buddy.shootT == 0 && fabsf(bw.x - buddy.x) < 190) {
    friendFireAt(bw.x + 16, bw.y + 12);
  }
  updateFriendShots();
}

void drawFriendWorld(int y0, int cam) {
  if (!friendCompanion || !buddy.alive) return;
  int x = (int)buddy.x - cam, y = (int)buddy.y - y0;
  if (x < -28 || x > SCRW + 28 || y < -28 || y > BANDH + 28) {
    // still draw shots
  } else {
    setFriendPalette();
    int frame = buddy.onGround ? (fabsf(buddy.vx) < 0.28f ? 0 : 1 + (((int)(tAnim / 5.0f)) & 1)) : 3;
    int bob = buddy.onGround ? (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.18f) * 0.6f) : 0;
    int w = 12, h = 16;
    spr.fillEllipse(x + 6, y + 16, 5, 2, RGB(20, 20, 28));
    drawMarioG(spr, x, y + bob, w, h, frame, buddy.face < 0, BANDH);
    // pistol
    int gx = x + 6 + buddy.face * 8;
    int gy = y + 9 + bob;
    spr.fillRoundRect(gx - (buddy.face < 0 ? 6 : 0), gy, 7, 3, 1, RGB(50, 52, 62));
    spr.fillRect(gx + (buddy.face < 0 ? -7 : 5), gy + 1, 4, 2, RGB(28, 30, 38));
    if (buddy.shootT > 12) {
      spr.fillCircle(gx + buddy.face * 8, gy + 1, 3, RGB(255, 230, 90));
      spr.fillCircle(gx + buddy.face * 8, gy + 1, 1, TFT_WHITE);
    }
  }
  for (auto &b : fshot) if (b.alive) {
    int sx = (int)b.x - cam, sy = (int)b.y - y0;
    if (sy < -6 || sy > BANDH + 6) continue;
    spr.fillCircle(sx, sy, 3, RGB(255, 220, 70));
    spr.fillCircle(sx, sy, 1, TFT_WHITE);
    spr.drawFastHLine(sx - (int)(b.vx * 1.6f), sy, 6, RGB(180, 255, 140));
  }
}

static inline bool hasP(int i) { return pwrCnt[i] > 0; }
static inline bool onP(int i)  { return pwrActive[i]; }

int marioFrame() {
  if (!onGround) return 3;
  if (fabsf(vx) < 0.25f) return 0;
  return 1 + (((int)(animT / 5.0f)) & 1);
}


void drawHulkbuster(int x, int y) {
  int w = HB_W + 8, h = HB_H + 8;
  int bx = x - (w - PW) / 2;
  int by = y + PH - h;
  float speed = fabsf(vx);
  int phase = (int)(animT * 0.45f) & 7;
  int stride = onGround ? ((phase < 4) ? phase : 7 - phase) : 1;
  int bob = onGround ? ((speed > 0.4f) ? (stride & 1) : 0) : (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.14f) * 2.0f);
  int armSwing = onGround ? ((stride & 1) ? 2 : -2) : -1;
  if (flyPhase == 3) armSwing = (tAnim & 8) ? 4 : -2;
  by += bob;
  uint16_t dark=RGB(38,45,60), mid=RGB(125,138,160), hi=RGB(220,228,238);
  uint16_t red=RGB(160,42,38), gold=RGB(255,175,45), glow=RGB(90,225,255);
  if (onGround && flyPhase != 3) spr.fillEllipse(bx+w/2-14,y+PH-1,28,3,RGB(28,28,40));
  if (speed > 2.2f && onGround) { spr.drawFastHLine(bx-face*8,by+48,8,RGB(100,180,220)); if ((tAnim&2)==0) fxTrail(bx-face*10,y+PH-6,RGB(110,210,255)); }
  int l1=stride*2,l2=(3-stride)*2;
  spr.fillRoundRect(bx+7,by+40+l1,12,23-l1,3,dark); spr.fillRoundRect(bx+w-19,by+40+l2,12,23-l2,3,dark);
  spr.fillRect(bx+9,by+47+l1/2,8,9,mid); spr.fillRect(bx+w-17,by+47+l2/2,8,9,mid);
  spr.fillRoundRect(bx+4,by+58+l1,17,7,2,dark); spr.fillRoundRect(bx+w-21,by+58+l2,17,7,2,dark);
  spr.fillRoundRect(bx+8,by+17,w-16,32,7,dark); spr.fillRoundRect(bx+11,by+20,w-22,25,5,mid);
  spr.fillRect(bx+15,by+25,w-30,14,red); spr.fillCircle(bx+w/2,by+32,5+((tAnim>>2)&1),glow); spr.drawRect(bx+w/2-7,by+25,14,14,hi);
  spr.fillRoundRect(bx+1,by+17+armSwing,13,25,4,mid); spr.fillRoundRect(bx+w-14,by+17-armSwing,13,25,4,mid);
  spr.fillRect(bx+2,by+28+armSwing,11,5,red); spr.fillRect(bx+w-13,by+28-armSwing,11,5,red);
  spr.fillCircle(bx+7,by+44+armSwing,4,gold); spr.fillCircle(bx+w-7,by+44-armSwing,4,gold);
  if ((tAnim&3)==0) { spr.fillCircle(bx+7,by+44+armSwing,2,glow); spr.fillCircle(bx+w-7,by+44-armSwing,2,glow); }
  spr.fillRoundRect(bx+13,by+2,w-26,20,7,dark); spr.fillRoundRect(bx+16,by+5,w-32,14,4,mid);
  spr.fillRect(bx+19,by+10,w-38,4,glow); spr.fillRect(bx+15,by+15,w-30,4,red); spr.drawFastHLine(bx+11,by+21,w-22,hi); spr.drawFastHLine(bx+15,by+45,w-30,hi);
  if (actionT > 0 && (tAnim&1)==0) { int rr=4+(actionT%5); spr.drawCircle(bx+w/2+face*24,by+32,rr,RGB(255,220,90)); spr.drawFastHLine(bx+w/2+face*10,by+32,face*18,glow); }
  if (flyPhase == 3) { int flame=7+((tAnim/2)&3); spr.fillTriangle(bx+8,by+h-2,bx+15,by+h-2,bx+11,by+h+flame,glow); spr.fillTriangle(bx+w-15,by+h-2,bx+w-8,by+h-2,bx+w-11,by+h+flame,gold); fxTrail(bx+11,by+h+2,glow); fxTrail(bx+w-11,by+h+2,gold); }
}

void drawPlayer(int x, int y) {
  bool hitGlow = (blinkT > 0);
  if (onP(PWR_FLY) && flyPhase >= 3) {
    drawHulkbuster(x, y);
    return;
  }
  if (onP(PWR_FLY) && flyPhase == 1) {
    setMarioPalette();
    int cx=x+PW/2, cy=y+PH/2;
    drawMarioG(spr,cx-10,cy-16,20,32,3,face<0,BANDH);
    int d=max(4,30-flyAnim);
    int ox[4]={-1,1,-1,1}, oy[4]={-1,-1,1,1};
    for(int k=0;k<4;k++){int ax=cx+ox[k]*d-4,ay=cy+oy[k]*d-4;spr.fillRoundRect(ax,ay,8,8,2,(k&1)?RGB(230,175,40):RGB(210,30,30));spr.drawRect(ax,ay,8,8,RGB(235,240,250));}
    spr.drawCircle(cx,cy,d/2+7,RGB(255,225,110));
    if((flyAnim&2)==0){fxSpark(cx-d,cy,RGB(255,220,100),2);fxSpark(cx+d,cy,RGB(120,220,255),2);}
    return;
  }
  setMarioPalette();
  bool hulk = onP(PWR_HULK);

  // Smooth squash/stretch: feet stay anchored while the body reacts to speed.
  float spd = fabsf(vx);
  float pulse = sinf(tAnim * 0.18f);
  int stretch = 0, squash = 0, bob = 0;
  if (!onGround) {
    // Rising = stretched, falling = slightly compressed.
    if (vy < -2.0f) stretch = 2;
    else if (vy > 2.0f) squash = 1;
  } else if (landT > 0) {
    squash = (landT > 5) ? 2 : 1;
  } else if (spd > 0.25f) {
    bob = (int)(sinf(animT * 0.75f) * (spd > 2.5f ? 1.5f : 1.0f));
    squash = ((int)(animT / 3.0f) & 1) ? 1 : 0;
  } else {
    // Tiny breathing animation while idle.
    bob = (int)(pulse * 0.45f);
  }

  int baseW = 16;
  int baseH = hulk ? 22 : 20;
  int dw = baseW + squash - stretch;
  int dh = baseH + stretch - squash;
  if (dw < 9) dw = 9;
  if (dh < 13) dh = 13;
  int dx = x - (dw - PW) / 2 - 2;
  int dy = y + PH - dh + bob;

  // Soft contact shadow makes jumps and landings read much better.
  if (onGround && !dying) {
    int sw = 5 + (int)(spd * 2.0f);
    if (sw > 10) sw = 10;
    spr.fillEllipse(dx + dw / 2 - sw / 2, y + PH - 1, sw, 2,
                    RGB(35, 35, 50));
  }


  int frame = (onP(PWR_FLY) && flyPhase == 3) ? 3 : marioFrame();
  if (rollT > 0) {
    frame = 3;
    // Fast rolling body with a pulsing squash.
    int q = (tAnim & 2) ? 2 : 0;
    dw += q; dh -= q; dy += q / 2;
  } else if (flipT > 0) {
    frame = 3;
    // Simulated somersault: alternate compact/stretch poses.
    int phase = (18 - flipT) / 3;
    if (phase & 1) { int q = 3; dw += q; dh -= q; dy += 1; }
  }
  // Ability-specific Mario pose/animation overlays. Everything is drawn in the same
  // sprite buffer, so there is no erase/redraw blink when an ability starts.
  int abilityPulse = (pwrAnimT > 0) ? pwrAnimT : 0;
  if (pwrAnimId >= 0 && abilityPulse > 0) {
    float q = 1.0f - (float)abilityPulse / 18.0f;
    int lift = (int)(sinf(q * 3.14159f) * 3.0f);
    dy -= lift;
  }
  drawMarioG(spr, dx, dy - 1, dw, dh, frame, face < 0, BANDH);

  // Activation poses are intentionally compact so Mario remains recognizable.
  int ax0 = dx + dw/2, ay0 = dy + dh/2;
  if (pwrAnimId == PWR_HEAL && abilityPulse > 0) {
    int r = 7 + (18 - abilityPulse) / 2;
    spr.drawCircle(ax0, ay0, r, RGB(100,255,150));
    spr.fillRect(ax0-1, ay0-5, 3, 10, RGB(210,255,220));
    spr.fillRect(ax0-5, ay0-1, 10, 3, RGB(210,255,220));
  } else if (pwrAnimId == PWR_SHOCK && abilityPulse > 0) {
    int r = 5 + (18 - abilityPulse);
    spr.drawCircle(ax0, ay0, r, RGB(255,235,80));
    spr.drawLine(ax0-r, ay0, ax0-3, ay0-3, RGB(255,255,180));
    spr.drawLine(ax0+r, ay0, ax0+3, ay0+3, RGB(255,255,180));
  } else if (pwrAnimId == PWR_ROCKET && abilityPulse > 0) {
    int flame = 3 + ((18 - abilityPulse) & 3);
    spr.fillTriangle(dx+dw/2-4, dy+dh, dx+dw/2+4, dy+dh, dx+dw/2, dy+dh+flame, RGB(255,150,30));
    spr.fillRect(dx+dw/2-2, dy+dh+1, 4, flame/2+1, RGB(255,245,120));
  } else if (pwrAnimId == PWR_INVIS && abilityPulse > 0) {
    int r = 8 + (18 - abilityPulse) / 2;
    spr.drawRoundRect(dx-2, dy-2, dw+4, dh+4, 4, RGB(90,255,150));
  } else if (pwrAnimId == PWR_HULK && abilityPulse > 0) {
    int r = 10 + (int)(sinf((18-abilityPulse)*0.5f)*3.0f);
    spr.drawCircle(ax0, ay0, r, RGB(100,255,110));
    spr.drawCircle(ax0, ay0, r-3, RGB(190,255,130));
  } else if (pwrAnimId == PWR_STAR && abilityPulse > 0) {
    for (int k=0;k<4;k++) {
      float a = (18-abilityPulse)*0.35f + k*1.57f;
      int rr = 10 + (18-abilityPulse)/2;
      spr.fillRect(ax0+(int)cosf(a)*rr, ay0+(int)sinf(a)*rr, 2, 2, RGB(255,255,100));
    }
  } else if (pwrAnimId == PWR_FIRE && abilityPulse > 0) {
    int fh = 6 + ((18-abilityPulse)&3);
    spr.fillTriangle(ax0-5, dy+dh+2, ax0, dy+dh-fh, ax0+5, dy+dh+2, RGB(255,120,20));
    spr.fillTriangle(ax0-2, dy+dh+1, ax0, dy+dh-fh+3, ax0+2, dy+dh+1, RGB(255,240,100));
  } else if (pwrAnimId == PWR_FREEZE && abilityPulse > 0) {
    int r = 8 + (18-abilityPulse)/2;
    spr.drawCircle(ax0, ay0, r, RGB(180,240,255));
    spr.drawLine(ax0-r,ay0,ax0+r,ay0,RGB(225,255,255));
    spr.drawLine(ax0,ay0-r,ax0,ay0+r,RGB(225,255,255));
  } else if (pwrAnimId == PWR_SPRING && abilityPulse > 0) {
    int squash = 2 + (abilityPulse / 6);
    for (int k=0;k<3;k++) spr.drawFastHLine(ax0-5, y+PH-2-k*squash, 10, RGB(120,255,170));
  } else if (pwrAnimId == PWR_CLONE && abilityPulse > 0) {
    int rr=8+(18-abilityPulse);
    for(int k=0;k<4;k++){
      float a=(18-abilityPulse)*0.18f+k*1.5708f;
      int sx=ax0+(int)(cosf(a)*rr), sy=ay0+(int)(sinf(a)*rr*0.7f);
      clonePalette(k);
      spr.drawCircle(sx,sy,5,CLONE_COL[k][0]);
      spr.fillRect(sx-2,sy-2,5,5,CLONE_COL[k][1]);
    }
  }
  if (hitGlow) {
    uint16_t hc = (tAnim & 1) ? RGB(255, 110, 90) : RGB(255, 245, 210);
    spr.drawRoundRect(dx - 1, dy - 1, dw + 2, dh + 2, 3, hc);
  }
  if (rollT > 0 || flipT > 0) {
    int rr = 7 + ((int)tAnim & 2);
    spr.drawCircle(dx + dw / 2, dy + dh / 2, rr, RGB(170, 220, 255));
    if ((tAnim & 1) == 0) fxTrail(dx - face * 6, y + PH / 2, RGB(180, 220, 255));
  }

  // Speed streaks and power aura are deliberately subtle so gameplay stays readable.
  if (onGround && spd > 2.5f && !hulk) {
    int trailX = dx - face * (4 + (int)(spd * 1.2f));
    spr.drawFastHLine(trailX, dy + dh - 5, 3, RGB(210, 230, 255));
    if ((tAnim & 3) == 0) fxTrail(dx - face * 4, y + PH - 3, RGB(210, 230, 255));
  }
  if (onP(PWR_STAR)) {
    int n = 2 + ((int)tAnim & 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      float a = tAnim * 0.12f + i * 2.1f;
      int rr = 8 + (i * 3);
      int sx = dx + dw / 2 + (int)(cosf(a) * rr);
      int sy = dy + dh / 2 + (int)(sinf(a) * rr * 0.65f);
      spr.fillRect(sx, sy, 2, 2, TFT_WHITE);
    }
  }
  if (landT > 0) {
    spr.drawFastHLine(dx - 2, y + PH + 1, dw + 4, RGB(235, 245, 255));
  }

  // Distinct, continuously animated visual for every non-Hulkbuster ability.
  int ax = dx + dw/2, ay = dy + dh/2;
  if (onP(PWR_HEAL)) {
    spr.drawCircle(ax, ay, 12 + ((tAnim>>2)&2), RGB(80,255,130));
    spr.fillCircle(ax-4, ay-1, 2, RGB(170,255,190)); spr.fillCircle(ax+4, ay-1, 2, RGB(170,255,190));
  }
  if (onP(PWR_SHOCK)) {
    int r = 7 + (int)(sinf(tAnim*0.25f)*2); spr.drawCircle(ax,ay,r,RGB(255,230,80));
    spr.drawLine(ax-r,ay,ax-3,ay-3,RGB(255,255,190)); spr.drawLine(ax+r,ay,ax+3,ay+3,RGB(255,255,190));
  }
  if (onP(PWR_INVIS)) {
    spr.drawRoundRect(dx-2,dy-2,dw+4,dh+4,4,RGB(80,255,140));
    if ((tAnim&3)==0) fxTrail(dx-face*5,ay,RGB(80,255,140));
  }
  if (onP(PWR_FIRE)) {
    int f = 2 + ((tAnim/3)&2); spr.fillTriangle(ax-5,dy+dh+2,ax,dy+dh-f-5,ax+5,dy+dh+2,RGB(255,130,20));
    spr.fillTriangle(ax-2,dy+dh+1,ax,dy+dh-f,ax+2,dy+dh+1,RGB(255,235,100));
  }
  if (onP(PWR_FREEZE)) {
    int r=12+((tAnim>>2)&2); spr.drawCircle(ax,ay,r,RGB(160,235,255));
    spr.drawLine(ax-r,ay,ax+r,ay,RGB(220,250,255)); spr.drawLine(ax,ay-r,ax,ay+r,RGB(220,250,255));
  }
  if (onP(PWR_SPRING)) {
    for(int k=0;k<3;k++){int yy=y+PH-3-k*3; spr.drawFastHLine(ax-5,yy,10,RGB(120,255,160));}
  }
  if (onP(PWR_HULK)) {
    int r=14+((int)sinf(tAnim*0.18f)*2); spr.drawCircle(ax,ay,r,RGB(90,255,110));
    spr.drawCircle(ax,ay,r-3,RGB(160,255,130));
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  ENEMY ART
// ============================================================
static void drawHpBar(int x, int y, int w, int hpNow, int hpMax, uint16_t fill) {
  if (hpMax < 1) hpMax = 1;
  if (hpNow < 0) hpNow = 0;
  if (hpNow > hpMax) hpNow = hpMax;
  if (w < 10) w = 10;
  spr.fillRect(x - 1, y - 1, w + 2, 5, RGB(12, 10, 16));
  spr.drawRect(x - 1, y - 1, w + 2, 5, RGB(230, 230, 240));
  spr.fillRect(x, y, w, 3, RGB(40, 18, 22));
  int fw = (w * hpNow) / hpMax;
  if (fw > 0) {
    spr.fillRect(x, y, fw, 3, fill);
    spr.drawFastHLine(x, y, fw, RGB(255, 255, 220));
  }
}

static void drawEye(int x, int y, int look, uint16_t white, uint16_t pupil) {
  spr.fillCircle(x, y, 2, white);
  spr.fillCircle(x + look, y + 1, 1, pupil);
  spr.drawPixel(x + look, y, TFT_WHITE);
}
static void drawShine(int x, int y) {
  spr.drawPixel(x, y, TFT_WHITE);
  spr.drawPixel(x + 1, y, RGB(255, 255, 210));
}

void drawEnemy(int x, int y, bool flat, uint8_t kind, uint8_t hpv, int anim, bool frozen, int face2, int shootT, bool hitFlash) {
  if (flat) {
    uint16_t br = RGB(150, 80, 30);
    if (kind == EK_FIRE) br = RGB(220, 60, 20);
    if (kind == EK_SNOW) br = RGB(200, 220, 240);
    if (kind == EK_KOOPA) br = RGB(40, 160, 50);
    if (kind == EK_ARMOR) br = RGB(140, 150, 175);
    if (kind == EK_FLY) br = RGB(150, 80, 200);
    if (kind == EK_RUNNER) br = RGB(40, 160, 200);
    if (kind == EK_JUMPER) br = RGB(160, 70, 200);
    if (kind == EK_BOWSERLING) br = RGB(105, 155, 58);
    spr.fillRoundRect(x, y + 11, 14, 4, 2, br);
    spr.drawFastHLine(x + 2, y + 12, 10, RGB(255, 230, 180));
    return;
  }
  int step = (anim / 5) & 1;
  int bob = 0;
  if (kind == EK_FLY) bob = (int)(sinf(anim * 0.12f) * 2.0f);
  else if (kind == EK_FIRE || kind == EK_SNOW) bob = (int)(sinf(anim * 0.14f) * 1.2f);
  else bob = (int)(sinf(anim * 0.09f) * 0.7f);
  y += bob;
  if (kind != EK_FLY) spr.fillEllipse(x + 4, y + 15, 8, 3, RGB(18, 16, 28));
  int look = (face2 < 0) ? -1 : 1;
  switch (kind) {
    case EK_FLY: {
      int fl = (anim / 3) & 1;
      uint16_t body = hitFlash ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(126, 48, 188);
      uint16_t belly = RGB(186, 118, 236);
      uint16_t wing = fl ? RGB(230, 198, 255) : RGB(176, 130, 230);
      spr.fillTriangle(x - 7, y + (fl ? -3 : 12), x + 5, y + 3, x + 5, y + 12, wing);
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 21, y + (fl ? -3 : 12), x + 9, y + 3, x + 9, y + 12, wing);
      spr.drawLine(x - 5, y + (fl ? -1 : 10), x + 5, y + 7, RGB(150, 80, 210));
      spr.drawLine(x + 19, y + (fl ? -1 : 10), x + 9, y + 7, RGB(150, 80, 210));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x, y + 1, 14, 11, 5, body);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + 2, y + 4, 10, 6, 3, belly);
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 2, y + 1, x + 5, y - 4, x + 7, y + 1, body);
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 7, y + 1, x + 10, y - 4, x + 12, y + 1, body);
      drawEye(x + 4 + look, y + 5, look, TFT_WHITE, TFT_BLACK);
      drawEye(x + 10 + look, y + 5, look, TFT_WHITE, TFT_BLACK);
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 5, y + 10, x + 7, y + 13, x + 9, y + 10, RGB(255, 90, 140));
      drawShine(x + 3, y + 3);
      break; }
    case EK_KOOPA: {
      uint16_t sh = hitFlash ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(28, 168, 62);
      uint16_t shL = RGB(86, 214, 110);
      uint16_t shD = RGB(14, 86, 30);
      uint16_t bel = RGB(248, 226, 78);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x, y + 3, 15, 11, 5, sh);
      spr.drawRoundRect(x, y + 3, 15, 11, 5, shD);
      spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 8, 4, shL);
      spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 8, 2, RGB(36, 140, 58));
      spr.fillRect(x + 3, y + 8, 10, 5, bel);
      spr.drawFastHLine(x + 3, y + 10, 10, RGB(196, 150, 30));
      int hx = (look < 0) ? x - 2 : x + 10;
      spr.fillRoundRect(hx, y - 2, 8, 8, 3, bel);
      spr.fillRect(hx + 1, y + 3, 6, 2, RGB(40, 150, 50));
      drawEye(hx + (look < 0 ? 2 : 5), y + 1, look, TFT_WHITE, TFT_BLACK);
      spr.fillTriangle(hx + 2, y - 2, hx + 4, y - 6, hx + 6, y - 2, sh);
      if (step) { spr.fillRoundRect(x + 1, y + 13, 5, 3, 1, shD); spr.fillRoundRect(x + 9, y + 14, 5, 2, 1, shD); }
      else      { spr.fillRoundRect(x + 2, y + 14, 5, 2, 1, shD); spr.fillRoundRect(x + 8, y + 13, 5, 3, 1, shD); }
      drawShine(x + 4, y + 5);
      break; }
    case EK_FIRE: {
      int fl = (anim / 2) % 4;
      uint16_t body = hitFlash ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(232, 42, 18);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x - 1, y + 2, 16, 12, 5, body);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + 2, y + 5, 11, 6, 3, RGB(255, 118, 28));
      spr.fillTriangle(x, y + 3, x + 7, y - 6 - fl, x + 14, y + 3, RGB(255, 160, 28));
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 3, y + 3, x + 7, y - 2 - (fl & 1), x + 11, y + 3, RGB(255, 236, 110));
      if ((anim & 2) == 0) spr.fillTriangle(x + 5, y + 2, x + 7, y - 4 - fl, x + 9, y + 2, RGB(255, 255, 210));
      drawEye(x + 4 + look, y + 6, look, RGB(255, 244, 160), TFT_BLACK);
      drawEye(x + 10 + look, y + 6, look, RGB(255, 244, 160), TFT_BLACK);
      spr.fillRect(x + 4, y + 10, 7, 2, RGB(80, 16, 8));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + (step ? 0 : 3), y + 13, 5, 3, 1, RGB(70, 16, 8));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + (step ? 9 : 8), y + 13, 5, 3, 1, RGB(70, 16, 8));
      drawShine(x + 3, y + 4);
      break; }
    case EK_SNOW: {
      uint16_t sn = hitFlash ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(240, 248, 255);
      spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 10, 7, RGB(168, 188, 214));
      spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 9, 6, sn);
      spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 3, 5, RGB(176, 196, 220));
      spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 2, 4, sn);
      drawShine(x + 4, y + 1);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + 3, y - 5, 9, 4, 1, RGB(36, 40, 72));
      spr.fillRect(x + 5, y - 8, 5, 4, RGB(36, 40, 72));
      spr.fillRect(x + 2, y - 4, 3, 2, RGB(36, 40, 72));
      spr.fillRect(x + 10, y - 4, 3, 2, RGB(36, 40, 72));
      drawEye(x + 5 + look, y + 2, look, TFT_BLACK, TFT_WHITE);
      drawEye(x + 10 + look, y + 2, look, TFT_BLACK, TFT_WHITE);
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 7, y + 4, x + (look < 0 ? -3 : 17), y + 6, x + 7, y + 8, RGB(255, 140, 40));
      spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 9, 1, RGB(36, 40, 72));
      spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 12, 1, RGB(36, 40, 72));
      spr.fillRect(x + 3, y + 13, 3, 2, RGB(36, 40, 72));
      spr.fillRect(x + 9, y + 13, 3, 2, RGB(36, 40, 72));
      break; }
    case EK_ARMOR: {
      uint16_t st = hitFlash ? RGB(255, 255, 255) : RGB(186, 196, 222);
      uint16_t dk = RGB(52, 60, 88);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x - 1, y + 4, 17, 11, 3, st);
      spr.drawRoundRect(x - 1, y + 4, 17, 11, 3, dk);
      spr.fillRect(x + 2, y + 7, 11, 4, RGB(96, 108, 140));
      spr.drawFastHLine(x + 2, y + 7, 11, RGB(230, 236, 255));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x, y - 4, 15, 10, 4, st);
      spr.drawRoundRect(x, y - 4, 15, 10, 4, dk);
      spr.fillRect(x + 3, y, 10, 3, RGB(12, 12, 22));
      spr.fillRect(x + (look < 0 ? 4 : 9), y + 1, 4, 2, RGB(255, 56, 46));
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 5, y - 4, x + 8, y - 10, x + 11, y - 4, RGB(220, 32, 40));
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 7, y - 4, x + 8, y - 8, x + 9, y - 4, RGB(255, 90, 80));
      spr.fillRect(x + 7, y + 10, 3, 3, RGB(230, 180, 40));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + (step ? 0 : 2), y + 14, 6, 3, 1, dk);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + (step ? 9 : 8), y + 14, 6, 3, 1, dk);
      drawShine(x + 3, y - 2);
      drawHpBar(x - 1, y - 12, 16, hpv, 3, RGB(255, 70, 70));
      break; }
    case EK_BOWSERLING: {
      uint16_t shell = hitFlash ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(78, 125, 48);
      uint16_t shellD = RGB(34, 58, 25);
      uint16_t skin = RGB(220, 142, 72);
      spr.fillEllipse(x+7,y+15,9,3,RGB(20,16,20));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x-2,y+2,19,13,5,shell);
      spr.drawRoundRect(x-2,y+2,19,13,5,shellD);
      for(int k=0;k<3;k++) spr.fillTriangle(x+2+k*5,y+3,x+4+k*5,y-2,x+6+k*5,y+3,RGB(225,205,105));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x+1,y-5,14,10,4,skin);
      spr.fillTriangle(x+3,y-5,x+6,y-11,x+8,y-5,RGB(235,210,125));
      spr.fillTriangle(x+8,y-5,x+11,y-11,x+13,y-5,RGB(235,210,125));
      drawEye(x+4+look,y-1,look,TFT_WHITE,TFT_BLACK);
      drawEye(x+10+look,y-1,look,TFT_WHITE,TFT_BLACK);
      spr.fillRect(x+5,y+4,8,2,RGB(60,24,18));
      spr.fillTriangle(x+4,y+6,x+6,y+9,x+8,y+6,TFT_WHITE);
      spr.fillTriangle(x+9,y+6,x+11,y+9,x+13,y+6,TFT_WHITE);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x+(step?0:2),y+14,6,3,1,shellD);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x+(step?9:8),y+14,6,3,1,shellD);
      drawHpBar(x-1,y-15,18,hpv,4,RGB(255,80,55));
      break; }
    case EK_RUNNER: {
      uint16_t c = hitFlash ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(20, 204, 236);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x - 1, y + 2, 16, 12, 5, c);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + 1, y + 5, 12, 6, 3, RGB(12, 120, 168));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + 2, y - 4, 11, 7, 3, RGB(18, 36, 78));
      spr.drawFastHLine(x + 3, y - 3, 8, RGB(80, 140, 220));
      drawEye(x + 5 + look, y, look, TFT_WHITE, TFT_BLACK);
      drawEye(x + 10 + look, y, look, TFT_WHITE, TFT_BLACK);
      spr.fillRect(x + 4, y + 8, 7, 3, RGB(255, 214, 60));
      spr.drawFastHLine(x + 4, y + 8, 7, RGB(255, 255, 180));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + (step ? -1 : 3), y + 13, 6, 3, 1, RGB(18, 36, 78));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + (step ? 10 : 7), y + 13, 6, 3, 1, RGB(18, 36, 78));
      drawShine(x + 2, y + 4);
      drawHpBar(x - 1, y - 11, 16, hpv, 2, RGB(60, 220, 255));
      break; }
    case EK_JUMPER: {
      uint16_t c = hitFlash ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(198, 72, 236);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x - 1, y + 2, 16, 12, 6, c);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + 2, y + 5, 11, 6, 4, RGB(150, 48, 200));
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 1, y + 3, x + 5, y - 5, x + 8, y + 3, RGB(168, 70, 230));
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 7, y + 3, x + 11, y - 5, x + 14, y + 3, RGB(168, 70, 230));
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 3, y + 2, x + 5, y - 3, x + 7, y + 2, RGB(230, 170, 255));
      spr.fillTriangle(x + 8, y + 2, x + 11, y - 3, x + 13, y + 2, RGB(230, 170, 255));
      drawEye(x + 5 + look, y + 7, look, TFT_WHITE, TFT_BLACK);
      drawEye(x + 10 + look, y + 7, look, TFT_WHITE, TFT_BLACK);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x, y + 13, 6, 3, 1, RGB(90, 28, 120));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + 9, y + 13, 6, 3, 1, RGB(90, 28, 120));
      drawShine(x + 3, y + 4);
      drawHpBar(x - 1, y - 10, 16, hpv, 2, RGB(210, 90, 255));
      break; }
    case EK_SHOOT: {
      bool charge = shootT < 20;
      uint16_t bd = charge ? RGB(216, 68, 36) : RGB(86, 92, 118);
      uint16_t dk = RGB(32, 36, 52);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x - 1, y + 5, 17, 10, 3, bd);
      spr.drawRoundRect(x - 1, y + 5, 17, 10, 3, dk);
      spr.fillRect(x + 2, y + 14, 12, 3, dk);
      spr.fillRect(x + 3, y + 8, 10, 4, RGB(48, 52, 70));
      int bx = (look < 0) ? x - 8 : x + 12;
      spr.fillRoundRect(bx, y + 4, 12, 6, 2, dk);
      spr.fillRect((look < 0) ? bx : bx + 10, y + 2, 2, 10, RGB(110, 118, 148));
      spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 3, 6, bd);
      spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 3, 4, charge ? RGB(255, 160, 50) : RGB(130, 140, 170));
      drawEye(x + 5 + look, y + 3, look, charge ? RGB(255, 240, 80) : RGB(240, 244, 255), TFT_BLACK);
      drawEye(x + 10 + look, y + 3, look, charge ? RGB(255, 240, 80) : RGB(240, 244, 255), TFT_BLACK);
      if (charge) {
        spr.fillCircle(bx + (look < 0 ? 0 : 11), y + 7, 3 + (shootT & 1), RGB(255, 210, 60));
        spr.fillCircle(bx + (look < 0 ? 0 : 11), y + 7, 1, TFT_WHITE);
      }
      drawShine(x + 3, y + 6);
      break; }
    default: { // goomba
      uint16_t br = hitFlash ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(186, 96, 32);
      uint16_t hi = RGB(214, 132, 58);
      uint16_t dk = RGB(86, 36, 12);
      int sq = step ? 1 : 0;
      spr.fillRoundRect(x - 2 - sq, y + sq, 18 + sq * 2, 12 - sq, 6, br);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + 1 - sq, y + 2 + sq, 13 + sq * 2, 7 - sq, 4, hi);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + 3, y + 9, 10, 4, 2, RGB(248, 220, 176));
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + (step ? -1 : 2), y + 12, 7, 4, 2, dk);
      spr.fillRoundRect(x + (step ? 9 : 7), y + 12, 7, 4, 2, dk);
      drawEye(x + 4 + look, y + 4, look, TFT_WHITE, TFT_BLACK);
      drawEye(x + 10 + look, y + 4, look, TFT_WHITE, TFT_BLACK);
      spr.drawLine(x + 1, y + 2, x + 5, y + 4, RGB(60, 22, 6));
      spr.drawLine(x + 15, y + 2, x + 11, y + 4, RGB(60, 22, 6));
      spr.fillRect(x + 5, y + 8, 6, 2, RGB(90, 34, 10));
      drawShine(x + 2, y + 2);
      break; }
  }
  if (frozen) {
    spr.drawRoundRect(x - 3, y - 5, 21, 23, 4, RGB(180, 240, 255));
    spr.fillRect(x - 1, y - 4, 5, 2, RGB(230, 250, 255));
    spr.drawFastHLine(x, y + 4, 14, RGB(200, 245, 255));
    spr.drawFastVLine(x + 12, y - 1, 8, RGB(210, 245, 255));
    spr.fillCircle(x + 2, y + 1, 1, RGB(230, 250, 255));
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  UFO BOSS ART
// ============================================================
void drawBoss(int y0) {
  if (!boss.active) return;
  int bx = (int)boss.x - camX, by = (int)boss.y - y0;
  int hover = (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.08f) * 2.0f);
  by += hover;
  if (boss.dieT > 0) { bx += (int)((tAnim * 7) % 5) - 2; by += (int)((tAnim * 3) % 3) - 1; }
  if (by > BANDH + 34 || by < -52) return;
  bool fl = (boss.hurtT > 0 && (boss.hurtT & 2)) || (boss.dieT > 0 && (tAnim & 2));
  uint16_t metal = fl ? RGB(255, 255, 255) : RGB(170, 180, 205);
  uint16_t metalD = fl ? RGB(255, 220, 220) : RGB(90, 100, 135);
  if (bossStyle == 1) { metal = fl ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(80, 190, 170); metalD = RGB(25, 80, 75); }
  else if (bossStyle == 2) { metal = fl ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(175, 70, 170); metalD = RGB(75, 20, 80); }
  else if (bossStyle == 3) { metal = fl ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(225, 110, 55); metalD = RGB(100, 35, 18); }
  else if (bossStyle == 4) { metal = fl ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(90, 110, 180); metalD = RGB(30, 40, 90); }
  // thrusters
  int fh = 4 + (tAnim & 3) + (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.35f) * 2.0f);
  spr.fillTriangle(bx + 12, by + 21, bx + 19, by + 21, bx + 15, by + 21 + fh, RGB(255, 170, 50));
  spr.fillTriangle(bx + 25, by + 21, bx + 32, by + 21, bx + 28, by + 21 + fh, RGB(255, 170, 50));
  spr.fillTriangle(bx + 14, by + 21, bx + 17, by + 21, bx + 15, by + 21 + fh - 2, RGB(255, 245, 160));
  spr.fillTriangle(bx + 27, by + 21, bx + 30, by + 21, bx + 28, by + 21 + fh - 2, RGB(255, 245, 160));
  // charging
  if (boss.freeze == 0 && boss.dieT == 0 && boss.atk < 26) {
    int r = 3 + (26 - boss.atk) / 3;
    uint16_t cc = (boss.phase == 3) ? RGB(255, 120, 60) : RGB(255, 90, 170);
    spr.drawCircle(bx + 22, by + 25, r, cc);
    spr.fillCircle(bx + 22, by + 25, 3 + (tAnim & 1), cc);
    spr.fillCircle(bx + 22, by + 25, 1, RGB(255, 240, 250));
  }
  // dome + alien
  spr.fillCircle(bx + 22, by + 8, 11, RGB(140, 230, 255));
  spr.drawCircle(bx + 22, by + 8, 11, RGB(220, 250, 255));
  spr.drawLine(bx + 16, by + 3, bx + 19, by + 1, RGB(255, 255, 255));
  spr.fillCircle(bx + 22, by + 9, 6, boss.phase == 3 ? RGB(190, 220, 60) : RGB(90, 220, 90));
  int look = (px + PW / 2 > boss.x + 22) ? 1 : -1;
  uint16_t eye = (boss.phase >= 2) ? RGB(255, 40, 40) : TFT_BLACK;
  spr.fillRect(bx + 19 + look, by + 7, 3, 4, eye); spr.fillRect(bx + 23 + look, by + 7, 3, 4, eye);
  if (boss.phase >= 2) {
    spr.drawLine(bx + 18 + look, by + 5, bx + 21 + look, by + 7, TFT_BLACK);
    spr.drawLine(bx + 27 + look, by + 5, bx + 24 + look, by + 7, TFT_BLACK);
  } else {
    spr.drawPixel(bx + 20 + look, by + 8, RGB(255, 255, 255)); spr.drawPixel(bx + 24 + look, by + 8, RGB(255, 255, 255));
  }
  spr.drawFastVLine(bx + 22, by - 5, 5, metalD);
  spr.fillCircle(bx + 22, by - 6, 2, (tAnim & 4) ? RGB(255, 80, 80) : RGB(255, 200, 80));
  // hull
  spr.fillRoundRect(bx, by + 10, BOSS_W, 11, 6, metal);
  spr.fillRoundRect(bx + 6, by + 17, BOSS_W - 12, 6, 3, metalD);
  spr.drawFastHLine(bx + 4, by + 12, BOSS_W - 8, RGB(230, 240, 255));
  if (boss.phase == 3) {                       // side cannons
    spr.fillRect(bx - 4, by + 13, 6, 5, metalD); spr.fillRect(bx + BOSS_W - 2, by + 13, 6, 5, metalD);
    spr.fillRect(bx - 5, by + 14, 2, 3, RGB(255, 120, 60)); spr.fillRect(bx + BOSS_W + 3, by + 14, 2, 3, RGB(255, 120, 60));
  }
  if (boss.hp * 3 <= boss.hpMax * 2) {         // cracks
    spr.drawLine(bx + 8, by + 12, bx + 13, by + 19, metalD); spr.drawLine(bx + 13, by + 19, bx + 11, by + 21, metalD);
  }
  if (boss.hp * 3 <= boss.hpMax) {
    spr.drawLine(bx + 33, by + 11, bx + 29, by + 18, metalD); spr.drawLine(bx + 29, by + 18, bx + 32, by + 21, metalD);
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    uint16_t lc = (((tAnim / 4) + i) & 1) ? RGB(255, 230, 80) : RGB(255, 80, 120);
    if (boss.phase >= 2) lc = (((tAnim / 2) + i) & 1) ? RGB(255, 60, 40) : RGB(255, 200, 60);
    spr.fillCircle(bx + 7 + i * 7, by + 15, 2, lc);
  }
  if (boss.shield > 0 && (boss.shield > 20 || (tAnim & 1))) {
    int sr = 30 + (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.16f) * 2.0f);
    spr.drawCircle(bx + 22, by + 12, sr, RGB(120, 230, 255));
    spr.drawCircle(bx + 22, by + 12, sr - 2, RGB(200, 250, 255));
    for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
      float a = tAnim * 0.10f + k * 1.5708f;
      int sx = bx + 22 + (int)(cosf(a) * sr);
      int sy = by + 12 + (int)(sinf(a) * sr);
      spr.fillCircle(sx, sy, 2, RGB(170, 250, 255));
    }
  }
  if (boss.freeze > 0) spr.drawRoundRect(bx - 2, by - 2, BOSS_W + 4, BOSS_H + 6, 6, RGB(180, 240, 255));
}
// ===== BOWSER REFERENCE SPRITES v3.6 =====
// These sprites are generated directly from the user's supplied reference image.
// 0xF81F is the transparent key color.
// Normal: 72x93. Lying: 93x72.

void drawBowserBody(int bx, int by, int face, bool down) {
  if (down) {
    int bob = ((tAnim / 8) & 1);
    int wob = ((tAnim / 6) & 1) ? 1 : -1;
    spr.pushImage(bx + wob, by + bob, 93, 72, (uint16_t *)BOWSER_DOWN, BOWSER_KEY);
    return;
  }

  const uint16_t *bmp = (face < 0) ? BOWSER_L : BOWSER_R;
  int bob = ((tAnim / 6) & 1);
  bool walk = (bw.st == BW_CHASE || bw.st == BW_UFO || bw.st == BW_VULN);
  int step = walk ? ((tAnim / 5) & 1) : 0;
  int dir = (face < 0) ? -1 : 1;
  int legX = walk ? (step ? 3 * dir : -2 * dir) : 0;
  int bodyX = walk ? (step ? -1 * dir : 1 * dir) : 0;
  int armY = walk && step ? 1 : 0;

  // Body + head stay mostly still, legs step like Mario's walk cycle.
  spr.pushImage(bx + bodyX, by + bob, 72, 58, (uint16_t *)bmp, BOWSER_KEY);
  spr.pushImage(bx + legX, by + bob + 58 + armY, 72, 35, (uint16_t *)(bmp + 58 * 72), BOWSER_KEY);

  bool attack = (bw.st == BW_UFO || bw.st == BW_CRASH || bw.st == BW_VULN || bw.st == BW_CHASE);
  if (attack && ((tAnim / 4) & 1)) {
    int fx = (face < 0) ? bx - 10 : bx + 70;
    spr.fillRect(fx, by + 31 + bob, 5, 4, RGB(255, 220, 70));
    spr.fillRect(fx + ((face < 0) ? -5 : 5), by + 34 + bob, 4, 3, RGB(255, 140, 30));
    spr.fillRect(fx + ((face < 0) ? -8 : 8), by + 36 + bob, 3, 2, RGB(255, 60, 20));
  }
}
void drawTalkBox(int y0) {
  if (bw.st != BW_TALK && bw.st != BW_HOOK) return;
  int boxY = 168 - y0;
  if (boxY > BANDH || boxY + 70 < 0) return;
  spr.fillRoundRect(8, boxY, 304, 64, 6, RGB(16, 14, 28));
  spr.drawRoundRect(8, boxY, 304, 64, 6, RGB(255, 220, 80));
  spr.drawRoundRect(10, boxY + 2, 300, 60, 5, RGB(80, 70, 40));
  const char *line = (bw.st == BW_HOOK) ? "BOW|Hook shot!" : BW_LINES[constrain(bw.talkI, 0, BW_LINES_N - 1)];
  bool marioTalk = (line[0] == 'M');
  spr.fillRoundRect(16, boxY + 8, 44, 48, 4, RGB(8, 8, 16));
  spr.drawRoundRect(16, boxY + 8, 44, 48, 4, RGB(220, 200, 80));
  if (marioTalk) {
    mR = RGB(224, 36, 28); mU = RGB(36, 70, 224); mS = RGB(255, 204, 150);
    mB = RGB(110, 58, 20); mY = RGB(255, 220, 40); mK = RGB(0, 0, 0); mW = RGB(255, 255, 255);
    drawMarioG(spr, 22, boxY + 12, 32, 40, 0, false, BANDH);
  } else {
    drawBowserBody(18, boxY + 14, 1, false);
  }
  spr.setTextColor(marioTalk ? RGB(120, 200, 255) : RGB(255, 160, 60));
  spr.drawString(marioTalk ? "MARIO" : "BOWSER", 70, boxY + 8, 1);
  spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
  spr.drawString(line + 4, 70, boxY + 24, 2);
  if (bw.st == BW_TALK) {
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(180, 180, 200));
    spr.drawString("A: next", 250, boxY + 48, 1);
  }
}
void drawBowserWorld(int y0) {
  if (bw.st == BW_OFF) return;
  int bx = (int)bw.x - camX, by = (int)bw.y - y0;
  for (auto &m : mush) if (m.alive) {
    int mx = (int)m.x - camX, my = (int)m.y - y0;
    if (my < -16 || my > BANDH + 8) continue;
    spr.fillEllipse(mx + 7, my + 12, 7, 3, RGB(16, 14, 20));
    spr.fillCircle(mx + 7, my + 4, 6, RGB(220, 40, 50));
    spr.fillCircle(mx + 7, my + 4, 3, RGB(245, 230, 220));
    spr.fillRect(mx + 6, my + 8, 3, 5, RGB(245, 230, 220));
    spr.fillCircle(mx + 5, my + 3, 1, TFT_BLACK);
    spr.fillCircle(mx + 9, my + 3, 1, TFT_BLACK);
  }
  for (auto &b : bombs) if (b.alive) {
    int x = (int)b.x - camX, y = (int)b.y - y0;
    if (y < -8 || y > BANDH + 8) continue;
    spr.fillCircle(x, y, 4, RGB(40, 40, 48));
    spr.fillCircle(x, y - 2, 2, RGB(255, 80, 40));
  }
  if (bw.st == BW_UFO || bw.st == BW_CRASH) {
    // reuse UFO hull with Bowser in the dome
    float oldx = boss.x, oldy = boss.y; bool oldA = boss.active;
    int oldp = boss.phase, oldh = boss.hurtT, oldd = boss.dieT, olds = boss.shield, oldf = boss.freeze, oldatk = boss.atk;
    boss.active = true; boss.x = bw.x; boss.y = bw.y; boss.phase = 2; boss.hurtT = bw.cd; boss.dieT = (bw.st==BW_CRASH)?20:0;
    boss.shield = 0; boss.freeze = 0; boss.atk = 40;
    drawBoss(y0);
    boss.x = oldx; boss.y = oldy; boss.active = oldA; boss.phase = oldp; boss.hurtT = oldh;
    boss.dieT = oldd; boss.shield = olds; boss.freeze = oldf; boss.atk = oldatk;
    drawBowserBody(bx + 6, by - 2, bw.face, false);
  } else {
    if (by > -40 && by < BANDH + 20) drawBowserBody(bx, by, bw.face, bw.st == BW_TALK || bw.st == BW_HOOK || bw.st == BW_VULN);
    if (bw.st == BW_HOOK) {
      int hx = bx + 18, hy = by + 4;
      int top = bw.hookY - y0;
      spr.drawFastVLine(hx, top, hy - top, RGB(220, 220, 230));
      spr.fillTriangle(hx - 4, top, hx + 4, top, hx, top - 6, RGB(200, 200, 210));
      spr.fillCircle(bx + 22, by + 8, 3, RGB(255, 210, 80));
    }
  }
  drawTalkBox(y0);
}
void drawOrbs(int y0) {
  for (auto &o : orbs) if (o.alive) {
    int ox = (int)o.x - camX, oy = (int)o.y - y0;
    if (oy < -14 || oy > BANDH + 14) continue;
    uint16_t c0, c1, c2;
    if (o.st == 0) {
      bool warn = o.t < 20 && (tAnim & 2);
      c0 = warn ? RGB(150, 90, 10) : RGB(140, 15, 90);
      c1 = warn ? RGB(255, 190, 50) : RGB(255, 70, 160);
      c2 = RGB(255, 235, 245);
    } else if (o.st == 2) { c0 = RGB(150, 50, 10); c1 = RGB(255, 140, 30); c2 = RGB(255, 240, 160); }
    else { c0 = RGB(15, 80, 150); c1 = RGB(60, 210, 255); c2 = RGB(230, 255, 255); }
    spr.drawCircle(ox, oy, 7 + ((tAnim >> 1) & 1), c0);
    spr.fillCircle(ox, oy, 6, c0); spr.fillCircle(ox, oy, 4, c1); spr.fillCircle(ox, oy, 2, c2);
    float a = tAnim * 0.35f + o.st * 1.5f;
    for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++)
      spr.fillRect(ox + (int)(cosf(a + k * 3.1416f) * 9) - 1, oy + (int)(sinf(a + k * 3.1416f) * 9) - 1, 2, 2, c2);
  }
}

static inline int wrapPos(int i, int gap, int cam, int div, int period, int margin) {
  int v = (i * gap - cam / div) % period;
  if (v < 0) v += period;
  return v - margin;
}

#define LY(v) ((v) - y0)

static inline bool smwCastleLook() {
  return inChapter2 || lvTheme == TH_CASTLE || lvTheme == TH_VOLCANO || (bw.st != BW_OFF);
}

static void drawSmwCloud(int cx, int cy) {
  if (cy < -16 || cy > BANDH + 16) return;
  uint16_t c = RGB(236, 246, 255);
  uint16_t e = RGB(200, 220, 245);
  spr.fillCircle(cx, cy, 9, c);
  spr.fillCircle(cx + 12, cy + 2, 8, c);
  spr.fillCircle(cx - 11, cy + 3, 7, c);
  spr.fillCircle(cx + 4, cy - 4, 7, c);
  spr.fillRect(cx - 12, cy + 2, 28, 8, c);
  spr.drawFastHLine(cx - 10, cy + 9, 24, e);
}

static void drawSmwBanner(int x, int y, int h) {
  if (y > BANDH || y + h < -4) return;
  int wav = (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.22f + x * 0.05f) * 3);
  spr.fillRect(x + 6, y, 3, h, RGB(92, 58, 28));
  spr.fillTriangle(x + 9, y + 4, x + 22 + wav, y + 12, x + 9, y + 22, RGB(176, 28, 28));
  spr.fillTriangle(x + 9, y + 6, x + 18 + wav, y + 12, x + 9, y + 18, RGB(210, 46, 40));
  spr.fillCircle(x + 13 + wav / 2, y + 12, 2, RGB(28, 16, 16));
}

static void drawSmwTower(int x, int y0, int baseY, int w, int h, bool right) {
  int top = baseY - h;
  int yy = top - y0;
  // body
  uint16_t stone = RGB(112, 118, 132);
  uint16_t dark  = RGB(64, 70, 86);
  uint16_t lite  = RGB(168, 174, 186);
  spr.fillRect(x, yy, w, h + 8, stone);
  spr.drawFastVLine(x, yy, h + 8, dark);
  spr.drawFastVLine(x + w - 1, yy, h + 8, dark);
  spr.drawFastVLine(x + 3, yy, h + 8, lite);
  // merlons
  for (int i = 0; i < w; i += 8) {
    spr.fillRect(x + i, yy - 7, 5, 8, stone);
    spr.drawRect(x + i, yy - 7, 5, 8, dark);
  }
  // windows
  for (int wy = top + 18; wy < baseY - 10; wy += 22) {
    int wy0 = wy - y0;
    if (wy0 < -10 || wy0 > BANDH) continue;
    spr.fillRect(x + w/2 - 5, wy0, 8, 11, RGB(18, 28, 48));
    spr.drawRect(x + w/2 - 5, wy0, 8, 11, RGB(40, 48, 64));
    spr.drawFastHLine(x + w/2 - 4, wy0 + 3, 6, RGB(70, 150, 210));
  }
  // brick lines
  for (int by = top + 10; by < baseY; by += 8) {
    int by0 = by - y0;
    if (by0 >= 0 && by0 < BANDH) spr.drawFastHLine(x + 1, by0, w - 2, dark);
  }
  drawSmwBanner(right ? (x - 4) : (x + w - 10), top + 8 - y0, 28);
}

static void drawSmwFarCastle(int y0) {
  // distant silhouettes, parallax
  uint16_t far = RGB(28, 62, 148);
  uint16_t mid = RGB(36, 74, 160);
  uint16_t lit = RGB(52, 96, 176);
  for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
    int cx = wrapPos(i, 70, camX, 10, 490, 30);
    int h = 38 + (i % 4) * 14;
    int top = 78 - h;
    spr.fillRect(cx, LY(top), 18 + (i & 1) * 6, h + 40, far);
    spr.fillRect(cx + 3, LY(top + 8), 4, 7, RGB(20, 48, 110));
    spr.fillRect(cx + 2, LY(top - 6), 5, 7, mid);
    spr.fillRect(cx + 10, LY(top - 10), 4, 11, mid);
    if ((i & 1) == 0) spr.fillTriangle(cx + 6, LY(top - 16), cx + 2, LY(top - 6), cx + 10, LY(top - 6), lit);
  }
  // wall line
  spr.fillRect(0, LY(118), SCRW, 18, RGB(32, 68, 150));
  spr.drawFastHLine(0, LY(118), SCRW, RGB(48, 90, 168));
}

static void drawSmwLava(int y0) {
  const int lavaY = 222;
  int yy = lavaY - y0;
  if (yy > BANDH) return;
  if (yy < -30) return;
  // under-walk supports
  for (int x = -((camX / 3) % 28); x < SCRW; x += 28) {
    spr.fillRect(x + 6, LY(198), 6, 24, RGB(58, 62, 74));
    spr.drawFastVLine(x + 6, LY(198), 24, RGB(36, 38, 48));
    spr.drawFastVLine(x + 11, LY(198), 24, RGB(90, 94, 108));
  }
  spr.fillRect(0, yy, SCRW, BANDH, RGB(210, 36, 12));
  spr.fillRect(0, yy + 8, SCRW, BANDH, RGB(230, 52, 14));
  for (int x = 0; x < SCRW; x += 5) {
    int w = (int)(sinf((x + tAnim * 6) * 0.11f) * 3.0f);
    spr.fillRect(x, yy + w, 5, 5, RGB(255, 150, 28));
    if (((x / 5 + tAnim / 3) & 3) == 0) spr.fillRect(x + 1, yy + w - 2, 3, 3, RGB(255, 230, 90));
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
    int x = (i * 37 + tAnim * 2) % SCRW;
    int y = yy + 10 + ((i * 13 + tAnim) % 18);
    if (y >= 0 && y < BANDH) spr.fillRect(x, y, 2, 2, (i & 1) ? RGB(255, 210, 70) : RGB(255, 120, 30));
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  BACKDROPS (parallax per biome)
// ============================================================

void drawBackdrop(int y0) {
  const int HZ = 208;
  switch (lvTheme) {
    case TH_GRASS:
    case TH_SKY: {
      for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
        int gy = y0 + y;
        spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(40 + gy / 20, 120 + gy / 16, 230 - gy / 18));
      }
      // sun
      spr.fillRect(268, LY(24), 20, 20, RGB(255, 236, 120));
      spr.fillRect(272, LY(28), 12, 12, RGB(255, 252, 200));
      // far hills, stepped 16-bit silhouettes
      for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 90, camX, 10, 540, 40);
        int h = 28 + (i % 3) * 10;
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - h), 50, h + 20, RGB(86, 176, 108));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 10, LY(HZ - h - 8), 18, 10, RGB(86, 176, 108));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 70, camX, 4, 490, 20);
        int h = 16 + (i % 2) * 8;
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - h), 36, h + 16, RGB(48, 150, 70));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 8, LY(HZ - h - 6), 12, 8, RGB(56, 168, 78));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 100, camX, 8, 500, 50) + (int)(sinf((tAnim + i * 18) * 0.03f) * 3);
        drawSmwCloud(cx, LY(22 + (i * 13) % 40));
      }
      break; }
    case TH_DESERT: {
      for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
        int gy = y0 + y;
        spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(236 - gy / 20, 188 - gy / 18, 96 + gy / 30));
      }
      spr.fillRect(248, LY(28), 22, 22, RGB(255, 168, 48));
      spr.fillRect(252, LY(32), 14, 14, RGB(255, 220, 120));
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 120, camX, 9, 480, 50);
        int h = 36 + (i % 3) * 14;
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - h), 64, h + 12, RGB(210, 154, 72));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 20, LY(HZ - h - 16), 24, 18, RGB(196, 138, 58));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 44, LY(HZ - h + 8), 20, h - 8, RGB(176, 120, 48));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 88, camX, 4, 440, 20);
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - 14), 40, 22, RGB(228, 178, 92));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 70, camX, 3, 420, 10);
        int h = 18 + (i % 3) * 6;
        spr.fillRect(cx + 4, LY(HZ - h), 4, h, RGB(56, 128, 48));
        spr.fillRect(cx - 4, LY(HZ - h + 6), 8, 4, RGB(56, 128, 48));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 8, LY(HZ - h + 4), 8, 4, RGB(72, 150, 56));
      }
      break; }
    case TH_JUNGLE: {
      for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
        int gy = y0 + y;
        spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(28 + gy / 30, 92 + gy / 18, 48 + gy / 40));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 78, camX, 7, 468, 24);
        spr.fillRect(cx + 8, LY(70), 8, 140, RGB(62, 48, 28));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 10, LY(70), 4, 140, RGB(92, 70, 36));
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(56), 28, 16, RGB(24, 110, 44));
        spr.fillRect(cx - 10, LY(70), 22, 14, RGB(32, 132, 52));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 16, LY(74), 22, 14, RGB(20, 96, 40));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 52, camX, 3, 416, 8);
        int len = 18 + (i * 17) % 40;
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(0), 3, len, RGB(16, 86, 32));
        spr.fillRect(cx - 3, LY(len), 9, 4, RGB(48, 168, 56));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 96, camX, 2, 480, 16);
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - 18), 28, 22, RGB(20, 88, 36));
      }
      break; }
    case TH_WINTER: {
      for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
        int gy = y0 + y;
        spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(168 + gy / 30, 200 + gy / 40, 236));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 110, camX, 10, 440, 40);
        int h = 50 + (i % 3) * 16;
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - h), 70, h + 16, RGB(148, 176, 214));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 18, LY(HZ - h - 18), 28, 20, RGB(236, 244, 255));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 26, LY(HZ - h - 28), 12, 12, RGB(255, 255, 255));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 64, camX, 3, 448, 12);
        int h = 22 + (i % 3) * 8;
        spr.fillRect(cx + 6, LY(HZ - 6), 4, 8, RGB(92, 60, 36));
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - h), 16, 8, RGB(28, 110, 72));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 3, LY(HZ - h - 8), 10, 8, RGB(28, 110, 72));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 5, LY(HZ - h - 14), 6, 6, RGB(240, 248, 255));
      }
      break; }
    case TH_CAVE: {
      for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
        int gy = y0 + y;
        spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(18 + gy / 40, 14 + gy / 50, 28 + gy / 30));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 56, camX, 6, 392, 16);
        int len = 24 + (i * 19) % 48;
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(0), 16, len, RGB(40, 32, 52));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 4, LY(len - 8), 8, 10, RGB(70, 56, 88));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 62, camX, 4, 434, 16);
        int len = 20 + (i * 17) % 40;
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - len), 18, len + 8, RGB(46, 38, 60));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 48, camX, 3, 384, 8);
        int cy = 36 + (i * 29) % 120;
        int on = ((tAnim / 5 + i) & 3);
        uint16_t cc = on ? ((i & 1) ? RGB(80, 220, 200) : RGB(180, 110, 255)) : RGB(40, 36, 52);
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(cy), 4, 4, cc);
      }
      break; }
    case TH_NIGHT: {
      for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
        int gy = y0 + y;
        spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(8, 10 + gy / 40, 36 + gy / 18));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
        int sx = (i * 47 + 13 - camX / 12) % 340; if (sx < 0) sx += 340;
        int sy = 6 + (i * 29) % 110;
        int yy = LY(sy);
        if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH) {
          int tw = ((tAnim / 8 + i) % 6 == 0) ? 2 : 1;
          spr.fillRect(sx, yy, tw, tw, RGB(240, 240, 255));
        }
      }
      spr.fillRect(48, LY(28), 18, 18, RGB(236, 236, 210));
      spr.fillRect(56, LY(24), 10, 10, RGB(8, 12, 40));
      for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 88, camX, 8, 440, 30);
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - 28), 54, 40, RGB(16, 22, 48));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 14, LY(HZ - 40), 16, 14, RGB(16, 22, 48));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 70, camX, 3, 420, 10);
        spr.fillRect(cx + 6, LY(HZ - 22), 4, 22, RGB(12, 16, 30));
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - 30), 16, 10, RGB(14, 28, 40));
      }
      break; }
    case TH_VOLCANO: {
      for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
        int gy = y0 + y;
        spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(48 + gy / 16, 18 + gy / 40, 16));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 110, camX, 10, 440, 40);
        int h = 54 + (i % 3) * 16;
        spr.fillRect(cx, LY(HZ - h), 72, h + 16, RGB(72, 28, 22));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 24, LY(HZ - h - 10), 16, 14, RGB(255, 96, 28));
        spr.fillRect(cx + 28, LY(HZ - h - 22 - ((tAnim / 4 + i) % 6)), 8, 14, RGB(40, 20, 16));
      }
      break; }
    case TH_CASTLE: {
      // SNES-style World 1-4 castle sky
      for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
        int gy = y0 + y;
        int r = 12 + gy / 18;
        int g = 62 + gy / 10;
        int b = 188 - gy / 14;
        spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(r, g, b));
      }
      drawSmwFarCastle(y0);
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
        int cx = wrapPos(i, 110, camX, 8, 440, 50) + (int)(sinf((tAnim + i * 20) * 0.03f) * 4);
        int cy = 28 + (i * 17) % 36 + (int)(sinf((tAnim + i * 11) * 0.05f) * 2);
        drawSmwCloud(cx, LY(cy));
      }
      // screen-locked side towers like the reference shot
      drawSmwTower(-8, y0, 196, 46, 150, false);
      drawSmwTower(282, y0, 196, 46, 150, true);
      break; }
    case TH_SPACE: {
      for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
        int gy = y0 + y;
        spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(8, 4, 22 + gy / 20));
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 36; i++) {
        int sx = (i * 41 + 7 - camX / 14) % 330; if (sx < 0) sx += 330;
        int sy = 4 + (i * 23) % 200;
        int yy = LY(sy);
        if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH) {
          int tw = ((tAnim / 5 + i) % 7 == 0) ? 2 : 1;
          spr.fillRect(sx, yy, tw, tw, (i & 3) ? RGB(230, 230, 255) : RGB(255, 210, 160));
        }
      }
      spr.fillRect(214, LY(58), 40, 40, RGB(96, 52, 160));
      spr.fillRect(222, LY(50), 24, 24, RGB(140, 86, 210));
      spr.fillRect(228, LY(46), 10, 10, RGB(180, 130, 240));
      spr.drawRect(206, LY(50), 56, 56, RGB(160, 120, 230));
      spr.fillRect(58, LY(44), 16, 16, RGB(200, 90, 70));
      spr.fillRect(62, LY(40), 6, 6, RGB(230, 130, 100));
      int sh = (tAnim * 5) % 500;
      if (sh < 140) spr.fillRect(sh, LY(16 + sh / 3), 18, 2, RGB(255, 255, 255));
      break; }
  }
}

void drawAmbient(int y0) {
  switch (lvTheme) {
    case TH_WINTER:
      for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
        int x = ((i * 61 - camX * (1 + i % 3) / 5 + (int)(sinf((tAnim + i * 7) * 0.07f) * 10)) % 330 + 330) % 330 - 5;
        int y = (i * 37 + (int)tAnim * (1 + i % 3)) % 250 - 6;
        int yy = y - y0;
        if (yy >= -2 && yy < BANDH) spr.fillRect(x, yy, 2, 2, RGB(255, 255, 255));
      }
      break;
    case TH_DESERT:
      for (int i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
        int x = ((i * 53 + (int)tAnim * (3 + i % 3)) % 360) - 20;
        int y = 30 + (i * 41) % 190;
        int yy = y - y0;
        if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH) spr.drawFastHLine(x, yy, 6 + i % 4, RGB(255, 235, 170));
      }
      break;
    case TH_VOLCANO:
      for (int i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
        int x = ((i * 43 + (int)(sinf((tAnim + i * 11) * 0.05f) * 12) - camX / 2) % 330 + 330) % 330;
        int y = 250 - ((int)tAnim * (1 + i % 3) + i * 29) % 250;
        int yy = y - y0;
        if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH) spr.fillRect(x, yy, 2, 2, (i & 1) ? RGB(255, 150, 40) : RGB(255, 230, 100));
      }
      break;
    case TH_NIGHT:
      for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        int x = ((i * 71 + (int)(sinf((tAnim + i * 20) * 0.03f) * 30) - camX / 2) % 340 + 340) % 340;
        int y = 90 + (i * 37) % 120 + (int)(cosf((tAnim + i * 9) * 0.04f) * 8);
        int yy = y - y0;
        if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH && ((tAnim / 10 + i) % 4)) spr.fillRect(x, yy, 2, 2, RGB(230, 255, 100));
      }
      break;
    case TH_JUNGLE:
      for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        int x = ((i * 59 - (int)tAnim * 1 - camX / 2) % 330 + 330) % 330;
        int y = ((int)tAnim * (1 + i % 2) + i * 53) % 250;
        int yy = y - y0;
        if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH) { spr.fillRect(x, yy, 3, 2, RGB(90, 200, 70)); spr.drawPixel(x + 1, yy + 2, RGB(50, 140, 40)); }
      }
      break;
    case TH_CAVE:
      for (int i = 0; i < 12; i++) {
        int x = ((i * 67 - camX / 2 + (int)(sinf((tAnim + i * 13) * 0.04f) * 8)) % 330 + 330) % 330;
        int y = 250 - ((int)tAnim / 2 * (1 + i % 2) + i * 41) % 250;
        int yy = y - y0;
        if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH) spr.fillRect(x, yy, 2, 2, (i & 1) ? RGB(120, 240, 220) : RGB(190, 140, 255));
      }
      break;
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  TILES  — 16x16 pixel-art, animated
// ============================================================
static inline void pixFill(int x, int y, int w, int h, uint16_t c) {
  if (w <= 0 || h <= 0) return;
  if (y + h <= 0 || y >= BANDH) return;
  if (y < 0) { h += y; y = 0; }
  if (y + h > BANDH) h = BANDH - y;
  if (h > 0) spr.fillRect(x, y, w, h, c);
}

static void drawPixGround(int x, int y, int tx, int ty, bool top) {
  uint16_t dirt  = C_GROUND;
  uint16_t dirtD = C_GROUNDD;
  uint16_t grass = C_GRASS;
  uint16_t ink   = RGB(20, 16, 24);
  uint16_t hi    = RGB(255, 255, 255);
  if (smwCastleLook()) {
    // SNES castle walkway brick
    uint16_t a = RGB(132, 136, 148), b = RGB(96, 100, 114), d = RGB(52, 54, 66), l = RGB(188, 192, 204);
    pixFill(x, y, 16, 16, a);
    int ox = (ty & 1) ? 8 : 0;
    pixFill(x + ox, y + 0, 8, 8, b);
    pixFill(x + (8 - ox), y + 8, 8, 8, b);
    pixFill(x, y, 16, 1, d);
    pixFill(x, y + 8, 16, 1, d);
    pixFill(x + ox, y, 1, 8, d);
    pixFill(x + (8 - ox), y + 8, 1, 8, d);
    pixFill(x + 1, y + 1, 3, 1, l);
    if (top) {
      pixFill(x, y, 16, 4, RGB(168, 172, 182));
      pixFill(x, y, 16, 1, RGB(230, 232, 238));
      pixFill(x, y + 3, 16, 1, d);
      if ((tx & 1) == 0) {
        pixFill(x + 4, y - 4, 8, 4, RGB(148, 152, 164));
        pixFill(x + 4, y - 4, 8, 1, RGB(210, 214, 222));
      }
    }
    return;
  }
  pixFill(x, y, 16, 16, dirt);
  pixFill(x, y + 15, 16, 1, ink);
  pixFill(x + 15, y, 1, 16, dirtD);
  pixFill(x, y, 1, 16, RGB((C_GROUND >> 11) << 3, ((C_GROUND >> 5) & 0x3F) << 2, (C_GROUND & 0x1F) << 3));
  if (top) {
    pixFill(x, y, 16, 6, grass);
    pixFill(x, y, 16, 1, hi);
    pixFill(x, y + 1, 16, 1, RGB(220, 255, 210));
    for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
      pixFill(x + k * 4, y + 6, 3, 2, grass);
      pixFill(x + k * 4 + 1, y + 5, 1, 1, hi);
    }
    pixFill(x, y + 6, 16, 1, RGB(18, 92, 28));
    // little flower / pebble accents
    int acc = (tx * 13 + ty * 7 + (int)tAnim / 20) & 7;
    if (acc == 0) { pixFill(x + 6, y - 2, 2, 2, RGB(255, 80, 90)); pixFill(x + 7, y - 3, 1, 2, RGB(40, 140, 50)); }
    if (acc == 3) pixFill(x + 11, y + 2, 2, 1, RGB(255, 240, 120));
  } else {
    int hs = (tx * 7 + ty * 13) & 15;
    pixFill(x + 2 + (hs & 7), y + 4 + (hs & 3), 2, 2, dirtD);
    if (hs > 10) pixFill(x + 8, y + 10, 3, 2, RGB(90, 50, 24));
    if ((hs & 3) == 0) pixFill(x + 4, y + 8, 1, 1, RGB(210, 150, 80));
  }
}

static void drawPixBrick(int x, int y) {
  uint16_t a = C_BRICK, d = C_BRICKD, l = RGB(255, 220, 180);
  pixFill(x, y, 16, 16, a);
  pixFill(x, y + 7, 16, 1, d);
  pixFill(x, y + 15, 16, 1, d);
  pixFill(x + 7, y, 1, 7, d);
  pixFill(x + 3, y + 8, 1, 7, d);
  pixFill(x + 11, y + 8, 1, 7, d);
  pixFill(x, y, 16, 1, l);
  pixFill(x, y, 1, 16, l);
  pixFill(x + 15, y, 1, 16, d);
  pixFill(x + 1, y + 1, 5, 1, RGB(255, 235, 200));
  pixFill(x + 9, y + 9, 4, 1, RGB(255, 190, 140));
}

static void drawPixQ(int x, int y, int tx) {
  int pulse = (tAnim / 6 + tx) & 3;
