  uint16_t body = (pulse == 0) ? RGB(255, 210, 40) : ((pulse == 2) ? RGB(230, 160, 20) : C_QUES);
  uint16_t ink = RGB(120, 50, 0), lite = RGB(255, 250, 200);
  pixFill(x, y, 16, 16, body);
  pixFill(x, y, 16, 1, ink); pixFill(x, y + 15, 16, 1, ink);
  pixFill(x, y, 1, 16, ink); pixFill(x + 15, y, 1, 16, ink);
  pixFill(x + 1, y + 1, 14, 1, lite);
  pixFill(x + 1, y + 1, 1, 14, lite);
  // ?
  pixFill(x + 5, y + 3, 6, 2, ink);
  pixFill(x + 9, y + 5, 2, 3, ink);
  pixFill(x + 6, y + 8, 4, 2, ink);
  pixFill(x + 7, y + 11, 2, 2, ink);
  int sh = (tAnim + tx * 5) % 18;
  if (sh < 12) pixFill(x + 1 + sh, y + 2, 1, 12, lite);
}

static void drawPixUsed(int x, int y) {
  pixFill(x, y, 16, 16, RGB(120, 92, 60));
  pixFill(x, y, 16, 1, RGB(60, 40, 20)); pixFill(x, y + 15, 16, 1, RGB(60, 40, 20));
  pixFill(x, y, 1, 16, RGB(60, 40, 20)); pixFill(x + 15, y, 1, 16, RGB(60, 40, 20));
  pixFill(x + 6, y + 6, 4, 4, RGB(80, 60, 36));
}

static void drawPixCoin(int x, int y, int tx) {
  int ph = (tAnim / 3 + tx * 2) & 7;
  int w = (ph <= 3) ? (8 - ph * 2) : ((ph - 4) * 2 + 2);
  if (w < 2) w = 2;
  int bob = (int)(sinf((tAnim + tx * 9) * 0.18f) * 1.5f);
  int cx = x + 8 - w / 2;
  int cy = y + 3 + bob;
  pixFill(cx, cy, w, 10, RGB(255, 208, 32));
  if (w > 3) {
    pixFill(cx + w / 2, cy + 2, 1, 6, RGB(160, 100, 10));
    pixFill(cx + 1, cy + 1, 1, 2, RGB(255, 250, 180));
    pixFill(cx + w - 2, cy + 7, 1, 1, RGB(255, 240, 140));
  }
  if (((tAnim + tx) & 7) == 0) pixFill(cx + w / 2, cy - 1, 1, 1, TFT_WHITE);
}

static void drawPixPipe(int x, int y, char c) {
  bool left = (c == 'L' || c == 'l');
  bool cap  = (c == 'L' || c == 'R');
  uint16_t g = C_PIPE, gd = C_PIPED, gl = RGB(170, 255, 170);
  if (cap) {
    pixFill(x - (left ? 2 : 0), y, 18, 6, g);
    pixFill(x - (left ? 2 : 0), y, 18, 1, gd);
    pixFill(x - (left ? 2 : 0), y + 5, 18, 1, gd);
    pixFill(x + (left ? 3 : 2), y + 1, 2, 4, gl);
    pixFill(x, y + 6, 16, 10, g);
  } else {
    pixFill(x + (left ? 2 : 0), y, 14, 16, g);
  }
  if (left) {
    pixFill(x + 2, y, 1, 16, gd);
    pixFill(x + 5, y, 2, 16, gl);
  } else {
    pixFill(x + 13, y, 1, 16, gd);
    pixFill(x + 9, y, 2, 16, RGB(70, 170, 80));
  }
}

static void drawPixFlag(int x, int y, int tx, int ty) {
  pixFill(x + 7, y, 2, 16, RGB(240, 240, 245));
  pixFill(x + 9, y, 1, 16, RGB(140, 140, 150));
  if (tileAt(tx, ty - 1) != 'F') {
    int wav = (int)(sinf((tAnim + tx) * 0.35f) * 3);
    pixFill(x + 9, y + 2, 8 + wav, 2, RGB(30, 200, 70));
    pixFill(x + 9, y + 4, 10 + wav, 2, RGB(50, 230, 90));
    pixFill(x + 9, y + 6, 8 + wav, 2, RGB(30, 200, 70));
    pixFill(x + 9, y + 8, 6 + wav, 2, RGB(20, 160, 55));
    pixFill(x + 6, y + 1, 4, 4, RGB(255, 220, 40));
  }
}

static void drawPixSpike(int x, int y) {
  uint16_t a = RGB(210, 214, 222), d = RGB(90, 94, 110);
  for (int k = 0; k < 2; k++) {
    int bx = x + k * 8;
    pixFill(bx + 3, y + 4, 2, 12, a);
    pixFill(bx + 2, y + 7, 4, 9, a);
    pixFill(bx + 1, y + 11, 6, 5, a);
    pixFill(bx + 4, y + 4, 1, 12, d);
  }
}

void drawTile(char c, int x, int y, int tx, int ty) {
  switch (c) {
    case '#': drawPixGround(x, y, tx, ty, tileAt(tx, ty - 1) != '#'); break;
    case 'B': drawPixBrick(x, y); break;
    case 'S': {
      pixFill(x, y, 16, 16, C_STONE);
      pixFill(x, y, 16, 1, RGB(40, 40, 48));
      pixFill(x, y + 15, 16, 1, RGB(40, 40, 48));
      pixFill(x, y, 1, 16, RGB(40, 40, 48));
      pixFill(x + 15, y, 1, 16, RGB(40, 40, 48));
      pixFill(x + 2, y + 2, 12, 12, RGB(200, 204, 214));
      break;
    }
    case '?': drawPixQ(x, y, tx); break;
    case 'U': drawPixUsed(x, y); break;
    case 'L': case 'R': case 'l': case 'r': drawPixPipe(x, y, c); break;
    case 'c': drawPixCoin(x, y, tx); break;
    case 'F': drawPixFlag(x, y, tx, ty); break;
    case '^': drawPixSpike(x, y); break;
    case 'D':
      pixFill(x + 2, y, 12, 16, RGB(110, 60, 30));
      pixFill(x + 4, y + 4, 8, 8, RGB(24, 16, 36));
      pixFill(x + 11, y + 9, 2, 2, RGB(255, 220, 80));
      break;
    case 'H':
      pixFill(x + 7, y, 2, 16, RGB(180, 80, 255));
      if (tileAt(tx, ty - 1) != 'H') {
        int wav = (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.28f) * 2);
        pixFill(x + 9, y + 3, 7 + wav, 8, RGB(255, 80, 220));
        pixFill(x + 6, y + 1, 4, 4, RGB(255, 200, 80));
      }
      break;
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  POWER ICONS
// ============================================================
void pwrColors(int id, uint16_t &bg, uint16_t &fg) {
  bg = RGB(20, 22, 40); fg = TFT_WHITE;
  switch (id) {
    case PWR_FLY:    bg = RGB(180, 30, 30);  fg = RGB(255, 200, 40); break;
    case PWR_HEAL:   bg = RGB(20, 140, 50);  fg = RGB(180, 255, 190); break;
    case PWR_SHOCK:  bg = RGB(150, 120, 20); fg = RGB(255, 255, 80); break;
    case PWR_ROCKET: bg = RGB(120, 40, 20);  fg = RGB(255, 120, 40); break;
    case PWR_INVIS:  bg = RGB(20, 90, 40);   fg = RGB(80, 255, 120); break;
    case PWR_HULK:   bg = RGB(30, 110, 40);  fg = RGB(140, 240, 120); break;
    case PWR_STAR:   bg = RGB(140, 110, 10); fg = RGB(255, 240, 80); break;
    case PWR_FIRE:   bg = RGB(150, 40, 10);  fg = RGB(255, 190, 50); break;
    case PWR_FREEZE: bg = RGB(20, 80, 140);  fg = RGB(200, 245, 255); break;
    case PWR_SPRING: bg = RGB(20, 120, 110); fg = RGB(150, 255, 220); break;
    case PWR_CLONE:  bg = RGB(120, 35, 120); fg = RGB(255, 240, 255); break;
  }
}
template <typename Gfx>
void drawPwrGlyph(Gfx &g, int id, int x, int y, int s, uint16_t fg) {
  int m = s / 2;
  switch (id) {
    case PWR_FLY:   g.fillTriangle(x + 3, y + s - 3, x + m, y + 3, x + s - 3, y + s - 3, fg); g.fillRect(x + m - 1, y + s - 5, 3, 4, RGB(255, 255, 255)); break;
    case PWR_HEAL:  g.fillRect(x + m - 1, y + 3, 3, s - 6, fg); g.fillRect(x + 3, y + m - 1, s - 6, 3, fg); break;
    case PWR_SHOCK: g.drawCircle(x + m, y + m, s / 3, fg); g.drawCircle(x + m, y + m, s / 5, fg); break;
    case PWR_ROCKET:g.fillTriangle(x + m, y + 2, x + 3, y + s - 4, x + s - 3, y + s - 4, fg); g.fillRect(x + m - 1, y + s - 4, 3, 3, RGB(255, 255, 120)); break;
    case PWR_INVIS: g.fillCircle(x + m, y + m, s / 4, fg); g.drawCircle(x + m, y + m, s / 3 + 1, fg); break;
    case PWR_HULK:  g.fillRoundRect(x + 4, y + 4, s - 8, s - 6, 2, fg); g.fillRect(x + 2, y + m, 3, 4, fg); g.fillRect(x + s - 5, y + m, 3, 4, fg); break;
    case PWR_STAR:  g.fillTriangle(x + m, y + 2, x + 3, y + m, x + s - 3, y + m, fg); g.fillTriangle(x + m, y + s - 2, x + 3, y + m, x + s - 3, y + m, fg); break;
    case PWR_FIRE:  g.fillCircle(x + m, y + m + 2, s / 4 + 1, fg); g.fillTriangle(x + m - 3, y + m, x + m, y + 2, x + m + 3, y + m, fg); break;
    case PWR_FREEZE:g.drawLine(x + 3, y + m, x + s - 3, y + m, fg); g.drawLine(x + m, y + 3, x + m, y + s - 3, fg);
                    g.drawLine(x + 4, y + 4, x + s - 4, y + s - 4, fg); g.drawLine(x + s - 4, y + 4, x + 4, y + s - 4, fg); break;
    case PWR_CLONE:  g.fillCircle(x + 5, y + m, 3, fg); g.fillCircle(x + s - 5, y + m, 3, fg); g.fillCircle(x + m, y + 5, 3, fg); g.fillCircle(x + m, y + s - 5, 3, fg); g.fillCircle(x + m, y + m, 3, RGB(255,120,220)); break;
    default:        g.drawLine(x + 3, y + m + 3, x + m, y + 3, fg); g.drawLine(x + m, y + 3, x + s - 3, y + m + 3, fg);
                    g.drawLine(x + 3, y + s - 4, x + m, y + m, fg); g.drawLine(x + m, y + m, x + s - 3, y + s - 4, fg); break;
  }
}
void drawPwrIcon(int id, int x, int y, bool big) {
  int s = big ? 18 : 12;
  uint16_t bg, fg; pwrColors(id, bg, fg);
  spr.fillRoundRect(x, y, s, s, 3, bg);
  spr.drawRoundRect(x, y, s, s, 3, fg);
  drawPwrGlyph(spr, id, x, y, s, fg);
}
void drawPwrIconTft(int id, int x, int y, bool big) {
  int s = big ? 18 : 12;
  uint16_t bg, fg; pwrColors(id, bg, fg);
  tft.fillRoundRect(x, y, s, s, 3, bg);
  tft.drawRoundRect(x, y, s, s, 3, fg);
  drawPwrGlyph(tft, id, x, y, s, fg);
}
void drawHeart(int x, int y, bool full) {
  uint16_t c = full ? RGB(240, 36, 56) : RGB(62, 28, 40);
  uint16_t edge = full ? RGB(120, 10, 24) : RGB(36, 16, 24);
  spr.fillCircle(x + 2, y + 2, 3, c);
  spr.fillCircle(x + 7, y + 2, 3, c);
  spr.fillTriangle(x - 1, y + 3, x + 10, y + 3, x + 4, y + 10, c);
  spr.drawLine(x - 1, y + 3, x + 4, y + 10, edge);
  spr.drawLine(x + 10, y + 3, x + 4, y + 10, edge);
  if (full) {
    spr.drawPixel(x + 1, y + 1, RGB(255, 200, 210));
    spr.drawPixel(x + 2, y + 1, TFT_WHITE);
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  BAND RENDER
// ============================================================
void renderBands(void (*fn)(int), uint16_t bg) {
  for (int y0 = 0; y0 < SCRH; y0 += BANDH) {
    spr.fillSprite(bg);
    fn(y0);
    spr.pushSprite(0, y0);
  }
}

void drawPlatBand(int y0) {
  drawBackdrop(y0);

  int tx0 = (camX < 0) ? -1 : camX / TS, tx1 = tx0 + SCRW / TS + 1;
  int ty0 = y0 / TS, ty1 = ty0 + BANDH / TS + 1;
  for (int ty = ty0; ty <= ty1; ty++) {
    if (ty < 0 || ty >= MAXH) continue;
    for (int tx = tx0; tx <= tx1; tx++) {
      if (tx < 0 || tx >= LW) continue;
      char c = lvl[ty][tx];
      if (c == ' ' || c == '.') continue;
      int off = (c == '?' || c == 'B' || c == 'U') ? bumpOffset(tx, ty) : 0;
      drawTile(c, tx * TS - camX, ty * TS - y0 - off, tx, ty);
    }
  }

  for (int i = 0; i < nPl; i++) {
    int pxp = (int)plats[i].x - camX, pyp = (int)plats[i].y - y0;
    if (pyp < -10 || pyp > BANDH + 8) continue;
    int bob = (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.12f + i) * 1.0f);
    pyp += bob;
    spr.fillRoundRect(pxp, pyp, TS * 2, 8, 2, RGB(180, 140, 70));
    spr.drawRoundRect(pxp, pyp, TS * 2, 8, 2, RGB(90, 60, 30));
    spr.drawFastHLine(pxp + 2, pyp + 2, TS * 2 - 4, RGB(230, 200, 120));
    if ((tAnim / 6 + i) & 1) spr.drawFastHLine(pxp + 5, pyp + 5, 6, RGB(245, 220, 150));
  }

  for (int i = 0; i < nEn; i++) {
    int ex = (int)en[i].x - camX, ey = (int)en[i].y - y0;
    if (ex < -20 || ex > SCRW + 6 || ey < -20 || ey > BANDH + 6) continue;
    int f2 = (en[i].vx < 0 || en[i].kind == EK_SHOOT) ? ((px < en[i].x) ? -1 : 1) : 1;
    if (en[i].kind != EK_SHOOT) f2 = (en[i].vx < 0) ? -1 : 1;
    if (en[i].alive) drawEnemy(ex, ey, false, en[i].kind, en[i].hp, tAnim + i * 5, freezeT > 0, f2, en[i].shoot, en[i].cd > 12);
    else if (en[i].squish) drawEnemy(ex, ey, true, en[i].kind, 0, 0, false, 1, 99, false);
  }

  // enemy bullets
  for (auto &b : ebul) if (b.alive) {
    int bx = (int)b.x - camX, by = (int)b.y - y0;
    if (by < -6 || by > BANDH + 6) continue;
    if (b.kind == 1) {
      int rr = 4 + ((tAnim + (int)b.x) & 1);
      spr.drawCircle(bx, by, rr + 2, RGB(110, 170, 230));
      spr.fillCircle(bx, by, rr, RGB(230, 245, 255));
      spr.fillCircle(bx - (b.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1), by - 1, 2, TFT_WHITE);
    } else {
      int rr = 4 + ((tAnim / 2) & 1);
      spr.drawCircle(bx, by, rr + 2, RGB(255, 100, 30));
      spr.fillCircle(bx, by, rr, RGB(255, 90, 30));
      spr.fillCircle(bx, by, 2, RGB(255, 235, 120));
      spr.drawFastHLine(bx - (b.vx > 0 ? 10 : -5), by, 5, RGB(255, 180, 60));
    }
  }
  // player fireballs — SNES-style bouncing fire
  for (auto &f : pfire) if (f.alive) {
    int fx = (int)f.x - camX, fy = (int)f.y - y0;
    if (fy < -8 || fy > BANDH + 8) continue;
    int dir = (f.vx >= 0) ? 1 : -1;
    int rr = 5 + ((tAnim / 2) & 1);
    spr.fillCircle(fx - dir * 7, fy + 1, 3, RGB(255, 90, 16));
    spr.fillCircle(fx - dir * 4, fy, 4, RGB(255, 140, 24));
    spr.fillCircle(fx, fy, rr, RGB(255, 168, 28));
    spr.fillCircle(fx + dir, fy - 1, 3, RGB(255, 220, 80));
    spr.fillCircle(fx + dir, fy - 1, 1, RGB(255, 252, 210));
    if ((tAnim & 1) == 0) spr.fillCircle(fx - dir * 10, fy + ((tAnim>>1)&1), 2, RGB(255, 110, 20));
  }

  // shock dome
  if (shockLife > 0) {
    int sx = (int)shockX - camX, sy = (int)shockY - y0, r = (int)shockR;
    if (sy + r > -4 && sy - r < BANDH) {
      spr.drawCircle(sx, sy, r, RGB(255, 230, 80));
      if (r > 4) spr.drawCircle(sx, sy, r - 2, RGB(255, 250, 200));
      if (r > 8) spr.drawCircle(sx, sy, r - 5, RGB(255, 160, 40));
    }
  }
  // rocket
  if (rkState && rkState < 4) {
    int rx = (int)rkX - camX, ry = (int)rkY - y0;
    if (ry > -14 && ry < BANDH + 10) {
      int dir = (rkVx >= 0) ? 1 : -1;
      spr.fillTriangle(rx + 12 * dir, ry, rx - 4 * dir, ry + 6, rx - 4 * dir, ry - 6, RGB(215, 40, 30));
      spr.fillRoundRect(rx - 8, ry - 3, 12, 6, 2, RGB(205, 205, 215));
      spr.fillCircle(rx + 10 * dir, ry, 3, RGB(255, 225, 80));
      spr.fillCircle(rx - 9 * dir, ry, 2 + (tAnim & 1), RGB(255, 150, 40));
    }
  }
  if (boomLife > 0) {
    int bx = (int)boomX - camX, by = (int)boomY - y0, r = (int)boomR;
    if (by + r > -4 && by - r < BANDH) {
      spr.drawCircle(bx, by, r, RGB(255, 200, 60));
      if (r > 8) spr.drawCircle(bx, by, r - 6, RGB(255, 90, 30));
      if (r > 14) spr.fillCircle(bx, by, 4, RGB(255, 240, 180));
    }
  }

  drawBoss(y0);
  drawBowserWorld(y0);
  drawOrbs(y0);
  drawStartPortal(y0);

  // power pop-ups
  for (auto &p : pops) if (p.life) {
    if (p.id < 0) {
      int tx = (int)p.x - camX, ty = (int)p.y - y0 - 4;
      if (ty > -10 && ty < BANDH + 2 && (p.life > 8 || (p.life & 1))) {
        char nb[8]; snprintf(nb, sizeof(nb), "%d", p.val);
        spr.setTextColor(TFT_BLACK); spr.drawCentreString(nb, tx + 1, ty + 1, 1);
        spr.setTextColor(RGB(255, 245, 120)); spr.drawCentreString(nb, tx, ty, 1);
      }
      continue;
    }
    int bob = (int)(sinf((50 - p.life) * 0.22f) * 3.0f);
    int ix = (int)p.x - camX - 9, iy = (int)p.y - y0 - 9 + bob;
    if (iy > -20 && iy < BANDH + 4 && (p.life > 10 || (p.life & 1))) {
      drawPwrIcon(p.id, ix, iy, true);
      if ((p.life & 3) == 0) spr.drawCircle(ix + 9, iy + 9, 11, RGB(255, 240, 140));
    }
  }

  drawCloneMarios(y0,camX);
  drawFriendWorld(y0, camX);
  drawPlayer((int)px - camX, (int)py - y0);

  // iron-man suit parts converging on Mario
  if (flyPhase == 1) {
    int cx = (int)px - camX + PW / 2, cy = (int)py - y0 + PH / 2;
    int d = 30 - flyAnim; if (d < 0) d = 0;
    const int ox[4] = { -1, 1, -1, 1 }, oy[4] = { -1, -1, 1, 1 };
    for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
      int ax = cx + ox[k] * d - 3, ay = cy + oy[k] * d - 3;
      spr.fillRoundRect(ax, ay, 6, 6, 2, (k & 1) ? RGB(230, 175, 40) : RGB(210, 30, 30));
    }
    spr.drawCircle(cx, cy, d / 2 + 4, RGB(255, 230, 120));
  }

  fxWorld(camX, y0);
  drawAmbient(y0);
  if (smwCastleLook()) drawSmwLava(y0);

  // ---------- HUD (Super Mario World layout) ----------
  if (y0 == 0) {
    char buf[24];
    // left: Mario icon + name + score
    spr.fillCircle(12, 12, 10, RGB(16, 20, 40));
    mR = RGB(224, 36, 28); mU = RGB(36, 70, 224); mS = RGB(255, 204, 150);
    mB = RGB(110, 58, 20); mY = RGB(255, 220, 40); mK = RGB(0, 0, 0); mW = RGB(255, 255, 255);
    drawMarioG(spr, 4, 2, 16, 20, 0, false, BANDH);
    spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
    spr.drawString("HP", 26, 1, 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < HPMAX; i++) drawHeart(42 + i * 12, 0, i < hp);
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%d/%d", hp, HPMAX);
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(255, 220, 80));
    spr.drawString(buf, 80, 1, 1);
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%06d", score);
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(255, 255, 255));
    spr.drawString(buf, 26, 14, 2);
    // coins
    spr.fillCircle(112, 10, 5, RGB(255, 210, 40));
    spr.fillCircle(112, 10, 3, RGB(255, 240, 120));
    spr.drawFastVLine(112, 6, 8, RGB(180, 120, 20));
    spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "x%02d", coins);
    spr.drawString(buf, 122, 4, 2);
    // world
    spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
    spr.drawCentreString("WORLD", 176, 1, 1);
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%d-%d", inChapter2 ? 2 : 1, constrain(worldLevelShown, 1, 9));
    spr.drawCentreString(buf, 176, 14, 2);
    // time left
    spr.drawCentreString("TIME", 230, 1, 1);
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%03d", levelClock);
    spr.setTextColor(levelClock <= 30 ? RGB(255, 80, 70) : TFT_WHITE);
    spr.drawCentreString(buf, 230, 14, 2);
    // lives left
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(220, 230, 255));
    snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "LIFE x%d", lives);
    spr.drawString(buf, 26, 30, 1);
    if (boss.active && boss.hpMax > 0) {
      spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
      spr.drawString("UFO", 258, 2, 1);
      spr.fillRect(258, 14, 56, 8, RGB(20, 16, 24));
      spr.drawRect(258, 14, 56, 8, RGB(230, 230, 240));
      int fw = 52 * boss.hp / boss.hpMax;
      spr.fillRect(260, 16, fw, 4, RGB(255, 70, 50));
    }
    if (bw.st != BW_OFF && bw.st < BW_TALK) {
      spr.fillCircle(302, 10, 9, RGB(40, 90, 40));
      spr.fillCircle(302, 8, 5, RGB(220, 140, 70));
      spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
      spr.drawString("BOWSER", 258, 2, 1);
      spr.fillRect(258, 14, 56, 8, RGB(20, 16, 24));
      spr.drawRect(258, 14, 56, 8, RGB(255, 220, 80));
      int fw = (bw.hpMax > 0) ? (52 * bw.hp / bw.hpMax) : 0;
      spr.fillRect(260, 16, fw, 4, RGB(230, 40, 36));
    }
    if (msgT > 0) {
      spr.setTextColor(RGB(255, 240, 100));
      spr.drawCentreString(msgTxt, 160, 36, 1);
    }
  }
  // level banner
  if (y0 == 48 && bannerT > 0) {
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(20, 20, 30));
    spr.drawCentreString(lvName, 162, 12, 4);
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(255, 235, 90));
    spr.drawCentreString(lvName, 160, 10, 4);
  }
  // inventory always bottom-left: larger icons + remaining uses
  if (y0 == 192) {
    int nP = 0; for (int i = 0; i < PWR_N; i++) if (hasP(i)) nP++;
    if (nP > 0) {
      int boxW = 8 + nP * 20;
      spr.fillRoundRect(1, 26, boxW, 32, 5, RGB(8, 8, 16));
      spr.drawRoundRect(1, 26, boxW, 32, 5, RGB(80, 110, 180));
      int ox = 4;
      for (int i = 0; i < PWR_N; i++) if (hasP(i)) {
        drawPwrIcon(i, ox, 32, true);
        if (onP(i)) spr.drawRect(ox - 1, 31, 20, 20, TFT_WHITE);
        char b[4]; snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "%d", pwrCnt[i]);
        spr.setTextColor(TFT_BLACK); spr.drawString(b, ox + 13, 44, 1);
        spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); spr.drawString(b, ox + 12, 43, 1);
        ox += 20;
      }
    }
  }
  if (fxFlash > 0) {   // scanline flash
    int st = fxFlash > 24 ? 2 : (fxFlash > 10 ? 3 : 5);
    for (int yy = (int)(tAnim & 1); yy < BANDH; yy += st) spr.drawFastHLine(0, yy, SCRW, RGB(255, 255, 235));
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  ABILITY WHEEL (SELECT)
// ============================================================
int wheelIndexOf(int nth) {
  int k = 0;
  for (int i = 0; i < PWR_N; i++) if (hasP(i)) { if (k == nth) return i; k++; }
  return -1;
}
int wheelCount() {
  int k = 0;
  for (int i = 0; i < PWR_N; i++) if (hasP(i)) k++;
  return k;
}
void drawWheel(int selNth) {
  tft.fillRect(30, 24, 260, 192, RGB(6, 8, 20));
  tft.drawRoundRect(30, 24, 260, 192, 12, RGB(80, 120, 220));
  tft.drawRoundRect(32, 26, 256, 188, 10, RGB(24, 34, 70));
  tft.setTextColor(TFT_YELLOW);
  tft.drawCentreString("ABILITIES  (1 use each)", 160, 32, 2);
  tft.setTextColor(RGB(160, 170, 200));
  tft.drawCentreString("LEFT/RIGHT  A=USE  B=CLOSE", 160, 196, 1);
  int n = wheelCount();
  if (!n) { tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString("No powers - hit ? blocks", 160, 110, 2); return; }
  int cx = 160, cy = 118, rad = 60;
  for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    int id = wheelIndexOf(i);
    float a = -1.5708f + (6.2832f * i) / n;
    int x = cx + (int)(cosf(a) * rad) - 12;
    int y = cy + (int)(sinf(a) * rad) - 12;
    bool sel = (i == selNth);
    int pulse = sel ? (int)(sinf(millis() * 0.012f) * 2.0f) : 0;
    tft.fillCircle(x + 12, y + 12, sel ? 17 + pulse : 14, sel ? RGB(255, 220, 60) : RGB(30, 40, 70));
    if (sel) tft.drawCircle(x + 12, y + 12, 20 + pulse, RGB(255, 240, 150));
    drawPwrIconTft(id, x + 3, y + 3, true);
    char b[4]; snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "%d", pwrCnt[id]);
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE, sel ? RGB(255, 220, 60) : RGB(30, 40, 70));
    tft.drawString(b, x + 20, y + 20, 1);
  }
  int id = wheelIndexOf(selNth);
  if (id >= 0) {
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE, RGB(6, 8, 20));
    tft.drawCentreString(PWR_NAME[id], 160, 112, 2);
    char b[12]; snprintf(b, sizeof(b), "x%d left", pwrCnt[id]);
    tft.setTextColor(RGB(160, 180, 230), RGB(6, 8, 20));
    tft.drawCentreString(b, 160, 134, 1);
  }
}
int openWheel() {
  int n = wheelCount();
  int sel = 0;
  drawWheel(sel);
  // Let the opening SELECT press leave the input buffer before reading the wheel.
  delay(90);
  readInput();
  nextFrame = millis();
  while (true) {
    frameWait(40);
    readInput();
    if (E & BTN_LEFT)  { if (n) sel = (sel + n - 1) % n; drawWheel(sel); }
    if (E & BTN_RIGHT) { if (n) sel = (sel + 1) % n; drawWheel(sel); }
    if (E & (BTN_B | BTN_START)) return -1;
    if (E & (BTN_A | BTN_SELECT)) { if (!n) return -1; return wheelIndexOf(sel); }
  }
}

// ============================================================
//  PORTAL CUTSCENE  (~5 seconds, Mario front and center)
// ============================================================
int   csT = 0;
float csMx = 36.0f, csBob = 0;
float csScale = 1.0f;
float csUx = 48.0f, csUy = 150.0f;
bool  csFinalGate = false;
uint8_t csTheme = 0;
char  csCaption[28];
char  csTitle[18];
uint16_t csP1, csP2, csP3, csCore;

static float clerp(float a, float b, float t) {
  if (t < 0) t = 0; if (t > 1) t = 1;
  return a + (b - a) * t;
}
static float cease(float t) {
  if (t < 0) t = 0; if (t > 1) t = 1;
  return t * t * (3.0f - 2.0f * t);
}

static void csPickPalette() {
  switch (csTheme) {
    case TH_WINTER:  csP1 = RGB(160, 230, 255); csP2 = RGB(230, 250, 255); csP3 = RGB(80, 170, 230);  csCore = RGB(200, 240, 255); break;
    case TH_VOLCANO: csP1 = RGB(255, 90, 30);   csP2 = RGB(255, 210, 70);  csP3 = RGB(180, 30, 10);   csCore = RGB(255, 160, 40);  break;
    case TH_CASTLE:  csP1 = RGB(190, 90, 255);  csP2 = RGB(255, 200, 90);  csP3 = RGB(90, 40, 140);   csCore = RGB(230, 180, 255); break;
    case TH_DESERT:  csP1 = RGB(255, 190, 50);  csP2 = RGB(255, 240, 160); csP3 = RGB(200, 110, 30);  csCore = RGB(255, 220, 90);  break;
    case TH_NIGHT:   csP1 = RGB(150, 90, 255);  csP2 = RGB(90, 255, 180);  csP3 = RGB(40, 20, 90);    csCore = RGB(200, 160, 255); break;
    case TH_JUNGLE:  csP1 = RGB(70, 230, 80);   csP2 = RGB(220, 255, 90);  csP3 = RGB(20, 110, 40);   csCore = RGB(160, 255, 140); break;
    case TH_SKY:     csP1 = RGB(90, 200, 255);  csP2 = RGB(255, 255, 255); csP3 = RGB(40, 110, 210);  csCore = RGB(180, 230, 255); break;
    case TH_CAVE:    csP1 = RGB(70, 240, 220);  csP2 = RGB(180, 120, 255); csP3 = RGB(20, 50, 70);    csCore = RGB(140, 255, 230); break;
    case TH_SPACE:   csP1 = RGB(255, 80, 200);  csP2 = RGB(80, 255, 230);  csP3 = RGB(40, 10, 80);    csCore = RGB(255, 180, 255); break;
    default:         csP1 = RGB(80, 220, 70);   csP2 = RGB(255, 230, 80);  csP3 = RGB(30, 130, 50);   csCore = RGB(180, 255, 140); break;
  }
  if (csFinalGate) { csP1 = RGB(255, 70, 40); csP2 = RGB(255, 220, 80); csP3 = RGB(120, 20, 20); csCore = RGB(255, 170, 60); }
}

void drawPortalFX(int cx, int cy, int y0, float open, float suck) {
  int px_ = cx, py_ = cy - y0;
  int R = (int)(42 * open);
  if (R < 2) return;
  int spin = (csTheme == TH_VOLCANO || csTheme == TH_SPACE) ? 18 : 11;
  for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
    int rr = R + 10 - i * 2 + (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.18f + i) * 2);
    spr.drawCircle(px_, py_, rr, (i & 1) ? csP1 : csP2);
  }
  spr.fillCircle(px_, py_, (int)(R * 0.62f), csP3);
  spr.fillCircle(px_, py_, (int)(R * 0.38f), RGB(8, 6, 20));
  int n = (csTheme == TH_CASTLE || csTheme == TH_CAVE) ? 8 : 10;
  for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    float a = tAnim * (spin * 0.01f) + i * (6.28318f / n);
    int x1 = px_ + (int)(cosf(a) * R * 0.22f);
    int y1 = py_ + (int)(sinf(a) * R * 0.22f);
    int x2 = px_ + (int)(cosf(a) * R * 0.92f);
    int y2 = py_ + (int)(sinf(a) * R * 0.92f);
    spr.drawLine(x1, y1, x2, y2, csP2);
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < 14; i++) {
    float a = tAnim * 0.16f + i * 0.45f;
    float rad = R * (0.55f + 0.38f * sinf(tAnim * 0.07f + i));
    int ox = px_ + (int)(cosf(a) * rad);
    int oy = py_ + (int)(sinf(a) * rad * 0.72f);
    spr.fillCircle(ox, oy, (i & 1) ? 2 : 1, (i & 1) ? csP2 : csP1);
  }
  int cr = 4 + (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.3f) * 2) + (int)(suck * 6);
  spr.fillCircle(px_, py_, cr + 3, csCore);
  spr.fillCircle(px_, py_, cr, TFT_WHITE);
}

void drawCutsceneWorld(int y0) {
#define LYC(v) ((v) - y0)
  // sky wash
  for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
    int gy = y0 + y;
    uint8_t r, g, b;
    switch (csTheme) {
      case TH_WINTER:  r = 150 + gy / 8; g = 190 + gy / 12; b = 230; break;
      case TH_VOLCANO: r = 48 + gy / 6;  g = 12 + gy / 20;  b = 10;  break;
      case TH_CASTLE:  r = 28 + gy / 16; g = 16 + gy / 22;  b = 48 + gy / 10; break;
      case TH_DESERT:  r = 245; g = 190 + gy / 10; b = 90 + gy / 8; break;
      case TH_NIGHT:   r = 8; g = 10; b = 34 + gy / 12; break;
      case TH_JUNGLE:  r = 50 + gy / 14; g = 140 + gy / 10; b = 180; break;
      case TH_SKY:     r = 100 + gy / 12; g = 180; b = 255; break;
      case TH_CAVE:    r = 16; g = 12; b = 26 + gy / 14; break;
      case TH_SPACE:   r = 8; g = 4; b = 24 + gy / 16; break;
      default:         r = 90 + gy / 10; g = 150 + gy / 12; b = 250; break;
    }
    spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(r, g, b));
  }

  // theme props
  if (csTheme == TH_WINTER) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
      int sx = ((i * 53 + (int)tAnim * 2) % 340) - 10;
      int sy = (i * 29 + (int)tAnim * (1 + i % 3)) % 210;
      int yy = LYC(sy);
      if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH) spr.fillRect(sx, yy, 2, 2, TFT_WHITE);
    }
    spr.fillCircle(40, LYC(250), 90, RGB(210, 230, 250));
    spr.fillCircle(170, LYC(268), 110, RGB(230, 240, 255));
    spr.fillCircle(290, LYC(248), 95, RGB(200, 220, 245));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 50, RGB(230, 240, 250));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 6, TFT_WHITE);
  } else if (csTheme == TH_DESERT) {
    spr.fillCircle(40, LYC(250), 90, RGB(214, 160, 70));
    spr.fillCircle(170, LYC(268), 120, RGB(232, 186, 96));
    spr.fillCircle(290, LYC(248), 100, RGB(200, 140, 56));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 50, RGB(222, 178, 88));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 5, RGB(255, 220, 140));
    spr.fillCircle(270, LYC(46), 18, RGB(255, 220, 90));
  } else if (csTheme == TH_VOLCANO) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 18; i++) {
      int sx = ((i * 41 + (int)tAnim) % 330);
      int sy = 220 - ((int)tAnim * (1 + i % 3) + i * 17) % 220;
      int yy = LYC(sy);
      if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH) spr.fillRect(sx, yy, 2, 2, (i & 1) ? RGB(255, 140, 30) : RGB(255, 220, 80));
    }
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 50, RGB(60, 22, 16));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 5, RGB(255, 90, 30));
    spr.drawFastHLine(0, LYC(202), SCRW, RGB(255, 170, 40));
  } else if (csTheme == TH_CASTLE) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      int cx = 20 + i * 70;
      spr.fillRect(cx, LYC(70), 36, 130, RGB(48, 36, 70));
      spr.fillRect(cx + 8, LYC(90), 8, 14, RGB(255, 170, 60));
      spr.fillRect(cx + 20, LYC(120), 8, 14, RGB(255, 140, 40));
    }
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 50, RGB(40, 30, 54));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 5, RGB(90, 70, 120));
  } else if (csTheme == TH_NIGHT) {
    spr.fillCircle(270, LYC(44), 16, RGB(240, 240, 210));
    spr.fillCircle(264, LYC(40), 10, RGB(8, 10, 34));
    for (int i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
      int sx = (i * 47) % 320, sy = (i * 31) % 160;
      int yy = LYC(sy);
      if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH) spr.drawPixel(sx, yy, RGB(220, 230, 255));
    }
    spr.fillCircle(40, LYC(250), 90, RGB(18, 40, 36));
    spr.fillCircle(180, LYC(268), 110, RGB(14, 32, 30));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 50, RGB(22, 28, 48));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 5, RGB(40, 80, 70));
  } else if (csTheme == TH_JUNGLE) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
      int cx = 10 + i * 48;
      spr.fillRect(cx + 10, LYC(80), 8, 120, RGB(70, 42, 18));
      spr.fillCircle(cx + 14, LYC(78), 22, RGB(30, 130, 40));
      spr.fillCircle(cx, LYC(96), 16, RGB(40, 150, 50));
    }
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 50, RGB(70, 44, 20));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 6, RGB(40, 160, 50));
  } else if (csTheme == TH_SKY) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      int cx = ((i * 110 - (int)tAnim) % 420 + 420) % 420 - 50;
      int cy = 50 + (i * 23) % 70;
      int yy = LYC(cy);
      if (yy > -16 && yy < BANDH + 16) {
        spr.fillCircle(cx, yy, 10, TFT_WHITE);
        spr.fillCircle(cx + 14, yy + 3, 8, TFT_WHITE);
        spr.fillCircle(cx - 12, yy + 4, 7, TFT_WHITE);
        spr.fillRect(cx - 12, yy + 4, 28, 8, TFT_WHITE);
      }
    }
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 50, RGB(230, 240, 255));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 6, TFT_WHITE);
  } else if (csTheme == TH_CAVE) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
      int cx = 16 + i * 40;
      int h = 20 + (i * 13) % 28;
      spr.fillTriangle(cx - 6, LYC(0), cx, LYC(h), cx + 6, LYC(0), RGB(90, 80, 120));
      spr.fillTriangle(cx - 5, LYC(240), cx, LYC(240 - h), cx + 5, LYC(240), RGB(60, 200, 190));
    }
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 50, RGB(36, 28, 44));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 5, RGB(70, 150, 140));
  } else if (csTheme == TH_SPACE) {
    for (int i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
      int sx = (i * 47 + (int)tAnim / 2) % 320;
      int sy = (i * 37 + i * i) % 190;
      int yy = LYC(sy);
      if (yy >= 0 && yy < BANDH) spr.fillRect(sx, yy, (i & 3) ? 1 : 2, 1, RGB(220, 230, 255));
    }
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 50, RGB(30, 24, 60));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 5, RGB(130, 100, 210));
    spr.drawFastHLine(0, LYC(202), SCRW, RGB(180, 160, 255));
  } else { // grass
    spr.fillCircle(40, LYC(250), 90, RGB(90, 185, 110));
    spr.fillCircle(170, LYC(268), 110, RGB(70, 165, 90));
    spr.fillCircle(290, LYC(248), 95, RGB(100, 195, 120));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 50, RGB(196, 120, 56));
    spr.fillRect(0, LYC(196), SCRW, 6, RGB(64, 184, 60));
    spr.drawFastHLine(0, LYC(202), SCRW, RGB(140, 235, 120));
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
    int sx = 18 + i * 40 + ((int)tAnim / 2 + i * 11) % 6;
    spr.fillRoundRect(sx, LYC(204), 9 + (i & 3), 3, 1, RGB(20, 16, 28));
  }
#undef LYC
}

// Compact UFO renderer used by portal/cutscene scenes.
// Drawn directly into the shared sprite so it works with the current 320x60 band.
static void drawUFOAt(int x, int y) {
  const uint16_t dark = RGB(18, 22, 35);
  const uint16_t hull = RGB(115, 130, 155);
  const uint16_t hull2 = RGB(175, 190, 215);
  const uint16_t glass = RGB(80, 205, 235);
  const uint16_t glow = RGB(120, 235, 255);
  spr.fillEllipse(x + 20, y + 16, 22, 7, dark);
  spr.fillEllipse(x + 20, y + 14, 20, 6, hull);
  spr.fillEllipse(x + 20, y + 12, 11, 5, glass);
  spr.fillRect(x + 5, y + 13, 30, 4, hull2);
  spr.drawFastHLine(x + 7, y + 17, 26, dark);
  for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
    int lx = x + 9 + i * 6;
    spr.fillCircle(lx, y + 19, 2, ((tAnim / 4 + i) & 1) ? glow : RGB(50, 130, 180));
  }
}

void drawCutsceneBand(int y0) {
#define LYC(v) ((v) - y0)
  drawCutsceneWorld(y0);

  float open = cease((csT - 4) / 22.0f);
  float suck = (csT > 95) ? cease((csT - 95) / 40.0f) : 0.0f;
  int pcx = 236, pcy = 148;
  drawPortalFX(pcx, pcy, y0, open, suck);

  if (open > 0.3f) {
    int by = LYC(196);
    if (by >= 0 && by < BANDH) spr.drawFastHLine(pcx - 50, by, 100, csP2);
  }

  if (sv.ufoOwned) {
    int ux = (int)csUx, uy = LYC((int)csUy);
    drawUFOAt(ux, uy);
    setMarioPalette();
    // Special boss arrival: Mario actually climbs out and walks to the portal,
    // while the captured UFO stays parked at the arena.
    if (!strncmp(csTitle, "UFO LANDING", 11)) {
      int mw = 24, mh = 34;
      int mx = (int)csMx - mw / 2;
      int my = 148 - mh;
      drawMarioG(spr, mx, LYC(my), mw, mh, (csT / 5) & 1 ? 2 : 1, false, BANDH);
      if (csT >= 55) {
        spr.drawLine(ux + 20, uy + 22, ux + 20, LYC(148), RGB(120, 230, 255));
        spr.drawCircle(ux + 20, LYC(148), 5 + (tAnim & 2), RGB(80, 220, 255));
      }
    } else {
      // Normal portal transition: Mario rides inside his UFO.
      int mw = 12, mh = 18;
      drawMarioG(spr, ux + 16, uy + 2, mw, mh, 3, false, BANDH);
    }
    if (suck > 0.05f) {
      for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        float a = tAnim * 0.22f + i * 0.63f;
        int x1 = ux + 22 + (int)(cosf(a) * 24 * (1.0f - suck));
        int y1 = LYC((int)csUy + 15 + (int)(sinf(a) * 14 * (1.0f - suck)));
        spr.drawLine(x1, y1, pcx, LYC(pcy), csP2);
      }
    }
  } else {
    setMarioPalette();
    mR = RGB(224, 36, 28); mU = RGB(36, 70, 224); mS = RGB(255, 204, 150);
    mB = RGB(110, 58, 20); mY = RGB(255, 220, 40); mK = RGB(0, 0, 0); mW = RGB(255, 255, 255);
    int dw = (int)(52 * csScale);
    int dh = (int)(70 * csScale);
    if (dw < 4) dw = 4;
    if (dh < 6) dh = 6;
    int mx = (int)csMx - dw / 2;
    int my = 196 - dh - (int)csBob;
    int walkSp = 5;
    if (csTheme == TH_CASTLE || csTheme == TH_WINTER) walkSp = 7;
    if (csTheme == TH_VOLCANO || csTheme == TH_JUNGLE) walkSp = 4;
    int fr;
    if (csT < 18) fr = 0;
    else if (csT < 96) fr = 1 + ((csT / walkSp) & 1);
    else fr = 3;
    if (csTheme == TH_SKY && csT >= 18 && csT < 96) fr = 3;
    int shY = LYC(194);
    if (shY >= 0 && shY < BANDH && csScale > 0.15f)
      spr.fillCircle((int)csMx, shY, (int)(6 * csScale) + 2, RGB(8, 6, 16));
    if (!(csTheme == TH_NIGHT && ((csT / 6) & 1) && csT > 20 && csT < 96))
      drawMarioG(spr, mx, LYC(my), dw, dh, fr, false, BANDH);

    if (suck > 0.05f) {
      for (int i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
        float a = tAnim * 0.2f + i * 0.8f;
        int x1 = (int)csMx + (int)(cosf(a) * 20 * (1.0f - suck));
        int y1 = LYC(my + dh / 2 + (int)(sinf(a) * 16 * (1.0f - suck)));
        spr.drawLine(x1, y1, pcx, LYC(pcy), csP2);
      }
    }
  }

  if (y0 == 0) {
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(10, 8, 24));
    spr.drawCentreString(csTitle, 161, 9, 4);
    spr.setTextColor(csP2);
    spr.drawCentreString(csTitle, 160, 8, 4);
  }
  if (y0 == 192) {
    spr.setTextColor(csP1);
    spr.drawCentreString(csCaption, 160, 22, 2);
    if ((csT / 12) & 1) {
      spr.setTextColor(RGB(180, 180, 190));
      spr.drawCentreString("A / START skip", 160, 38, 1);
    }
  }
#undef LYC
}

void playPortalCutscene(bool finalGate, const char *caption) {
  csT = 0;
  csMx = 48.0f;
  csUx = 48.0f; csUy = 150.0f;
  csBob = 0;
  csScale = 1.0f;
  csFinalGate = finalGate;
  csTheme = lvTheme;
  csPickPalette();
  strncpy(csCaption, caption ? caption : "", 27);
  csCaption[27] = 0;
  if (finalGate) strncpy(csTitle, "FINAL GATE", 17);
  else {
    switch (csTheme) {
      case TH_WINTER:  strncpy(csTitle, "ICE GATE", 17); break;
      case TH_VOLCANO: strncpy(csTitle, "LAVA GATE", 17); break;
      case TH_CASTLE:  strncpy(csTitle, "DARK GATE", 17); break;
      case TH_DESERT:  strncpy(csTitle, "SAND GATE", 17); break;
      case TH_NIGHT:   strncpy(csTitle, "NIGHT GATE", 17); break;
      case TH_JUNGLE:  strncpy(csTitle, "LEAF GATE", 17); break;
      case TH_SKY:     strncpy(csTitle, "SKY GATE", 17); break;
      case TH_CAVE:    strncpy(csTitle, "CRYSTAL GATE", 17); break;
      case TH_SPACE:   strncpy(csTitle, "STAR GATE", 17); break;
      default:         strncpy(csTitle, "PIPE GATE", 17); break;
    }
  }
  csTitle[17] = 0;
  fxClear();
  tAnim = 0;
  nextFrame = millis();
  readInput();
  while (csT < 156) {
    frameWait(33);
    readInput();
    if (csT > 18 && (E & (BTN_A | BTN_B | BTN_START | BTN_SELECT))) break;
    tAnim++;
    csT++;

    if (csT < 18) {
      csMx = 48.0f;
      csUx = 48.0f; csUy = 150.0f;
      csScale = clerp(0.82f, 1.0f, cease(csT / 18.0f));
    } else if (csT < 96) {
      float u = cease((csT - 18) / 78.0f);
      csMx = clerp(48.0f, 204.0f, u);
      csUx = clerp(48.0f, 190.0f, u);
      csUy = 150.0f + sinf(csT * 0.16f) * 7.0f;
      csScale = 1.0f;
      if (csTheme == TH_SKY)       csBob = 10.0f + sinf(csT * 0.22f) * 8.0f;
      else if (csTheme == TH_SPACE) csBob = 6.0f + sinf(csT * 0.16f) * 6.0f;
      else if (csTheme == TH_JUNGLE) csBob = fabsf(sinf(csT * 0.35f)) * 10.0f;
      else if (csTheme == TH_WINTER) csBob = sinf(csT * 0.4f) * 2.0f;
      else csBob = 0;
      if ((csT % 6) == 0) fxDust(csMx - 8, 196);
    } else {
      float u = cease((csT - 96) / 48.0f);
      csMx = clerp(204.0f, 236.0f, u);
      csUx = clerp(190.0f, 236.0f, u);
      csUy = clerp(150.0f, 148.0f, u);
      csScale = clerp(1.0f, 0.08f, u);
      csBob += (18.0f - csBob) * 0.12f;
      if ((csT % 3) == 0) {
        fxSpark(236, 148, csP2, 3);
        fxBurst(236, 148, csP1, 2);
      }
    }
    fxUpdate();
    renderBands(drawCutsceneBand, themeSky(csTheme));
  }
  for (int k = 0; k < 10; k++) {
    int m = 12 + k * 12;
    tft.fillRect(0, 0, 320, m, TFT_BLACK);
    tft.fillRect(0, 240 - m, 320, m, TFT_BLACK);
    tft.fillRect(0, 0, m, 240, TFT_BLACK);
    tft.fillRect(320 - m, 0, m, 240, TFT_BLACK);
    delay(22);
  }
  tft.fillScreen(TFT_BLACK);
  delay(80);
}

// ============================================================
//  LEVEL LOOP    returns 0 = menu, 1 = died, 2 = complete
// ============================================================
void updateWin() {
  if (undergroundActive && ugFade >= 255) {
    // Keep the camera inside the tunnel during the elevator exit instead of
    // running surface collision against the procedural underground coordinates.
    fxRing(px + PW/2, py + PH/2, RGB(120,220,255), 8, 1.8f);
    if ((winT & 3) == 0) fxSpark(px + PW/2, py + PH/2, RGB(220,250,255), 4);
    return;
  }
  if (winT == 1) {
    pwrResetRun();
    if (PW != 12) { PW = 12; PH = 15; py += 9; }
    unstick();
    for (auto &o : orbs) o.alive = false;
    for (auto &b : ebul) b.alive = false;
  }
  face = 1; vx = 1.2f; vy += 0.5f; if (vy > 8) vy = 8;
  onGround = false;
  movePX(vx); movePY(vy);
  animT += 1.2f;
  if (winT % 8 == 0) {
    static const uint16_t rb[5] = { RGB(255,80,80), RGB(255,220,60), RGB(80,255,120), RGB(90,170,255), RGB(230,110,255) };
    float fx = px + random(-40, 41), fy = py - random(10, 60);
    fxBurst(fx, fy, rb[random(5)], 10); fxRing(fx, fy, rb[random(5)], 8);
  }
}

int playLevel() {
  sfxGameStart();
  nEn = 0; nPl = 0; ridePl = -1; secretExit = false; warpLock = 0;
  px = 32; py = 160; vx = vy = 0; face = 1;
  fxClear(); popsClear();
  for (auto &b : ebul) b.alive = false;
  for (auto &f : pfire) f.alive = false;
  for (auto &o : orbs) o.alive = false;
  memset(&boss, 0, sizeof(boss));
  memset(&bw, 0, sizeof(bw));
  bwClearMinions();
  onGround = false; dying = false; won = false;
  PW = 12; PH = 15; hp = HPMAX; coyote = jbuf = 0; animT = 0; rollT = flipT = actionT = 0; skidT = 0; msgT = 0; bannerT = 100;
  startPortalActive = false; startPortalClose = 0;
  undergroundActive=false; undergroundEntering=false; undergroundExiting=false; ugTimer=0; ugFade=0; nUGE=0; endPipeX=-1; pipeStandT=0;
  ugLiftIn = UG_CEIL + 22; ugLiftOut = UG_FLOOR;
  levelBossCourse=false;
  pwrResetRun();
  friendSpawn();
  levelClock = 300;
  if (worldLevelShown < 1) worldLevelShown = 1;
  for (int y = 0; y < MAXH; y++)
    for (int x = 0; x < LW; x++) {
      char c = lvl[y][x];
      if (c == '@') { px = x * TS + 2; py = y * TS + 1; lvl[y][x] = '.'; }
      else if (c == 'O') { spawnBoss(x * TS, 24); levelBossCourse=true; lvl[y][x] = '.'; }
      else if (c == 'W') { spawnBowser(x * TS, (MAXH - 2) * TS - 28); if (chapter2Level == 10) { bw.hpMax = 120; bw.hp = 120; bw.st = BW_CHASE; } lvl[y][x] = '.'; }
      else if (c == 'g' || c == 'k' || c == 'f' || c == 'e' || c == 'n' || c == 'a' || c == 't' || c == 'r' || c == 'j' || c == 'q') {
        spawnEnemy(c, x, y);
        lvl[y][x] = '.';
      } else if ((c == '=' || c == '~') && nPl < MAXPL) {
        MPlat &p = plats[nPl++];
        p.x = x * TS; p.y = y * TS + 8; p.axis = (c == '~') ? 1 : 0;
        p.spd = (c == '~') ? 0.7f : 0.9f;
        if (p.axis == 0) { p.minv = p.x - 48; p.maxv = p.x + 48; }
        else { p.minv = p.y - 40; p.maxv = p.y + 24; }
        lvl[y][x] = '.';
      }
    }
  installEndPipe();
  startPortalX = px + PW / 2;
  startPortalY = py + PH;
  bgSelect(lvTheme, boss.active);
  memcpy(lvOrig, lvl, sizeof(lvl));
  camF = constrain((int)(px + PW / 2) - SCRW / 2, 0, max(0, LW * TS - SCRW));

  bool paused = false;
  nextFrame = millis();
  readInput();
  playWorldCard(inChapter2 ? 2 : 1, worldLevelShown, lvName[0] ? lvName : "MARIO");
  while (true) {
    frameWait(33);
    readInput();
    buzzerUpdate();
    if (!paused && !dying && !won && (tAnim % 20) == 0 && levelClock > 0) levelClock--;

    if ((E & BTN_SELECT) && !dying && !won) {
      if (wheelCount() > 0) {
        int pick = openWheel();
        if (pick >= 0) activatePower(pick);
      } else setMsg("NO POWERS - HIT ?");
    }
    if (E & BTN_START) {
      paused = !paused;
      if (paused) {
        tft.fillRoundRect(70, 88, 180, 64, 10, RGB(8, 10, 24));
        tft.drawRoundRect(70, 88, 180, 64, 10, RGB(90, 140, 255));
        tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
        tft.drawCentreString("PAUSE", 160, 98, 4);
        tft.setTextColor(RGB(180, 190, 220));
        tft.drawCentreString("START = resume", 160, 130, 1);
      }
    }
    if (paused) continue;
    if (hitStop > 0) { hitStop--; if(undergroundActive) renderBands(drawUndergroundBand, RGB(5,5,9)); else renderBands(drawPlatBand, lvSky); continue; }

    tAnim++;
    if (bannerT > 0) bannerT--;
    fxUpdate();
    popsUpdate();
    updatePowers();
    updateFire();
    updateBoss();
    updateBowser();
    if(!undergroundActive) updateBullets();

    if (dying) {
      bgStop();
      py += vy; vy += 0.5f;
      if (++dyingT > 55) return 1;
    } else if (won) {
      bgStop();
      winT++;
      updateWin();
      if (winT > 95) return 2;
    } else if (undergroundActive) {
      updateUnderground();
    } else {
      updatePlayer();
      if (bw.st == BW_TALK || bw.st == BW_HOOK || bw.st == BW_CRASH) { vx = 0; if (vy < 0) vy = 0; }
      updatePlats();
      tryWarp();
      collectStuff();
      updateEnemies();
    }

    float target = undergroundActive ? ((px + PW / 2) - SCRW / 2) : ((px + PW / 2) - SCRW / 2 + face * 18);
    float maxCam = (float)max(0, LW * TS - SCRW);
    camF += (target - camF) * 0.18f;
    if (camF < 0) camF = 0;
    if (camF > maxCam) camF = maxCam;
    camX = (int)camF;
    if (shakeT > 0) camX += random(-2, 3);

    if(undergroundActive) renderBands(drawUndergroundBand, RGB(5,5,9)); else renderBands(drawPlatBand, lvSky);
  }
}


void drawSkinPreview(int id, int x, int y) {
  uint8_t old = sv.skin; sv.skin = (uint8_t)id;
  setMarioPalette();
  drawMarioG(tft, x, y, 24, 32, 0, false, 240);
  sv.skin = old;
}

void openShop() {
  int sel = 0;
  nextFrame = millis();
  while (true) {
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(8, 10, 24));
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_YELLOW);
    tft.drawCentreString("SKIN SHOP", 160, 8, 4);
    char cb[24]; snprintf(cb, sizeof(cb), "Coins %d", coins + sv.bank);
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString(cb, 160, 40, 2);
    for (int i = 0; i < NSKIN; i++) {
      int y = 62 + i * 22;
      bool own = sv.owned & (1 << i);
      tft.setTextColor(i == sel ? TFT_YELLOW : TFT_WHITE);
      char row[40];
      if (own) snprintf(row, sizeof(row), "%s%s", SKIN_NAME[i], sv.skin == i ? "  ON" : "  HAVE");
      else snprintf(row, sizeof(row), "%s  %d", SKIN_NAME[i], SKIN_COST[i]);
      tft.drawString(row, 70, y, 2);
    }
    tft.setTextColor(RGB(160, 170, 200));
    tft.drawCentreString("LEFT/RIGHT select   A buy/wear   B back", 160, 214, 1);
    frameWait(40);
    readInput();
    if (E & BTN_LEFT) sel = (sel + NSKIN - 1) % NSKIN;
    if (E & BTN_RIGHT) sel = (sel + 1) % NSKIN;
    if (E & (BTN_B | BTN_START)) return;
    if (E & BTN_A) {
      int purse = coins + sv.bank;
      if (sv.owned & (1 << sel)) { sv.skin = (uint8_t)sel; writeSave(); }
      else if (purse >= SKIN_COST[sel]) {
        int pay = SKIN_COST[sel];
        if (sv.bank >= pay) sv.bank -= pay;
        else { pay -= sv.bank; sv.bank = 0; coins -= pay; if (coins < 0) coins = 0; }
        sv.owned |= (1 << sel); sv.skin = (uint8_t)sel; writeSave();
      }
    }
  }
}

int worldMap() {
  int levels = nLevelsCached;
  if (levels < 1) levels = 1;
  int sel = 0;
  while (sel < levels - 1 && (sv.unlock & (1 << (sel + 1)))) sel++;
  nextFrame = millis();
  while (true) {
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(12, 40, 90));
    tft.fillCircle(260, 36, 16, RGB(255, 230, 90));
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_YELLOW);
    tft.drawCentreString("WORLD MAP", 160, 6, 4);
    char cb[28]; snprintf(cb, sizeof(cb), "Coins %d  Best %d", coins + sv.bank, sv.hi);
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString(cb, 160, 36, 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < levels && i < MAXLV; i++) {
      int col = i % 4, row = i / 4;
      int x = 28 + col * 74, y = 58 + row * 42;
      bool open = sv.unlock & (1 << i);
      uint16_t bg = (i == sel) ? RGB(255, 210, 50) : (open ? RGB(40, 140, 70) : RGB(40, 40, 50));
      tft.fillRoundRect(x, y, 66, 34, 6, bg);
      tft.setTextColor(open ? TFT_WHITE : RGB(90, 90, 90));
      char nb[8]; snprintf(nb, sizeof(nb), "%d", i + 1);
      tft.drawCentreString(nb, x + 33, y + 4, 2);
      if (open && lvTitles[i][0]) {
        char shortn[10]; strncpy(shortn, lvTitles[i], 8); shortn[8] = 0;
        tft.drawCentreString(shortn, x + 33, y + 20, 1);
      }
    }
    tft.setTextColor(RGB(180, 200, 255));
    tft.drawCentreString("LEFT/RIGHT select   A play   B title", 160, 220, 1);
    frameWait(40);
    readInput();
    // Controller has no UP/DOWN: world map is a horizontal list.
    if (E & BTN_LEFT)  sel = (sel + levels - 1) % levels;
    if (E & BTN_RIGHT) sel = (sel + 1) % levels;
    if (sel < 0) sel = 0;
    if (sel >= levels) sel = levels - 1;
    if (E & BTN_SELECT) { openShop(); }
    if (E & BTN_B) return 0;
    if (E & (BTN_A | BTN_START)) {
      if (sv.unlock & (1 << sel)) return sel + 1;
    }
  }
}

void playUfoBossArrival() {
  // The captured UFO lands at the boss arena. Mario climbs out, leaves it parked,
  // then a portal sends him into the ordinary boss world.
  csT = 0; csTheme = TH_SPACE; csPickPalette();
  csUx = 110; csUy = 82; csMx = 110; csBob = 0; csScale = 1.0f;
  strncpy(csTitle, "UFO LANDING", 17); csTitle[17] = 0;
  strncpy(csCaption, "Mario leaves the UFO here...", 27); csCaption[27] = 0;
  fxClear(); tAnim = 0; nextFrame = millis(); readInput();
  while (csT < 150) {
    frameWait(33); readInput();
    if (csT > 24 && (E & (BTN_A | BTN_B | BTN_START | BTN_SELECT))) break;
    tAnim++; csT++;
    if (csT < 55) {
      csUx = 110 + sinf(csT * 0.12f) * 4;
      csUy = 48 + (55 - csT) * 0.55f;
      csMx = csUx;
    } else if (csT < 95) {
      csUx = 110; csUy = 78;
      csMx = clerp(110, 150, cease((csT - 55) / 40.0f));
    } else {
      csUx = 110; csUy = 80;
      csMx = clerp(150, 218, cease((csT - 95) / 55.0f));
    }
    if ((csT % 5) == 0) fxSpark(csUx + random(-18, 19), csUy + 22, RGB(80, 220, 255), 2);
    fxUpdate(); renderBands(drawCutsceneBand, RGB(8, 6, 20));
  }
  tft.fillScreen(TFT_BLACK); delay(80);
}

void delayWithSound(uint32_t ms) {
  uint32_t endAt = millis() + ms;
  while ((int32_t)(millis() - endAt) < 0) {
    soundUpdate();
    delay(4);
  }
}

static void playWorldCard(int world, int stage, const char *subtitle) {
  char line[24];
  snprintf(line, sizeof(line), "%d-%d", world, constrain(stage, 1, 9));
  for (int f = 0; f < 70; f++) {
    frameWait(33); tAnim++; readInput();
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(8, 8, 16));
    tft.fillRect(40, 70, 240, 100, RGB(16, 16, 28));
    tft.drawRect(40, 70, 240, 100, RGB(255, 220, 70));
    tft.drawRect(42, 72, 236, 96, RGB(80, 70, 40));
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
    tft.drawCentreString("WORLD", 160, 84, 2);
    tft.setTextColor(RGB(255, 230, 90));
    tft.drawCentreString(line, 160, 108, 4);
    tft.setTextColor(RGB(200, 210, 230));
    if (subtitle && subtitle[0]) tft.drawCentreString(subtitle, 160, 142, 1);
    mR = RGB(224, 36, 28); mU = RGB(36, 70, 224); mS = RGB(255, 204, 150);
    mB = RGB(110, 58, 20); mY = RGB(255, 220, 40); mK = TFT_BLACK; mW = TFT_WHITE;
    drawMarioG(tft, 148, 178, 24, 32, ((f / 5) & 1) + 1, false, 240);
  }
}

void playLevelTransition(int fromLv, int toLv) {
  (void)fromLv;
  soundStop(); sfxLevelClear();
  playWorldCard(inChapter2 ? 2 : 1, toLv, lvName);
  buzzerOff();
}

template <typename G> void drawIronHulkHero(G &g, int x, int y, int scale, bool flying, int frame) {
  // Original pixel-art finale hero: Hulk-powered Mario in a chunky iron suit.
  int w = 34 * scale, h = 48 * scale;
  uint16_t metal = RGB(150, 165, 180), dark = RGB(45, 55, 70), glow = RGB(90, 240, 255);
  int bob = flying ? (int)(sinf(tAnim * 0.22f) * 3) : 0;
  y += bob;
  // shadow/jet glow
  if (flying) {
    g.fillTriangle(x + w/2, y + h, x + w/2 - 8*scale, y + h + 14*scale, x + w/2 + 8*scale, y + h + 14*scale, RGB(70, 180, 255));
    g.fillTriangle(x + w/2, y + h, x + w/2 - 4*scale, y + h + 9*scale, x + w/2 + 4*scale, y + h + 9*scale, RGB(255, 220, 70));
  }
  // legs
  g.fillRoundRect(x + 5*scale, y + 30*scale, 9*scale, 17*scale, 3*scale, dark);
  g.fillRoundRect(x + 20*scale, y + 30*scale, 9*scale, 17*scale, 3*scale, dark);
  // huge armored torso
  g.fillRoundRect(x + 3*scale, y + 12*scale, 28*scale, 24*scale, 5*scale, metal);
  g.drawRoundRect(x + 3*scale, y + 12*scale, 28*scale, 24*scale, 5*scale, TFT_WHITE);
  // green Hulk core
  g.fillCircle(x + 17*scale, y + 24*scale, 7*scale, RGB(60, 205, 80));
  g.drawCircle(x + 17*scale, y + 24*scale, 8*scale, glow);
  // arms
  int arm = (frame & 1) ? 2 : 0;
  g.fillRoundRect(x - 4*scale, y + (14+arm)*scale, 8*scale, 19*scale, 3*scale, dark);
  g.fillRoundRect(x + 30*scale, y + (10-arm)*scale, 8*scale, 23*scale, 3*scale, dark);
  // helmet + green face
  g.fillRoundRect(x + 7*scale, y, 20*scale, 16*scale, 5*scale, dark);
  g.fillRoundRect(x + 10*scale, y + 5*scale, 14*scale, 8*scale, 2*scale, RGB(70, 210, 90));
  g.fillRect(x + 12*scale, y + 7*scale, 4*scale, 2*scale, RGB(230, 255, 255));
  g.fillRect(x + 19*scale, y + 7*scale, 3*scale, 2*scale, RGB(230, 255, 255));
  // shoulder lights
  g.fillCircle(x + 2*scale, y + 15*scale, 2*scale, glow);
  g.fillCircle(x + 32*scale, y + 15*scale, 2*scale, glow);
}

static void drawFriendBand(int x, int y, bool power) {
  drawFriend(x, y, false, false);
  if (power) spr.drawCircle(x + 8, y + 8, 11, RGB(120, 240, 255));
}

static void drawFriend(int x, int y, bool hurt, bool carrying) {
  setFriendPalette();
  if (hurt) { mR = RGB(24, 120, 40); mU = RGB(12, 80, 30); }
  int frame = carrying ? 3 : (1 + ((tAnim / 6) & 1));
  drawMarioG(spr, x, y, 24, 36, frame, false, 240);
  // tiny pistol for cutscenes
  spr.fillRoundRect(x + 20, y + 18, 8, 3, 1, RGB(50, 52, 62));
  if (carrying) {
    spr.drawLine(x + 4, y + 16, x - 6, y + 8, mS);
    spr.drawLine(x + 20, y + 16, x + 30, y + 8, mS);
  }
}

static void drawPrologueMapFrame(TFT_eSprite &g, int px, bool nearDoor) {
  g.fillSprite(RGB(100,180,245));
  g.fillCircle(275,32,20,RGB(255,235,100));
  for(int i=0;i<5;i++) g.fillCircle(35+i*70,135-(i%2)*10,42,RGB(72,165,78));
  g.fillRect(0,175,320,65,RGB(155,105,55));
  g.fillRect(0,175,320,6,RGB(65,175,62));
  // Mushroom house
  g.fillRect(70,92,116,83,RGB(225,205,150));
  g.fillTriangle(58,94,128,38,198,94,RGB(225,72,65));
  g.fillCircle(92,70,12,TFT_WHITE); g.fillCircle(163,73,14,TFT_WHITE);
  g.fillRect(112,132,30,43,RGB(95,60,35));
  g.fillCircle(136,153,3,RGB(245,205,60));
  g.fillRect(86,112,20,20,RGB(90,185,220));
  g.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); g.drawString("MARIO'S HOUSE",72,24,2);
  if(nearDoor) { g.drawRoundRect(106,126,42,51,5,RGB(255,230,70)); g.drawString("A",122,185,2); }
  g.fillCircle(235,190,18,RGB(110,235,255)); g.drawCircle(235,190,23,RGB(230,255,255));
  g.setTextColor(RGB(30,50,80)); g.drawString("MAP",10,215,2);
  g.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); g.drawString("LEFT/RIGHT move   A enter",82,216,1);
  drawMarioG(g,px,138,24,36,((tAnim/6)&1)+1,false,240);
}

static void drawFriendOnTFT(int x, int y, bool hurt) {
  setFriendPalette();
  if (hurt) { mR = RGB(24, 120, 40); mU = RGB(12, 80, 30); }
  drawMarioG(tft, x, y, 24, 36, 1 + ((tAnim / 6) & 1), false, 240);
  tft.fillRoundRect(x + 20, y + 18, 8, 3, 1, RGB(50, 52, 62));
}

static bool playHousePrologue() {
  float px=36; int phase=0; int floorY=150; bool choice=false; friendCompanion=false;
  nextFrame=millis(); tAnim=0;

  // The house/map used to redraw directly to the TFT every 33 ms, which caused
  // visible tearing/flicker. Render the whole scene into one framebuffer and
  // transfer it in a single SPI operation instead.
  TFT_eSprite houseFrame(&tft);
  houseFrame.setColorDepth(16);
  bool houseBuffered = (houseFrame.createSprite(SCRW, 240) != nullptr);

  if (!houseBuffered) {
    // Extremely low-memory fallback: keep the scene static and avoid rapid clears.
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(100,180,245));
  }

  // Outside map: only LEFT/RIGHT + A are needed.
  while(phase==0) {
    frameWait(33); readInput(); tAnim++;
    if(H&BTN_LEFT) px-=2.2f; if(H&BTN_RIGHT) px+=2.2f; px=constrain(px,18.0f,270.0f);
    bool nearDoor=(px>88 && px<155);
    if((E&BTN_A)&&nearDoor) { phase=1; px=126; }
    if(houseBuffered) {
      drawPrologueMapFrame(houseFrame,(int)px,nearDoor);
      houseFrame.pushSprite(0,0);
    } else {
      // Low-RAM fallback only: direct rendering is less smooth, but safe.
      tft.fillScreen(RGB(100,180,245));
      tft.fillCircle(275,32,20,RGB(255,235,100));
      for(int i=0;i<5;i++) tft.fillCircle(35+i*70,135-(i%2)*10,42,RGB(72,165,78));
      tft.fillRect(0,175,320,65,RGB(155,105,55)); tft.fillRect(0,175,320,6,RGB(65,175,62));
      tft.fillRect(70,92,116,83,RGB(225,205,150));
      tft.fillTriangle(58,94,128,38,198,94,RGB(225,72,65));
      tft.fillCircle(92,70,12,TFT_WHITE); tft.fillCircle(163,73,14,TFT_WHITE);
      tft.fillRect(112,132,30,43,RGB(95,60,35)); tft.fillCircle(136,153,3,RGB(245,205,60));
      tft.fillRect(86,112,20,20,RGB(90,185,220));
      tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawString("MARIO'S HOUSE",72,24,2);
      if(nearDoor) { tft.drawRoundRect(106,126,42,51,5,RGB(255,230,70)); tft.drawString("A",122,185,2); }
      tft.fillCircle(235,190,18,RGB(110,235,255)); tft.drawCircle(235,190,23,RGB(230,255,255));
      tft.setTextColor(RGB(30,50,80)); tft.drawString("MAP",10,215,2);
      tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawString("LEFT/RIGHT move   A enter",82,216,1);
      drawMarioG(tft,(int)px,138,24,36,((tAnim/6)&1)+1,false,240);
    }
  }

  // House interior: LEFT/RIGHT to walk. B is used as the contextual
  // "climb stairs" button because the controller has no UP/DOWN buttons.
  while(phase==1) {
    frameWait(33); readInput(); tAnim++;
    // Redraw the complete interior into the framebuffer, then push it once.
    // This is still flicker-free because the TFT never sees a partially drawn frame.
    if(floorY==150) {
      if(H&BTN_RIGHT) px+=2; if(H&BTN_LEFT) px-=2; px=constrain(px,45.0f,280.0f);
      if((E&BTN_B) && px>120 && px<220) floorY=148;
    } else {
      floorY-=2;
      if(floorY<=76) { phase=2; }
    }

    if(houseBuffered) {
      houseFrame.fillSprite(RGB(235,214,170));
      houseFrame.fillRect(0,188,320,52,RGB(130,88,52));
      houseFrame.fillRect(26,38,268,150,RGB(220,190,135));
      houseFrame.fillRect(38,58,62,40,RGB(105,180,225));
      houseFrame.fillRect(220,54,48,70,RGB(125,78,42));
      houseFrame.fillRect(190,144,72,10,RGB(125,78,42));
      for(int i=0;i<6;i++) houseFrame.fillRect(135+i*9,160-i*15,55-i*9,8,RGB(120,75,40));
      houseFrame.setTextColor(RGB(70,45,25)); houseFrame.drawString("MARIO'S HOUSE",102,18,2);
      if(floorY==150) {
        drawMarioG(houseFrame,(int)px,150,24,36,((tAnim/6)&1)+1,false,240);
        houseFrame.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); houseFrame.drawString("LEFT/RIGHT: walk   B: climb",82,214,1);
      } else {
        drawMarioG(houseFrame,172,floorY,24,36,2,false,240);
        houseFrame.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); houseFrame.drawString("Going upstairs...",112,214,1);
      }
      houseFrame.pushSprite(0,0);
    } else {
      // Safe low-RAM fallback.
      tft.fillScreen(RGB(235,214,170)); tft.fillRect(0,188,320,52,RGB(130,88,52));
      tft.fillRect(26,38,268,150,RGB(220,190,135)); tft.fillRect(38,58,62,40,RGB(105,180,225));
      tft.fillRect(220,54,48,70,RGB(125,78,42)); tft.fillRect(190,144,72,10,RGB(125,78,42));
      for(int i=0;i<6;i++) tft.fillRect(135+i*9,160-i*15,55-i*9,8,RGB(120,75,40));
      tft.setTextColor(RGB(70,45,25)); tft.drawString("MARIO'S HOUSE",102,18,2);
      if(floorY==150) { drawMarioG(tft,(int)px,150,24,36,((tAnim/6)&1)+1,false,240); tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawString("LEFT/RIGHT: walk   B: climb",82,214,1); }
      else { drawMarioG(tft,172,floorY,24,36,2,false,240); tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawString("Going upstairs...",112,214,1); }
    }
  }

  // Friend dialogue is static per line, so there is no need to redraw it 30 times/sec.
  const char* lines[] = {
    "FRIEND: Mario! You're going on a journey?",
    "MARIO: Yes. I have to find Bowser.",
    "FRIEND: Then let me come with you!",
    "MARIO: It could be dangerous...",
    "FRIEND: I won't leave you alone."
  };
  for(int i=0;i<5;i++) {
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(205,178,132)); tft.fillRect(0,188,320,52,RGB(105,70,45));
    tft.fillRect(45,48,230,140,RGB(225,200,155));
    // Dialogue uses the TFT directly, so draw the companion with the TFT surface.
    drawFriendOnTFT(90,125,false);
    drawMarioG(tft,205,125,24,36,2,false,240);
    tft.fillRoundRect(8,10,304,52,7,RGB(18,18,28)); tft.drawRoundRect(8,10,304,52,7,RGB(255,220,90));
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawString(lines[i],16,25,1); tft.drawString("A next",250,46,1);
    while(true) { frameWait(33); readInput(); if(E&BTN_A) break; }
  }
  while(true) {
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(205,178,132));
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_YELLOW); tft.drawCentreString("TAKE YOUR FRIEND?",160,30,2);
    tft.fillRoundRect(30,92,120,55,8,choice?RGB(55,90,55):RGB(235,225,175));
    tft.fillRoundRect(170,92,120,55,8,!choice?RGB(55,90,55):RGB(235,225,175));
    tft.setTextColor(choice?TFT_WHITE:RGB(40,40,40)); tft.drawCentreString("TAKE FRIEND",90,112,2);
    tft.setTextColor(!choice?TFT_WHITE:RGB(40,40,40)); tft.drawCentreString("GO ALONE",230,112,2);
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString("LEFT/RIGHT + A",160,178,1);
    frameWait(33); readInput();
    if(E&BTN_LEFT) choice=false; if(E&BTN_RIGHT) choice=true;
    if(E&BTN_A) { friendCompanion=choice; houseFrame.deleteSprite(); return true; }
  }
}

static void playCompanionBowserEnding() {
  for(int f=0;f<210;f++) {
    frameWait(33); tAnim++;
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(22,30,45));
    tft.fillRect(0,190,320,50,RGB(55,45,35));
    if(f<75) {
      tft.setTextColor(TFT_YELLOW); tft.drawCentreString("BOWSER STRIKE!",160,14,2);
      drawBowserBody(210,115, -1, false); drawFriend(80,142,false,false); drawMarioG(tft,145,145,24,36,2,false,240);
      if((f%6)==0) fxBurst(105,150,RGB(255,90,55),4);
    } else if(f<125) {
      tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString("FRIEND: I'm okay... go!",160,20,2);
      drawFriend(92,150,true,false); drawMarioG(tft,142,128,24,36,2,false,240);
      tft.setTextColor(TFT_YELLOW); tft.drawCentreString("Mario picks up his friend",160,210,1);
    } else {
      tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString("Mario carries his friend to the portal.",160,18,1);
      drawMarioG(tft,120+(f-125)*2,142,28,42,2,false,240);
      drawFriend(118+(f-125)*2,124,true,true);
      tft.drawCircle(250,135,20+(f-125)/5,RGB(100,235,255));
      if(f>175) { tft.setTextColor(TFT_YELLOW); tft.drawCentreString("STORY CONTINUES...",160,82,3); }
    }
  }
  tft.fillScreen(TFT_BLACK); delay(350);
}

static void playBowserHookEscape() {
  for(int f=0;f<190;f++) {
    frameWait(33); tAnim++;
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(8,10,25));
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString("Mario catches Bowser's shell.",160,14,1);
    int bx=185+(int)(sinf(f*0.08f)*25), by=92-(f/8);
    drawBowserBody(bx,by,-1,false);
    drawMarioG(tft,bx-38,by+28,22,34,2,false,240);
    tft.drawLine(bx-16,by+30,bx-4,by+24,RGB(255,230,80));
    if(f>120) { tft.setTextColor(RGB(120,230,255)); tft.drawCentreString("HOLD ON!",160,210,2); }
    if(f>155) tft.drawCircle(bx+10,by+15,28+(f-155),RGB(120,235,255));
  }
  tft.fillScreen(TFT_BLACK); delay(300);
}

void runPlatformerRange(GameInfo &g, int startLevel, int levelCount, bool chapter2) {
  int total = countLevels(g.file);
  if (total < 1) total = 1;
  if (startLevel < 1) startLevel = 1;
  if (startLevel > total) startLevel = total;
  if (startLevel + levelCount - 1 > total) levelCount = total - startLevel + 1;
  if (levelCount < 1) return;

  loadSave(g.file);
  lives = 3; score = 0; coins = 0; pwrResetAll();
  inChapter2 = chapter2;
  chapter2Level = 0;
  companionCombat = chapter2 && friendCompanion;

  int lv = startLevel;
  int played = 0;
  while (true) {
    if (!loadLevel(g.file, lv)) {
      bigMsg("BAD LEVEL", g.file, TFT_RED); delay(1000); lv = startLevel; continue;
    }
    if (chapter2) chapter2Level = (startLevel - 13) + played;
    worldLevelShown = chapter2 ? (1 + played) : lv;
    applyThemeColors();
    if (LR < MAXH) {
      int sh = MAXH - LR;
      memmove(lvl[sh], lvl[0], LR * MAXW);
      memset(lvl[0], ' ', sh * MAXW);
    }
    int r = playLevel();
    if (r == 1) {
      lives--;
      if (lives <= 0) {
        char m[40]; snprintf(m, sizeof(m), "Score %d", score);
        bigMsg("GAME OVER", m, TFT_RED); delay(700);
        lives = 3; score = 0; coins = 0; pwrResetAll(); lv = startLevel; played = 0;
      }
    } else if (r == 2) {
      played++;
      if (played >= levelCount) return;
      playLevelTransition(lv, lv + 1);
      lv++;
    }
  }
}

static void showTalkLine(const char *line, bool showFriend) {
  tft.fillScreen(RGB(205,178,132)); tft.fillRect(0,188,320,52,RGB(105,70,45));
  tft.fillRect(45,48,230,140,RGB(225,200,155));
  if (showFriend) drawFriendOnTFT(90,125,false);
  drawMarioG(tft, showFriend ? 205 : 148, 125, 24, 36, 2, false, 240);
  tft.fillRoundRect(8,10,304,52,7,RGB(18,18,28));
  tft.drawRoundRect(8,10,304,52,7,RGB(255,220,90));
  tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawString(line,16,25,1); tft.drawString("A next",250,46,1);
  while (true) { frameWait(33); readInput(); if (E & BTN_A) break; }
}

static void playChapter2Briefing() {
  if (friendCompanion) {
    const char* lines[] = {
      "FRIEND: This world belongs to Bowser's race.",
      "MARIO: Then every enemy here is one of them.",
      "FRIEND: I am still with you. We finish this together.",
      "MARIO: Stay close. We take the long road."
    };
    for (int i=0;i<4;i++) showTalkLine(lines[i], true);
  } else {
    const char* lines[] = {
      "MARIO: Bowser's homeworld...",
      "MARIO: Everyone here is his race.",
      "MARIO: I came alone. I leave when he falls."
    };
    for (int i=0;i<3;i++) showTalkLine(lines[i], false);
  }
}

void runPlatformer(GameInfo &g) {
  int levels = countLevels(g.file);
  if (levels < 1) levels = 1;
  loadSave(g.file);
  lives = 3; score = 0; coins = 0; pwrResetAll();
  inChapter2 = false;
  if (!introStoryDone) { playHousePrologue(); introStoryDone = true; }
  // friendCompanion is set by the Chapter 1 choice and remains active.
  runPlatformerRange(g, 1, (levels > 13 ? 13 : levels), false);
  // Two Chapter 1 endings: with friend, or alone on Bowser's hook.
  if (friendCompanion) playCompanionBowserEnding();
  else playBowserHookEscape();
}

static void drawChapterMenuBand(int y0, int chapter) {
  (void)chapter;
  const int C1X=16, C2X=164, CY=54, CW=140, CH=142;
  for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
    int gy = y0 + y;
    spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(12 + gy / 30, 28 + gy / 22, 78 + gy / 16));
  }

  static const uint16_t stars[][3] = {
    {12,18,1},{42,36,1},{74,16,2},{108,30,1},{141,14,1},{176,28,1},{211,12,2},
    {246,34,1},{286,18,1},{303,42,2},{25,208,1},{58,194,1},{94,218,1},{126,202,2},
    {196,211,1},{230,194,1},{270,217,1},{298,201,2}
  };
  for (size_t i=0;i<sizeof(stars)/sizeof(stars[0]);++i) {
    int sy=(int)stars[i][1]-y0;
    if (sy>=-3 && sy<BANDH+3) spr.fillCircle(stars[i][0],sy,stars[i][2],RGB(150,180,220));
  }

  int ty=10-y0;
  if (ty>-24 && ty<BANDH) {
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(255,235,105));
    spr.drawCentreString("MARIO ADVENTURE",160,ty,4);
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(170,190,225));
    spr.drawCentreString("SELECT CHAPTER",160,ty+28,1);
  }

  auto card=[&](int x,const char* name,const char* sub,uint16_t accent){
    int y=CY-y0;
    if (y> BANDH || y+CH < 0) return;
    spr.fillRoundRect(x+3,y+4,CW,CH,12,RGB(2,4,12));
    spr.fillRoundRect(x,y,CW,CH,12,RGB(18,25,43));
    spr.drawRoundRect(x,y,CW,CH,12,RGB(55,70,98));
    spr.drawRoundRect(x+3,y+3,CW-6,CH-6,9,RGB(30,42,65));
    spr.setTextColor(accent); spr.drawCentreString(name,x+CW/2,y+10,2);
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(215,225,245)); spr.drawCentreString(sub,x+CW/2,y+33,1);
  };

  card(C1X,"CHAPTER 1","MUSHROOM KINGDOM",RGB(255,95,75));
  card(C2X,"CHAPTER 2","BOWSER'S HOMEWORLD",RGB(110,245,135));

  int my=CY+57-y0;
  if (my>-100 && my<BANDH+10) {
    mR=RGB(224,36,28); mU=RGB(36,70,224); mS=RGB(255,204,150);
    mB=RGB(110,58,20); mY=RGB(255,220,40); mK=TFT_BLACK; mW=TFT_WHITE;
    drawMarioG(spr,C1X+52,my,28,42,0,false,BANDH);
    drawBowserBody(C2X+34,my-2,-1,false);
  }

  int fy=CY+111-y0;
  if (fy>-20 && fy<BANDH+5) {
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(165,180,210));
    spr.drawCentreString("LEFT / RIGHT",160,fy,1);
  }
  int by=216-y0;
  if (by>-20 && by<BANDH+4) {
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(255,235,105));
    spr.drawCentreString("A  START",160,by,2);
  }
}

static void paintChapterSelection(int chapter) {
  // Only thin borders change after the first frame; no full-screen redraw.
  const int C1X=16, C2X=164, CY=54, CW=140, CH=142;
  uint16_t c1=(chapter==0)?RGB(255,220,70):RGB(55,70,98);
  uint16_t c2=(chapter==1)?RGB(110,255,145):RGB(55,70,98);
  tft.startWrite();
  tft.drawRoundRect(C1X,CY,CW,CH,12,c1);
  tft.drawRoundRect(C1X+3,CY+3,CW-6,CH-6,9,(chapter==0)?RGB(255,150,45):RGB(30,42,65));
  tft.drawRoundRect(C2X,CY,CW,CH,12,c2);
  tft.drawRoundRect(C2X+3,CY+3,CW-6,CH-6,9,(chapter==1)?RGB(80,220,120):RGB(30,42,65));
  tft.endWrite();
}

static void drawChapterMenuSelectedBand(int y0) { drawChapterMenuBand(y0, selectedChapter); }

int chapterMenu(GameInfo &g) {
  (void)g;
  selectedChapter = 0;
  titleT = 0;
  readInput();

  // Build the complete menu only once.
  renderBands(drawChapterMenuSelectedBand, 0);
  paintChapterSelection(selectedChapter);

  while (true) {
    buzzerUpdate();
    readInput();
    int old = selectedChapter;
    if (E & BTN_LEFT)  selectedChapter = 0;
    if (E & BTN_RIGHT) selectedChapter = 1;

    if (selectedChapter != old) {
      // Only four small border strokes are sent over SPI.
      paintChapterSelection(old);
      paintChapterSelection(selectedChapter);
      sfxJump();
    }
    if (E & BTN_A) {
      sfxGameStart();
      return selectedChapter;
    }
    delay(2);
  }
}

static void playChapter2HomeReturn(bool companion) {
  for (int f=0; f<180; f++) {
    frameWait(33); tAnim++;
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(24,40,58));
    tft.fillRect(0,180,320,60,RGB(72,48,32));
    tft.fillRoundRect(92,78,136,102,12,RGB(194,120,70));
    tft.fillTriangle(82,80,160,25,238,80,RGB(150,55,45));
    tft.fillRect(145,128,30,52,RGB(90,48,35));
    tft.fillRect(106,106,30,30,RGB(120,205,245)); tft.fillRect(184,106,30,30,RGB(120,205,245));
    if (companion) {
      drawMarioG(tft,118,146,20,30,((f/8)&1),false,240);
      drawFriend(160,146, false,false);
      tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString("HOME",160,12,3);
      if (f>75) tft.drawCentreString("A NEW JOURNEY BEGINS...",160,210,1);
    } else {
      drawMarioG(tft,145,146,24,36,((f/8)&1),false,240);
      tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString("BACK HOME",160,12,3);
      if (f>80) tft.drawCentreString("Mario goes to sleep.",160,210,1);
    }
  }
  tft.fillScreen(TFT_BLACK); delay(300);
}

static void playChapter2PortalReturn() {
  for(int f=0;f<150;f++) {
    frameWait(33); tAnim++;
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(5,9,14));
    for(int i=0;i<28;i++) { int x=(i*47+(int)tAnim*2)%320; int y=(i*31+(int)tAnim*3)%180; tft.fillCircle(x,y,1+(i&1),RGB(70,100,90)); }
    int r=18+(f%24); tft.drawCircle(160,112,r,RGB(80,220,180)); tft.drawCircle(160,112,r+8,RGB(40,120,120));
    drawMarioG(tft,142,145,24,36,((f/6)&1),false,240);
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString("THE PORTAL",160,16,2);
    if(f>90) tft.drawCentreString("HOME",160,205,2);
  }
  playChapter2HomeReturn(false);
}

static void playChapter2FinalBoss() {
  // The 10th chapter-2 level already contains the final boss marker.
  // A short cinematic follows the playable fight.
  for(int f=0;f<120;f++) {
    frameWait(33); tAnim++;
    tft.fillScreen(RGB(10,7,18));
    drawBowserBody(205-(f/20),125+(int)sinf(f*0.1f)*3,-1,(f/12)&1);
    drawMarioG(tft,90+(f/18),148,24,36,(f/5)&1,false,240);
    drawFriend(125+(f/18),150,false,false);
    if((f%10)==0) fxBurst(205,130,RGB(255,80,50),5);
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE); tft.drawCentreString("BOWSER DEFEATED",160,15,3);
  }
  playChapter2HomeReturn(true);
}

void playChapter2(GameInfo &g) {
  int total = countLevels(g.file);
  if (total < 23) { bigMsg("CHAPTER 2 MISSING", "update MARIO.TXT", TFT_RED); delay(1200); return; }
  // Never skip the opening house dialogue, even if Chapter 2 is started first.
  if (!introStoryDone) { playHousePrologue(); introStoryDone = true; }
  playChapter2Briefing();
  inChapter2 = true;
  // Route A (alone): 5 levels, then portal home.
  // Route B (friend): 10 levels, then Bowser finale and home together.
  runPlatformerRange(g, 14, 5, true);
  if (!friendCompanion) { playChapter2PortalReturn(); inChapter2 = false; return; }
  runPlatformerRange(g, 19, 5, true);
  playChapter2FinalBoss();
  inChapter2 = false;
}

// ============================================================
//  TITLE SCREEN (animated, rendered in bands)
// ============================================================

void drawTitleBand(int y0) {
  for (int y = 0; y < BANDH; y += 2) {
    int gy = y0 + y;
    spr.fillRect(0, y, SCRW, 2, RGB(28 + gy / 18, 110 + gy / 14, 228 - gy / 16));
  }
  spr.fillRect(262, LY(28), 22, 22, RGB(255, 236, 110));
  spr.fillRect(266, LY(32), 14, 14, RGB(255, 252, 200));
  for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    int cx = ((i * 97 - (int)(titleT / 2)) % 440 + 440) % 440 - 60;
    int cy = 52 + (i * 29) % 50 + (int)(sinf(titleT * 0.04f + i) * 2.0f);
    drawSmwCloud(cx, LY(cy));
  }
  for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
    int cx = ((i * 90 - (int)(titleT / 4)) % 480 + 480) % 480 - 40;
    spr.fillRect(cx, LY(168), 70, 40, RGB(78, 168, 96));
    spr.fillRect(cx + 16, LY(156), 24, 14, RGB(78, 168, 96));
  }
  spr.fillRect(0, LY(196), SCRW, 44, RGB(176, 108, 48));
  spr.fillRect(0, LY(196), SCRW, 5, RGB(48, 176, 56));
  for (int x = -((int)titleT * 2) % 16; x < SCRW; x += 16) {
    spr.drawFastVLine(x, LY(201), 35, RGB(128, 72, 28));
    spr.fillRect(x + 1, LY(201), 15, 1, RGB(210, 150, 80));
  }
  // ? blocks
  for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    int bx = 40 + i * 120, by = 120;
    spr.fillRect(bx, LY(by), 16, 16, RGB(250, 190, 40));
    spr.drawRect(bx, LY(by), 16, 16, RGB(140, 70, 0));
    spr.fillRect(bx + 5, LY(by + 3), 6, 2, RGB(120, 55, 0)); spr.fillRect(bx + 9, LY(by + 5), 2, 2, RGB(120, 55, 0));
    spr.fillRect(bx + 7, LY(by + 7), 3, 2, RGB(120, 55, 0)); spr.fillRect(bx + 7, LY(by + 11), 2, 2, RGB(120, 55, 0));
  }
  // title
  spr.setTextColor(RGB(20, 10, 40));
  for (int dx = -2; dx <= 2; dx++) for (int dy = -2; dy <= 2; dy++)
    if (dx || dy) spr.drawCentreString("SUPER MARIO", 160 + dx, LY(20 + dy), 4);
  int titleBob = (int)(sinf(titleT * 0.06f) * 2.0f);
  spr.setTextColor(RGB(255, 60, 40));
  spr.drawCentreString("SUPER MARIO", 160, LY(20 + titleBob), 4);
  spr.setTextColor(RGB(255, 235, 90));
  spr.drawCentreString("SUPER MARIO", 160, LY(19 + titleBob), 4);
  spr.setTextColor(RGB(20, 10, 40));
  spr.drawCentreString("16-BIT CLASSIC", 161, LY(56) + 1, 2);
  spr.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
  spr.drawCentreString("16-BIT CLASSIC", 160, LY(56), 2);
  // Mario runs + jumps
  int mx = ((int)titleT * 2) % 380 - 40;
  int ph = titleT % 120;
  int jy = (ph < 44) ? (int)(sinf(ph / 44.0f * 3.1416f) * 44) : 0;
  int fr = jy > 0 ? 3 : 1 + ((titleT / 4) & 1);
  mR = RGB(224, 36, 28); mU = RGB(36, 70, 224); mS = RGB(255, 204, 150);
  mB = RGB(110, 58, 20); mY = RGB(255, 220, 40); mK = RGB(0, 0, 0); mW = RGB(255, 255, 255);
  drawMarioG(spr, mx, LY(200 - 32 - jy), 24, 32, fr, false, BANDH);
  if (((titleT / 12) & 1) == 0) {
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(20, 10, 40));
    spr.drawCentreString("PRESS START", 161, LY(216) + 1, 2);
    spr.setTextColor(RGB(255, 240, 80));
    spr.drawCentreString("PRESS START", 160, LY(216), 2);
  }
}

void titleScreen() {
  titleT = 0;
  sfxTitle();
  readInput();
  while (true) {
    titleT++;
    renderBands(drawTitleBand, 0);
    buzzerUpdate();
    readInput();
    if (E || H) break;
  }
  for (int y = 0; y <= 120; y += 6) {
    tft.fillRect(0, y, 320, 6, TFT_BLACK);
    tft.fillRect(0, 234 - y, 320, 6, TFT_BLACK);
    delay(10);
  }
  tft.fillScreen(TFT_BLACK);
}

void playSplash() {
  // Automatic animated title splash; no button press and no story cutscene.
  titleT = 0;
  nextFrame = millis();
  for (int f = 0; f < 72; f++) {
    frameWait(33);
    titleT++;
    renderBands(drawTitleBand, 0);
    tft.fillRect(0, 216, 320, 24, RGB(50, 110, 235));
    tft.setTextColor(TFT_WHITE);
    tft.drawCentreString("STARTING...", 160, 216, 2);
  }
  tft.fillScreen(TFT_BLACK);
}

void autoStart() {
  GameInfo g;
  memset(&g, 0, sizeof(g));
  if (nGames > 0) {
    g = games[0];
  } else {
    const char *paths[] = { "/MARIO.txt", "/MARIO.TXT", "/mario.txt" };
    bool ok = false;
    for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      if (SD.exists(paths[i])) {
        strncpy(g.file, paths[i], 31);
        strncpy(g.name, "Mario", 23);
        strncpy(g.type, "platformer", 11);
        ok = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (!ok) {
      while (true) {
        bigMsg("NO MARIO.TXT", "put it on the SD card", TFT_RED);
        delay(1200);
      }
    }
  }
  // Classic animated title -> two-chapter menu. LEFT/RIGHT switches chapters.
  titleScreen();
  while (true) {
    int ch = chapterMenu(g);
    if (ch == 0) runPlatformer(g);
    else playChapter2(g);
    // Return to the chapter selector after each chapter sequence.
    // The Chapter 1 friend choice intentionally remains active for Chapter 2.
    introStoryDone = true;
  }
}

void setup() {
  Serial.begin(115200);
  buzzerBegin();
  tft.init();
  tft.setRotation(1);
  spr.setColorDepth(16);
  spr.createSprite(SCRW, BANDH);

  WiFi.mode(WIFI_STA);
  WiFi.disconnect();
  esp_wifi_set_channel(1, WIFI_SECOND_CHAN_NONE);
  if (esp_now_init() != ESP_OK) { bigMsg("ESP-NOW error", "restarting", TFT_RED); delay(1500); ESP.restart(); }
  esp_now_register_recv_cb(onRx);

  sdSPI.begin(SD_SCK, SD_MISO, SD_MOSI, SD_CS);
  while (!SD.begin(SD_CS, sdSPI, 16000000)) {
    bigMsg("NO SD CARD", "FAT32 + MARIO.TXT", TFT_RED);
    delay(1000);
  }
  randomSeed(micros());
  scanGames();
  autoStart();
}

void loop() {}
